Abstract: This paper is devoted to the adaptation of generative large language models for the Tajik language, a low-resource language with Cyrillic script. To overcome the shortage of digital text resources, the author created and publicly released the Tajik Web Corpus, the largest open-access corpus of Tajik, comprising 319,298 documents (~1.11 billion characters). On a subsample of 10,000 documents, 17 configurations were benchmarked, covering autoregressive, encoder-decoder, and encoder-only models with three fine-tuning strategies: full fine-tuning, LoRA, and QLoRA (ranks 8 and 16). Quality was assessed via perplexity and cross-entropy loss; peak GPU memory and training time were also recorded. Best results were achieved by Mistral 7B with QLoRA (r=16): mean perplexity 5.03, standard deviation 0.03. Increasing rank from 8 to 16 gave statistically insignificant improvement while raising memory consumption. For small GPT-2 family models, full fine-tuning yielded lower perplexity (3.48 for GPT-2 Medium) than LoRA (7.60-8.42), but induced catastrophic forgetting. The encoder-only XLM-RoBERTa showed the worst results (perplexity 59.3). The novelty lies in creating the largest verified Tajik corpus and the first systematic analysis of PEFT effectiveness for Tajik text generation. Practical value lies in recommendations for architecture and fine-tuning strategy selection, optimizing computational costs without substantial quality loss.

</details>


### [33] [Natural Language Processing: A Comprehensive Practical Guide from Tokenisation to RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03799)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 一篇介绍系统性NLP实践课程的预印本，涵盖从词法处理到LLM微调的完整管道，包含12个动手实验，强调开源可复现性，并原创性地探索了Tajik和Tatar两种低资源语言的NLP应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合NLP教育中理论与实践的鸿沟，提供一个可复现的、端到端的现代NLP教学框架，涵盖从传统机器学习到最新LLM方法的完整技术栈，同时拓展低资源语言的NLP研究。

Method: 整合了12个动手实践课程，结合理论讲解与详细实现计划，使用统一进化的语料库进行实验，强制要求在公共仓库发布代码、模型和报告，采用开放权重模型而非商业API，特别关注Hugging Face生态系统。

Result: 成功设计了一个包含完整评估指标和透明评分标准的12模块教学体系，原创性地开发了Tajik和Tatar语的低资源语言NLP资源（子词分词器、词嵌入、词汇数据库和音译基准），为数据稀缺环境下的现代NLP适应提供了可参考的方案。

Conclusion: 该预印本通过12个实践课程构建了一个系统化的现代NLP教学框架，强调可复现性和开源模型的使用，特别是在低资源语言（Tajik和Tatar）上的应用创新，为从经典ML到LLM的系统性教学提供了独特的研究型教材范式。

Abstract: This preprint presents a systematic, research-oriented practicum that guides the reader through the entire modern NLP pipeline: from tokenisation and vectorisation to fine-tuning of large language models, retrieval-augmented generation, and reinforcement learning from human feedback. Twelve hands-on sessions combine concise theory with detailed implementation plans, formalised evaluation metrics, and transparent assessment criteria. The work is not a conventional textbook: it is designed as a reproducible research artefact where every session requires publishing code, models, and reports in public repositories. All experiments are conducted on a single evolving corpus, and the work advocates open-weight models over commercial APIs, with special attention to the Hugging Face ecosystem. The material is enriched by original research on low-resource languages, incorporating linguistic resources for Tajik and Tatar (subword tokenisers, embeddings, lexical databases, and transliteration benchmarks), demonstrating how modern NLP can be adapted to data-scarce environments. Designed for senior undergraduates, graduate students, and practising developers seeking to implement, compare, and deploy methods from classical ML to state-of-the-art LLM-based systems.

</details>


### [34] [Reproducing Complex Set-Compositional Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03824)
*Vincent Degenhart,Dewi Timman,Arjen P. de Vries,Faegheh Hasibi,Mohanna Hoveyda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Complex information needs may involve set-compositional queries using conjunction, disjunction, and exclusion, yet it remains unclear whether current retrieval paradigms genuinely satisfy such constraints or exploit `semantic shortcuts'. We conduct a reproducibility study to benchmark major retrieval families and reasoning-targeted methods on QUEST and QUEST+Variants, and introduce LIMIT+, a controlled benchmark where relevance depends on arbitrary attribute predicates and constraint satisfaction, and less on pretrained knowledge. Our findings show that (i) on QUEST, the best neural retrievers achieve an effectiveness that is more than double what can be achieved with BM25 (Recall@100 ${>}$0.41 vs.\ 0.20), but reasoning-targeted methods like ReasonIR and Search-R1 do not outperform general-purpose retrievers uniformly; (ii) on LIMIT+, gains fail to transfer, where the strongest QUEST method collapses from Recall@100${\approx}$0.42 to below 0.02, while classic lexical retrieval gains to ${\sim}$0.96. Lastly, (iii) stratifying by compositional depth reveals a consistent degradation across all methods, where algebraic sparse and lexical methods show more stable performance while dense approaches collapse. We release code and LIMIT+ data generation scripts to support future reproducibility and controlled evaluation.

</details>


### [35] [TRACE: A Metrologically-Grounded Engineering Framework for Trustworthy Agentic AI Systems in Operationally Critical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03838)
*Serhii Zabolotnii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRACE是一个面向操作关键领域的跨域可信赖AI工程框架，通过四层架构、传统ML与LLM验证器的显式分离、状态化编排升级策略、有限人类监督，以及基于国际计量标准（GUM/VIM/ISO 17025）的信任度量体系，实现了LLM使用的有意设计决策而非架构默认，并引入计算精简比率（CPR）作为模型精简的核心设计原则，在临床、工业、司法三个差异化的治理场景中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可信赖AI系统缺乏统一的工程化框架，尤其在操作关键领域（医疗、工业、司法）中缺乏系统性方法。现有系统往往将LLM作为默认架构选择，缺乏对其使用必要性、风险和成本的显式评估机制，导致系统复杂度过高且信任度量缺乏计量学基础。

Method: 提出四层参考架构，核心为L2a（传统ML）与L2b（LLM验证器）的显式分离；L3层实现有状态的编排与升级策略；L4层确保有限的人类监督。构建基于GUM/VIM/ISO 17025的计量级信任度量套件，并引入计算精简比率（CPR）作为模型精简原则的量化指标。三个领域实例（临床决策支持、工业多域操作、司法AI助手）验证框架的跨域迁移能力。

Result: 成功构建了可跨域迁移的可信赖AI工程框架，通过L2a/L2b分离实现了LLM使用的有意设计，通过CPR量化了模型精简原则。三个不同治理环境下的实例验证了框架的通用性和适应性。

Conclusion: TRACE框架成功地将LLM的使用从架构默认转变为可量化的设计决策，通过CPR指标实现模型精简原则。三个跨域实例验证了该框架在不同治理环境下的通用性和适应性，为可信赖AI系统工程提供了可复用的方法论框架。

Abstract: We introduce TRACE, a cross-domain engineering framework for trustworthy agentic AI in operationally critical domains. TRACE combines a four-layer reference architecture with an explicit classical-ML vs. LLM-validator split (L2a/L2b), a stateful orchestration-and-escalation policy (L3), and bounded human supervision (L4); a metrologically grounded trust-metric suite mapped to GUM/VIM/ISO 17025; and a Model-Parsimony principle quantified by the Computational Parsimony Ratio (CPR). Three instantiations--clinical decision support, industrial multi-domain operations, and a judicial AI assistant--transfer the samearchitecture and metrics across principally different governance contexts. The L2a/L2b separation makes the use of large language models a deliberate design decision rather than an architectural default, with parsimony quantified through CPR. TRACE introduces CPR as a first-class design principle in trustworthy-AI engineering.

</details>


### [36] [MCJudgeBench: A Benchmark for Constraint-Level Judge Evaluation in Multi-Constraint Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03858)
*Jaeyun Lee,Junyoung Koh,Zeynel Tok,Hunar Batra,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MCJudgeBench是一个用于评估LLM法官在多约束指令遵循任务中断言级判断能力的基准测试。研究发现当前LLM法官评估存在的关键问题：整体响应层面的表现无法反映约束层面的可靠性，推理增强虽能提升正确率但不能保证稳定性，且正确性与一致性之间不存在正相关关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM法官评估仅关注整体响应判断，忽略了多约束指令遵循场景中每个独立约束的验证需求。LLM法官在约束级别的可靠性评估存在空白，需要新的评估框架来揭示法官可靠性的多维特征。

Method: 构建MCJudgeBench基准数据集，每个样本包含指令、候选响应、显式约束列表、每个约束的金标准标签（yes/partial/no）以及受控的响应扰动。设计了多种评估提示变体以测试法官稳定性，并使用正确性和不一致性双指标评估。

Result: 研究表明：1）强整体性能不保证各标签类别（尤其是稀缺的partial和no案例）检测同样可靠；2）高正确性不等于低不一致性；3）推理增强（CoT）提升正确率但不能统一改善稳定性；4）识别出随机解码导致的内在不一致和扰动导致的程序性不一致两种不同类型。

Conclusion: LLM法官评估应在约束级别进行，以揭示不同维度的失败模式。正确性与一致性是相互独立的评估维度，需分别考察。

Abstract: Multi-constraint instruction following requires verifying whether a response satisfies multiple individual requirements, yet LLM judges are often assessed only through overall-response judgments. We introduce MCJudgeBench, a benchmark for constraint-level judge evaluation in multi-constraint instruction following. Each instance includes an instruction, a candidate response, an explicit constraint list, per-constraint gold labels in {yes, partial, no}, and controlled response-side perturbations. The evaluation protocol further includes evaluation prompt variants to test judge stability. We evaluate proprietary and open-source LLM judges using both correctness and inconsistency metrics, distinguishing intrinsic inconsistency under stochastic decoding from procedural inconsistency under prompt and response perturbations. Our results show that judge reliability has multiple dimensions: strong overall performance does not guarantee equally reliable detection across label categories, especially for rarer partial and no cases. Judges with higher correctness do not always have lower inconsistency. Evaluation with reasoning improves correctness but does not uniformly improve stability. These findings motivate evaluating LLM judges at the constraint level to study these failure modes.

</details>


### [37] [CC-OCR V2: Benchmarking Large Multimodal Models for Literacy in Real-world Document Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03903)
*Zhipeng Xu,Junhao Ji,Zulong Chen,Zhenghao Liu,Qing Liu,Chunyi Peng,Zubao Qin,Ze Xu,Jianqiang Wan,Jun Tang,Zhibo Yang,Shuai Bai,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对现有OCR基准测试与实际应用场景脱节的问题，提出了CC-OCR V2综合基准数据集，涵盖5大OCR核心任务和7,093个高难度样本。实验评估了14个先进LMM，结果显示当前模型性能与实际应用需求之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCR基准测试与实际应用场景不对齐，假设条件过于理想化（同质采集条件），缺乏对实际应用中困难和边缘案例的覆盖。

Method: 提出了CC-OCR V2，一个包含5大核心任务（文本识别、文档解析、文档定位、关键信息提取、文档问答）、7,093个高难度样本的综合OCR基准测试，用于评估LMM在真实应用场景下的表现。

Result: 14个先进LMM在CC-OCR V2上的实验表明，当前模型远未达到实际应用要求，即使是最先进的模型在多样化任务和场景下也表现出显著的性能下降。

Conclusion: 当前LMM在基准测试上的性能与实际应用效果之间存在显著差距，现有模型难以满足实际企业文档处理需求。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently shown strong performance on Optical Character Recognition (OCR) tasks, demonstrating their promising capability in document literacy. However, their effectiveness in real-world applications remains underexplored, as existing benchmarks adopt task scopes misaligned with practical applications and assume homogeneous acquisition conditions. To address this gap, we introduce CC-OCR V2, a comprehensive and challenging OCR benchmark tailored to real-world document processing. CC-OCR V2 focuses on practical enterprise document processing tasks and incorporates hard and corner cases that are critical yet underrepresented in prior benchmarks, covering 5 major OCR-centric tracks: text recognition, document parsing, document grounding, key information extraction, and document question answering, comprising 7,093 high-difficulty samples. Extensive experiments on 14 advanced LMMs reveal that current models fall short of real-world application requirements. Even state-of-the-art LMMs exhibit substantial performance degradation across diverse tasks and scenarios. These findings reveal a significant gap between performance on current benchmarks and effectiveness in real-world applications. We release the full dataset and evaluation toolkit at https://github.com/eioss/CC-OCR-V2.

</details>


### [38] [Steer Like the LLM: Activation Steering that Mimics Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03907)
*Geert Heyman,Frederik Vandeputte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PSR框架，通过将提示导向行为提炼为可解释的激活导向模型，弥补了激活导向与提示导向之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活导向方法性能通常不如基于提示的方法，需要理解这种性能差距的根源。

Method: 提出Prompt Steering Replacement (PSR)模型，从激活中估计令牌级导向系数，并训练模型模仿基于提示的干预行为。

Result: PSR模型在三个导向基准测试中优于现有激活导向方法，特别是在控制高连贯性生成方面。

Conclusion: PSR模型在三个基准测试中优于现有激活导向方法，尤其在控制高连贯性生成方面表现突出，并与提示工程具有竞争力。

Abstract: Large language models can be steered at inference time through prompting or activation interventions, but activation steering methods often underperform compared to prompt-based approaches. We propose a framework that formulates prompt steering as a form of activation steering and investigates whether distilling successful prompt steering behavior into simpler, interpretable models can close this gap. Our analysis reveals that popular activation steering methods are not faithful to the mechanics of prompt steering, which applies strong interventions on some tokens while barely affecting others. Based on these insights, we introduce Prompt Steering Replacement (PSR) models that estimate token-specific steering coefficients from the activations themselves and are trained to imitate prompt-based interventions. Experiments on three steering benchmarks across multiple language models show that PSR models outperform existing activation steering methods, especially when controlling for high-coherence completions, and also compare favorably to prompting on AxBench and persona steering.

</details>


### [39] [Atomic Fact-Checking Increases Clinician Trust in Large Language Model Recommendations for Oncology Decision Support: A Randomized Controlled Trial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03916)
*Lisa C. Adams,Linus Marx,Erik Thiele Orberg,Keno Bressem,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Denise Bernhardt,Markus Graf,Marcus R. Makowski,Stephanie E. Combs,Florian Matthes,Jan C. Peeken*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 原子级事实核查（将AI建议分解为可单独验证的声明并链接到源指南文档）相比传统可解释性方法，能显著提升临床医生对AI的信任度，效果量达到0.94（大效应），信任比例从26.9%跃升至66.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生对AI治疗建议的信任度不足，影响AI在临床决策中的应用。传统可解释性方法（如特征重要性、注意力图等）可能无法有效建立信任，需要探索新的透明度机制。

Method: 随机试验设计，纳入356名临床医生，产生7,476个信任评分。对比原子级事实核查方法与多种传统透明度机制对临床医生信任的影响。

Result: 原子级事实核查产生大效应量（Cohen's d = 0.94），临床医生信任比例从26.9%提升至66.5%。传统透明度机制呈剂量-反应梯度改善，效应量为0.25至0.50。

Conclusion: 在高风险临床决策中，将AI建议分解为可单独验证并链接到源指南的声明，比传统可解释性方法能产生更高的临床医生信任度。

Abstract: Question: Does atomic fact-checking, which decomposes AI treatment recommendations into individually verifiable claims linked to source guideline documents, increase clinician trust compared to traditional explainability approaches?
  Findings: In this randomized trial of 356 clinicians generating 7,476 trust ratings, atomic fact-checking produced a large effect on trust (Cohen's d = 0.94), increasing the proportion of clinicians expressing trust from 26.9% to 66.5%. Traditional transparency mechanisms showed a dose-response gradient of improvement over baseline (d = 0.25 to 0.50).
  Meaning: Decomposing AI recommendations into individually verifiable claims linked to source guidelines produces substantially higher clinician trust than traditional explainability approaches in high-stakes clinical decisions.

</details>


### [40] [The Counterexample Game: Iterated Conceptual Analysis and Repair in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03936)
*Daniel Drucker,Kyle Mahowald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究语言模型是否能通过迭代的"反例-修复"循环进行哲学概念分析。实验涵盖20个概念和数千个循环，发现LM判断接受的反例数量是人类的2倍，但人机判断具有中等一致性；延长迭代仅增加定义长度而不提升准确性；部分概念本身难以获得稳定定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念分析（通过反例精炼定义）是哲学方法论的核心。研究旨在探索语言模型能否执行这一任务，评估其进行高水平迭代哲学推理的能力。

Method: 设计迭代分析-修复链：LM生成器提出定义→另一LM或人类生成反例→修复器改进定义，循环往复。在20个哲学概念上运行数千个反例-修复周期，由专家人类和LM裁判分别评估反例有效性。

Result: LM裁判接受的反例数量约为人类的2倍，但人类间及人机间的逐项有效性判断呈中等一致性；扩展迭代仅使定义冗长化，准确率未获提升；部分概念本质上抗拒稳定定义。

Conclusion: 语言模型可参与哲学推理，但反例-修复循环很快出现收益递减；该范式可作为评估LLM持续高水平迭代哲学推理能力的有效测试用例。

Abstract: Conceptual analysis -- proposing definitions and refining them through counterexamples -- is central to philosophical methodology. We study whether language models can perform this task through iterated analysis and repair chains: one model instance generates counterexamples to a proposed definition, another repairs the definition, and the process repeats. Across 20 concepts and thousands of counterexample-repair cycles, we find that, although many LM-generated counterexamples are judged invalid by both expert humans and an LM judge, the LM judge accepts roughly twice as many as humans do. Nonetheless, per-item validity judgments are moderately consistent across humans and between humans and the LM. We further find that extended iteration produces increasingly verbose definitions without improving accuracy. We also see that some concepts resist stable definitions in general. These findings suggest that while LMs can engage in philosophical reasoning, the counterexample-repair loop hits diminishing returns quickly and could be a fruitful test case for evaluating whether LMs can sustain high-level iterated philosophical reasoning.

</details>

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are prone to factual hallucinations, risking their reliability in real-world applications. Existing hallucination detectors mainly extract micro-level intrinsic patterns for uncertainty quantification or elicit macro-level self-judgments through verbalized prompts. However, these methods address only a single facet of the hallucination, focusing either on implicit neural uncertainty or explicit symbolic reasoning, thereby treating these inherently coupled behaviors in isolation and failing to exploit their interdependence for a holistic view. In this paper, we propose LaaB (Logical Consistency-as-a-Bridge), a framework that bridges neural features and symbolic judgments for hallucination detection. LaaB introduces a "meta-judgment" process to map symbolic labels back into the feature space. By leveraging the inherent logical bridge where response and meta-judgment labels are either the same or opposite based on the self-judgment's semantics, LaaB aligns and integrates dual-view signals via mutual learning and enhances the hallucination detection. Extensive experiments on 4 public datasets, across 4 LLMs, against 8 baselines demonstrate the superiority of LaaB.

</details>


### [42] [Safety and accuracy follow different scaling laws in clinical large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04039)
*Sebastian Wind,Tri-Thien Nguyen,Jeta Sopa,Mahshad Lotfinia,Sebastian Bickelhaup,Michael Uder,Harald Köstler,Gerhard Wellein,Sven Nebelung,Daniel Truhn,Andreas Maier,Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Clinical LLMs are often scaled by increasing model size, context length, retrieval complexity, or inference-time compute, with the implicit expectation that higher accuracy implies safer behavior. This assumption is incomplete in medicine, where a few confident, high-risk, or evidence-contradicting errors can matter more than average benchmark performance. We introduce SaFE-Scale, a framework for measuring how clinical LLM safety changes across model scale, evidence quality, retrieval strategy, context exposure, and inference-time compute. To instantiate this framework, we introduce RadSaFE-200, a Radiology Safety-Focused Evaluation benchmark of 200 multiple-choice questions with clinician-defined clean evidence, conflict evidence, and option-level labels for high-risk error, unsafe answer, and evidence contradiction. We evaluated 34 locally deployed LLMs across six deployment conditions: closed-book prompting (zero-shot), clean evidence, conflict evidence, standard RAG, agentic RAG, and max-context prompting. Clean evidence produced the strongest improvement, increasing mean accuracy from 73.5% to 94.1%, while reducing high-risk error from 12.0% to 2.6%, contradiction from 12.7% to 2.3%, and dangerous overconfidence from 8.0% to 1.6%. Standard RAG and agentic RAG did not reproduce this safety profile: agentic RAG improved accuracy over standard RAG and reduced contradiction, but high-risk error and dangerous overconfidence remained elevated. Max-context prompting increased latency without closing the safety gap, and additional inference-time compute produced only limited gains. Worst-case analysis showed that clinically consequential errors concentrated in a small subset of questions. Clinical LLM safety is therefore not a passive consequence of scaling, but a deployment property shaped by evidence quality, retrieval design, context construction, and collective failure behavior.

</details>

### [43] [From Barrier to Bridge: The Case for AI Data Center/Power Grid Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03090)
*Noman Bashir,Rob Sherwood,Le Xie,Minlan Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AI训练数据中心打破了一个世纪以来电力系统的核心统计假设——负荷多样性。文章认为算力与电力基础设施从隐式共存转向显式协同开发是必要的，并指出了关键研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电力系统依赖负荷多样性假设（数百万小用户的非相关需求产生平滑可预测的聚合负荷），但AI超大规模训练数据中心可产生相当于中型城市的电力需求，由单一高度同步的任务驱动，需求波动可达数百兆瓦/秒。这打破了原有假设，亟需算力与电力行业的协调开发。

Method: 文章引入了数据中心和电力行业各自的设计原则、运营理念和经济激励模式，分析了两个行业在文化和技术上的错位，识别了实现可持续可靠AI供电的关键研究方向。

Result: 明确了两个行业协调困难的根本原因在于文化和技术层面的错位。

Conclusion: 需要从隐式共存转向显式协同开发，涵盖联合容量规划、多时间尺度控制、算力-电力协议栈、市场创新等关键研究方向，以实现AI的可持续可靠供电。

Abstract: For over a century, the electric grid has relied on a single statistical assumption: \emph{load diversity}, the principle that the uncorrelated demands of millions of small consumers produce a smooth, predictable aggregate. AI training data centers break that assumption. A single hyperscale training campus can draw power comparable to a mid-sized city, driven by one tightly synchronized job whose demand swings by hundreds of megawatts in seconds. This paper argues that the resulting entanglement of compute and power infrastructure requires a shift from implicit coexistence to explicit co-development between the historically decoupled data center and electric power industries. We introduce the distinct design principles, operational philosophies, and economic incentives of each sector, and show why their cultural and technical misalignment makes coordination difficult. We identify key research directions, from joint capacity planning, multi-timescale control, a compute--power protocol stack, to market innovation, that must be pursued to power the future of AI sustainably and reliably.

</details>


### [44] [VDCores: Resource Decoupled Programming and Execution for Asynchronous GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03190)
*Zijian He,Adrian Sampson,Yiying Zhang,Zhiyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: VDCores提出一种解耦的GPU编程和执行模型，通过将异步硬件抽象为虚拟核心，将工作负载表示为依赖连接的微操作，从而自动重叠内存和计算，提高异步硬件利用率。该方案在LLM推理任务中实现24%平均吞吐率提升，最高77%，同时减少90%的内核编程工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU依赖专门的异步硬件单元来提升性能，但这些单元利用率不足。原因在于当前GPU软件栈仍围绕 monolithic kernel model 组织编程和执行，无法有效匹配异步硬件特性，导致专用硬件资源浪费。

Method: 1) 提出VDCores模型：将异步硬件执行单元抽象为资源隔离的虚拟核心(virtual cores)；将工作负载表示为依赖连接的微操作(micro-ops)
2) 特点：移除程序员的静态编排负担，实现基于依赖和资源就绪状态的自动内存与计算重叠
3) GPU专用设计：保留灵活性的同时最小化实现开销

Result: 在GH200、H100和RTX 6000 Pro GPU上针对4个LLM推理工作负载测试：
- 解码吞吐率平均提升24%，最高达77%
- 内核编程和专业工作量减少90%
- 已开源：https://github.com/vdcores/vdcores

Conclusion: VDCores通过解耦编程模型有效解决了monolithic kernel与异步硬件不匹配的问题，显著提升GPU资源利用率，同时降低编程复杂度，为LLM推理等异步密集型 workloads 提供高效解决方案。

Abstract: Modern GPUs increasingly rely on specialized and asynchronous hardware units to deliver high performance. Yet these units are often underutilized because today's GPU software stacks still organize programming and execution around a monolithic kernel model that mismatches asynchronous hardware. To address this issue, Virtual Decoupled Engines (VDCores) presents a new decoupled programming and execution model for asynchronous GPUs. VDCores abstracts asynchronous hardware execution units as resource isolated virtual cores and represents workloads as dependency-connected micro-operations (micro-ops). this abstraction removes static orchestration from the programmer, enables automatic overlap of memory and compute based on dependency and resource readiness, and thereby improves utilization of asynchronous hardware resources.
  Realizing such a decoupled abstraction efficiently on today's GPUs is itself challenging, VDCores addresses this through a GPU-specialized programming model and GPU runtime design that preserves the flexibility while minimizing implementation overhead. Across four LLM inference workloads on GH200, H100, and RTX 6000 Pro GPUs, VDCores significantly improves decoding throughput by 24% on average and by up to 77% under dynamic inputs, while reducing kernel programming and specialization effort by 90%. We have open sourced VDCores at https://github.com/vdcores/vdcores.

</details>

  complexity in the model of Massively Parallel Computation, where $N$

  the time complexity of the given problem.

### [46] [Enhancing Performance Insight at Scale: A Heterogeneous Framework for Exascale Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03561)
*Dragana Grbic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: This paper presents an accelerated hpcanalysis framework combining C++ API and GPU parallelism to address performance analysis overhead at exascale, achieving rapid ingestion (9.69s for 100K MPI ranks), significant GPU speedups (314x), topology-aware congestion localization, and a novel tri-dimensional performance model identifying 32.28% speedup potential for GAMESS on Frontier.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional performance analysis tools incur excessive overhead when handling telemetry data at exascale concurrency levels, rendering them impractical for large-scale HPC systems.

Method: The solution combines a high-performance C++ API for efficient data ingestion, GPU-accelerated parallel processing for trace analysis, and a topology-aware workflow that maps logical performance outliers to physical interconnect coordinates (Slingshot on Aurora). Additionally, a novel tri-dimensional performance model re-materializes iterative behavior from execution traces.

Result: The C++ API achieves 9.69-second ingestion time for 100,000 MPI ranks on Aurora; GPU-accelerated processing delivers up to 314x speedup over CPU-based processing when analyzing 100,000 execution traces; topology-aware workflow localizes network congestion across 22 racks on Aurora; tri-dimensional model identifies 32.28% potential speedup for GAMESS workload on Frontier.

Conclusion: The accelerated hpcanalysis framework successfully enables high-throughput performance diagnostics at exascale, with the C++ API achieving rapid ingestion and GPU acceleration providing substantial speedups, while the topology-aware workflow enables physical network congestion localization across distributed systems.

Abstract: As exascale systems reach unprecedented concurrency, traditional performance analysis tools struggle with the overhead of massive-scale telemetry. We present an accelerated infrastructure for the hpcanalysis framework that leverages a high-performance C++ API and GPU parallelism to enable high-throughput diagnostics. Our C++ API achieves a 9.69-second ingestion time for 100,000 MPI ranks on Aurora. Furthermore, our GPU-accelerated layer achieves up to 314x speedup over CPU-based processing when analyzing 100,000 execution traces. Finally, we implement a topology-aware workflow that maps logical performance outliers to physical Slingshot interconnect coordinates, localizing network congestion across 22 distinct racks on Aurora.
  We also demonstrate how the framework's advanced interface seamlessly integrates with external tools to provide sophisticated analytical models. We introduce a novel tri-dimensional performance model that "re-materializes" iterative behavior from execution traces; using this model, we identified a 32.28% potential speedup for a GAMESS workload on Frontier.

</details>


### [47] [Lifting to tensors when compiling scientific computing workloads for AI Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03566)
*Nick Brown,Gabriel Rodriguez-Canal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将科学计算代码中的循环有效编译映射到AMD AI引擎的方法。通过将循环语义提升为张量表示并利用OpenMP注释，仅需极少的代码修改即可实现高效映射。实验表明该方案能效显著优于传统多核CPU方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMD AI引擎已集成到CPU中，具备边缘计算和本地计算资源优化的潜力，但将现有代码移植到AI引擎执行模型需要大量专业知识和时间，这是主要障碍。

Method: 提出一种编译流水线，将通用循环语义提升为张量表示，利用OpenMP注释的循环作为唯一输入，通过张量信息映射到AI引擎执行模型。

Result: 6个AI和科学计算内核基准测试中，NPU在float32下与多核CPU性能持平且能效更优；2个科学计算内核在CPU+NPU协同执行时，相比纯CPU实现可获得最高40%性能提升和15%能耗降低。

Conclusion: 通过将应用语义提升为张量表示，仅需OpenMP装饰的循环即可有效映射到AMD AI引擎，显著降低了针对该架构的代码复杂性。NPU在float32精度下与多核CPU性能相当且能效更优，CPU+NPU协同执行可带来40%性能提升和15%能耗降低。

Abstract: It has been demonstrated that specialised architectures, such as FPGAs and AMD's AI Engines (AIEs), have the potential to deliver energy and performance advantages for scientific computing. Given the integration of AIEs into AMD's CPUs, this is an interesting potential avenue especially when executing on the edge or making better use of local compute constrained resources. However, a major challenge is in enabling existing codes to run on this architecture without extensive modification. Put simply, it requires significant expertise and time to port codes to the AIE's execution model.
  In this paper we explore a compilation pipeline for efficiently mapping loops in general purpose, scientific codes to AIEs. Lifting the semantics of an application into tensors, we demonstrate that this is able to capture the intention of general purpose loops annotated with OpenMP and such high-level tensor information provides a richness that is effective when mapping to the AIEs. Requiring only an OpenMP decorated loop, our approach significantly reduces code complexity when targeting the architecture. For six kernel benchmarks, representing AI and scientific computing, using our approach the NPU performs comparatively to the multicore CPU for float32, in all cases at reduced energy to solution. For two scientific computing kernels running across both the CPU and NPU together delivers up to a 40% improvement in performance and 15% reduction in energy usage compared to the CPU alone.

</details>


### [48] [A Workflow-Oriented Framework for Asynchronous Human-AI Collaboration in Hybrid and Compute-Intensive HPC Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03743)
*Sergio Mendoza,Cedric Bhihe,Natalia Zamora,David Modesto,Jose Martin Bugallo Batalla,Jesus Gomez Canovas,Rafel Palomo Avellaneda,Miguel Perez Espinosa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个异步人机协作工作流框架，用于高风险防御和安全场景下的AI系统训练与部署。该框架可在混合基础设施（HPC集群、本地机器、云平台）上运行，支持在检查点暂停工作流等待人工输入，同时保持底层计算任务运行，避免资源闲置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在国防和安全等高风险场景中，AI系统的训练和部署需要人类参与，但HPC环境的计算密集性和资源约束使得实时人机交互不切实际。现有工作流系统缺乏灵活的人工干预机制，导致要么人工全程监控造成资源浪费，要么完全自动化而缺乏必要的监督和调整能力。

Method: 设计并实现了一个异步人机协作工作流框架，通过在预定义检查点暂停工作流等待人工输入，同时保持底层计算任务继续运行。该框架支持与SLURM调度系统的集成，兼容容器化和原生任务，并针对需要人类判断和适应性的场景进行了定制。

Result: 该框架在MareNostrum 5等系统上进行了模型训练演示，展现了操作型AI工作流在可移植性、效率和监督方面的优势。实现了非阻塞式监督机制，有效避免了资源闲置问题。

Conclusion: 该框架为HPC环境中的实用人机协作提供了可行方案，能够在保持计算效率的同时实现必要的非阻塞式人工监督，适用于国防和安全领域对AI系统进行训练和部署的实际需求。

Abstract: Human involvement is critical in training and deploying AI systems in high-stakes defence and security contexts. However, real-time interaction is impractical in HPC environments due to compute intensity and resource constraints. We present a workflow framework that enables asynchronous human-AI collaboration across hybrid infrastructures, including HPC clusters, local machines, and cloud platforms. Workflows can pause at defined checkpoints for human input without halting underlying compute jobs, preventing idle resources and enabling non-blocking supervision. The framework supports interaction with SLURM-based scheduling, containerized and native tasks, and is customized for scenarios requiring human judgment and adaptability. We demonstrate its application in model training on systems like MareNostrum 5, highlighting benefits in portability, efficiency, and oversight in operational AI workflows.

</details>

Abstract: Sessions is one of the major features introduced in the MPI-4 standard. It offers an alternative to the traditional world communicator model by allowing applications to construct communicators from process sets, thereby eliminating the dependency on MPI_COMM_WORLD. The Sessions model was proposed as a more scalable solution for exascale systems, where MPI_COMM_WORLD was viewed as a potential scalability bottleneck. However, supporting Sessions is a significant challenge for established codebases like MPICH due to the deep integration of the world model in traditional MPI implementations. Although MPICH added support for the MPI-4 standard upon its release, it still internally relied on a global world communicator. This approach enabled applications written using the Sessions model to function, but it did not fulfill the full design intent of Sessions, which meant to decouple MPI from MPI_COMM_WORLD. We describe MPICH effort to support true MPI Sessions, including a major internal refactoring. We describe the architectural changes required to support true Sessions and evaluate the resulting implementation scalability. Our results demonstrate that true Sessions can offer significant scalability benefits by adopting explicit hierarchical designs.

</details>

### [50] [CreativityBench: Evaluating Agent Creative Reasoning via Affordance-Based Tool Repurposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02910)
*Cheng Qian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Jiayu Liu,Bingxiang He,Jeonghwan Kim,Jiateng Liu,Bingxuan Li,Aditi Tiwari,Dwip Dalal,Zhenhailong Wang,Xiusi Chen,Mahdi Namazifar,Yunzhu Li,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreativityBench benchmark reveals that current LLMs struggle with creative tool use despite strong general reasoning capabilities. Models fail at identifying object parts, affordances, and physical mechanisms needed for non-obvious solutions, with scaling and CoT strategies providing minimal gains.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Despite advances in LLMs for reasoning and environment-interaction tasks, their creative problem-solving capability remains underexplored. The researchers aim to study this through creative tool use - repurposing objects by reasoning about affordances and attributes rather than canonical usage.

Method: Introduced CreativityBench benchmark comprising a large-scale affordance knowledge base (4K entities, 150K+ annotations linking objects, parts, attributes, and actionable uses) and 14K grounded tasks requiring non-obvious yet physically plausible solutions under constraints. Evaluated 10 state-of-the-art LLMs.

Result: Models can often select plausible objects but fail to identify correct parts, affordances, and underlying physical mechanisms. Performance degrades significantly. Model scaling gains saturate quickly, strong general reasoning doesn't transfer to creative affordance discovery, and Chain-of-Thought reasoning yields limited improvements.

Conclusion: Creative tool use remains a significant challenge for current LLMs. CreativityBench provides a valuable testbed for studying this under-explored dimension of intelligence, with potential applications for planning and reasoning modules in future agents.

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have led to strong performance on reasoning and environment-interaction tasks, yet their ability for creative problem-solving remains underexplored. We study this capability through the lens of creative tool use, where a model repurposes available objects by reasoning about their affordances and attributes rather than relying on canonical usage. As a first step, we introduce CreativityBench, a benchmark for evaluating affordance-based creativity in LLMs. To this end, we build a large-scale affordance knowledge base (KB) with 4K entities and 150K+ affordance annotations, explicitly linking objects, parts, attributes, and actionable uses. Building on this KB, we generate 14K grounded tasks that require identifying non-obvious yet physically plausible solutions under constraints. Evaluations across 10 state-of-the-art LLMs, including closed and open-source models, show that models can often select a plausible object, but fail to identify the correct parts, their affordances, and the underlying physical mechanism needed to solve the task, leading to a significant drop in performance. Furthermore, improvements from model scaling quickly saturate, strong general reasoning does not reliably translate to creative affordance discovery, and common inference-time strategies such as Chain-of-Thought yield limited gains. These results suggest that creative tool use remains a major challenge for current models, and that CreativityBench provides a useful testbed for studying this missing dimension of intelligence, with potential implications for planning and reasoning modules in future agents.

</details>


### [51] [Stable Agentic Control: Tool-Mediated LLM Architecture for Autonomous Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03034)
*Kerri Prinos,Lilianne Brush,Cameron Denton,Zhanqi Wang,Joshua Knox,Snehal Antani,Anton Foltz,Amy Villaseñor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种工具中介架构，为高风险对抗环境中的LLM智能体提供形式化稳定性保证。通过Lean 4证明的复合Lyapunov函数验证可控性、可观性和输入状态稳定性，并在282个真实企业攻击图和攻防遥测数据上验证。Claude Sonnet 4将攻击者期望收益降低59%，Claude Haiku 4.5虽次优但保持有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心(SOC)必须在对抗性压力下配置端点检测与响应(EDR)策略，现有方法无法提供正式保证。

Method: 工具中介架构：LLM智能体使用确定性工具（Stackelberg最优响应、贝叶斯观测更新、攻击图原语），从有限动作目录中选择行动，在工具输出接口强制执行。通过Lean 4机器检查的复合Lyapunov函数证明可控性、可观性和ISS鲁棒性。

Result: 在282个真实企业攻击图上验证成立；Claude Sonnet 4相比贪心基线将攻击者期望收益降低59%，40次运行零方差；Claude Haiku 4.5收敛至次优值但在40次额外运行中保持目录有界，证明架构稳定性不依赖控制器能力。

Conclusion: 工具中介架构成功将LLM非确定性（促进策略探索）与系统稳定性（由工具接口强制执行）解耦，架构稳定性不依赖于控制器能力水平。

Abstract: Agentic systems involved in high-stake decision-making under adversarial pressure need formal guarantees not offered by existing approaches. Motivated by the operational needs of security operations centers (SOCs) that must configure endpoint detection and response (EDR) policies under adversarial pressure, we present a tool-mediated architecture: LLM agents use deterministic tools (Stackelberg best-response, Bayesian observer updates, attack-graph primitives) and select from finite action catalogs enforced at the tool-output interface. A composite Lyapunov function machine-checked in Lean 4 with zero sorry certifies controllability, observability from asymmetric sensor data, and Input-to-State Stability (ISS) robustness under intelligent adversarial disturbance, with two corollaries extending the certificate to any controller or adversary from the catalogs. On 282 real enterprise attack graphs, the claims hold with margin. On paired offensive/defensive telemetry, a tool-mediated Claude Sonnet 4 controller reduces the attacker's expected payoff (game value) by 59% relative to a deterministic greedy baseline, with zero variance across 40 runs at four temperatures. A Claude Haiku 4.5 controller converges to suboptimal game values but stays catalog-bounded over an additional 40 runs, demonstrating that architectural stability is not dependent on the controller capability. The LLM agent's non-determinism furthers creative exploration of strategies, while the tool-mediated architecture ensures system stability.

</details>

*Dimitris Avramidis,Alexandra Lassota,Ulrike Schmidt-Kraepelin,Adrian Vetta*

Main category: cs.AI

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Abstract: Approval-based committee voting has received significant attention in the social choice community. Among the studied rules, Thiele rules, and especially Proportional Approval Voting (PAV), stand out for desirable properties such as proportional representation, Pareto optimality, and support monotonicity. Their main drawback is that computing a Thiele outcome is NP-hard in general.
  A glimpse of hope comes from the fact that Thiele rules are better behaved under structured preferences. On the candidate interval (CI) domain, they are computable in polynomial time via a linear program (LP) that has a totally unimodular constraint matrix. Surprisingly, this approach fails for the related voter interval (VI) domain, and the complexity of the problem has repeatedly been posed as an open question. Our main result resolves this question: although the relevant matrix is not totally unimodular, the ``standard'' LP still admits at least one optimal integral solution, and we provide a fast algorithm for finding it.
  Our technique naturally extends to the voter-candidate interval (VCI) domain, also known as the 1-dimensional voter-candidate range (1D-VCR) domain, and to the linearly consistent (LC) domain, both of which generalize the candidate and voter interval domains. Although both the VCI and LC domains have been studied in social choice, their relationship was unknown. We show, through connections to graph theory, that LC strictly contains VCI. We also provide an alternative definition of LC that is closer in spirit to VCI and has a natural interpretation in approval elections; this equivalence may be of independent interest. Finally, we study an alternative tree-based generalization of VCI and show that Thiele rules become NP-hard to compute on this domain.

</details>


### [53] [Making the Invisible Visible: Understanding the Mismatch Between Organizational Goals and Worker Experiences in AI Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03078)
*Christine P. Lee,Min Kyung Lee,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 许多组织在引入AI时遭遇失败，尽管AI本应提升创新和效率。根本原因在于：负责与AI协作的一线员工，往往被排斥在AI设计和使用的决策之外。通过对医疗、金融、管理领域的从业人员进行访谈，研究揭示了实施失败的核心障碍，并呼吁将工人视为AI整合的核心参与者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI在组织中的应用旨在推动创新和效率，但大量AI采用项目以失败告终——员工普遍存在抵制情绪且难以将AI系统融入工作流程。深层问题在于：员工虽被期望与AI协作，却往往在AI设计和使用的决策中缺席。这种供需脱节现象尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 采用质性访谈法，调研对象为医疗、金融、管理三个领域中日常与AI系统互动的专业人员。通过半结构化访谈，深入了解员工在实际工作中的AI使用体验、遇到的障碍及其与组织期望之间的差距。

Result: 研究识别出四大核心障碍：1）可用性和互操作性差；2）期望不匹配；3）控制权受限；4）沟通不足。这些挑战揭示了组织AI实施方式与员工实际需求、任务及工作流程之间的显著差距。

Conclusion: 成功的AI采用需要将员工作为核心参与者，而非被动接受者。研究提出三层次适应策略：个人层面、任务层面和组织层面，旨在使AI系统与真实工作实践更好地对齐。

Abstract: While AI is often introduced into organizations to drive innovation and efficiency, many adoption efforts fail as workers resist and struggle to integrate these systems. These failures point to a deeper issue: workers, the very people expected to collaborate with AI, are often invisible in decisions about how AI is designed and used. Drawing on interviews with professionals who interact with AI systems daily in healthcare, finance, and management, we examine the disconnect between organizational expectations and worker experiences. We identify key barriers, including poor usability and interoperability, misaligned expectations, limited control, and insufficient communication. These challenges highlight a gap between how organizations implement AI and the evolving worker needs, tasks, and workflows that it fails to support. We argue that successful adoption requires recognizing workers as central to AI integration and propose adaptation strategies at the individual, task, and organizational levels to better align AI systems with real-world practices.

</details>


### [54] [Programmatic Context Augmentation for LLM-based Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03101)
*Hao Liu,Xiao-Wen Yang,Atharva Sehgal,Yixin Wang,Lan-Zhe Guo,Yu-Feng Li,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的进化搜索框架，通过编程式上下文增强技术改进符号回归。该方法能够主动进行数据分析并提取信息信号，而非仅依赖均方误差等标量评估指标。在LLM-SRBench等基准测试中表现出更高的效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号回归方法主要基于遗传算法等进化方法，存在可扩展性和表达能力的局限性。虽然近期LLM-based进化搜索方法被引入该领域，但现有方法通常仅依赖标量评估指标（如MSE）作为唯一反馈来源，忽略了数据集中嵌入的丰富信息。

Method: 提出一种新颖的基于LLM的进化搜索框架，通过编程式上下文增强（programmatic context augmentation）。该方法支持基于代码的数据集交互，能够主动执行数据分析并提取信息性信号，而非仅依赖聚合评估分数。

Result: 在高级基准测试（如LLM-SRBench）上的评估表明，该框架相比强基线方法展现出更优的效率和准确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的编程式上下文增强框架有效解决了现有LLM-based符号回归方法中信息利用不充分的问题，为科学发现中的符号回归任务提供了一种更高效、更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR), the task of discovering mathematical expressions that best describe a given dataset, remains a fundamental challenge in scientific discovery. Traditional approaches, primarily based on genetic algorithms and related evolutionary methods, have proven useful but suffer from scalability and expressivity limitations. Recently, large language model (LLM)-based evolutionary search methods have been introduced into SR and show promise. However, existing LLM-based approaches typically rely on scalar evaluation metrics, such as mean squared error, as the sole source of feedback during the search process, thereby overlooking the rich information embedded in the dataset. To address this limitation, we propose a novel LLM-based evolutionary search framework that incorporates programmatic context augmentation. By enabling code-based interactions with the dataset, our method can actively perform data analysis and extract informative signals, beyond aggregated evaluation scores. We evaluate our framework on advanced benchmarks, such as LLM-SRBench, and demonstrate superior efficiency and accuracy compared to strong baselines.

</details>


### [55] [Stop Automating Peer Review Without Rigorous Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03202)
*Joachim Baumann,Jiaxin Pei,Sanmi Koyejo,Dirk Hovy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文反对将当前AI系统用于生成论文评审。通过对比人类与AI生成的ICLR 2026评审，发现AI评审者存在过度一致的"蜂群思维"问题，且评审分数可通过简单的论文改写被轻易操纵。研究者认为解决同行评审危机需要系统性的自动化科学研究，而非盲目部署通用大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被视为解决同行评审危机的方案，但现有AI系统的适用性存疑

Method: 实证研究：对比人类与AI生成的ICLR 2026评审；评估自动化论文改写对不同AI评审者的影响

Result: 识别出两个关键问题：1) AI评审者存在"蜂群思维"效应，在论文内部和跨论文间表现出过度一致性，降低了观点多样性；2) AI评审分数可通过"论文洗涤"轻易操纵——通过LLM改写论文即可显著提高AI评审分数

Conclusion: 解决同行评审危机需要同行评审自动化的科学，而非未经严格评估就部署的通用LLM

Abstract: Large language models offer a tempting solution to address the peer review crisis. This position paper argues that today's AI systems should not be used to produce paper reviews. We ground this position in an empirical comparison of human- versus AI-generated ICLR 2026 reviews and an evaluation of the effect of automated paper rewriting on different AI reviewers. We identify two critical issues: 1) AI reviewers exhibit a hivemind effect of excessive agreement within and across papers that reduces perspective diversity. 2) AI review scores are trivially gameable through paper laundering: prompting an LLM to rewrite a paper could significantly increase the scores from AI reviewers, demonstrating that LLM reviewers are easy to game through stylistic changes rather than scientific results. However, non-gameability and review diversity are necessary but not sufficient conditions for automation. We argue that addressing the peer review crisis requires a science of peer review automation -- not general-purpose LLMs deployed without rigorous evaluation.

</details>

Abstract: Modeling latent clinical constructs from unconstrained clinical interactions is a unique challenge in affective computing. We present ADAPTS (Agentic Decomposition for Automated Protocol-agnostic Tracking of Symptoms), a framework for automated rating of depression and anxiety severity using a mixture-of-agents LLM architecture. This approach decomposes long-form clinical interviews into symptom-specific reasoning tasks, producing auditable justifications while preserving temporal and speaker alignment. Generalization was evaluated across two independent datasets ($N=204$) with distinct interview structures. On high-discrepancy interviews, automated ratings approximated expert benchmarks ($\text{absolute error}=22$) more closely than original human ratings ($\text{absolute error}=26$). Implementing an ``extended'' protocol that incorporates qualitative clinical conventions significantly stabilized ratings, with absolute agreement reaching $\text{ICC(2,1)} = 0.877$. These findings suggest that the ADAPTS framework enables promising evaluations of psychiatric severity. While the current implementation is purely text-based, the underlying architecture is readily extensible to multimodal inputs, including acoustic and visual features. By approximating expert-level precision in a protocol-agnostic manner, this framework provides a foundation for objective and scalable psychiatric assessment, especially in resource-limited settings.

</details>


### [57] [Evaluating Prompting and Execution-Based Methods for Deterministic Computation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03227)
*Hongkun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种提示策略在精确计算任务上的表现，发现PoT通过生成可执行代码实现完美准确率，而纯LLM推理存在局限性，建议将LLM与外部工具结合用于确定性任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估LLMs在需要精确、确定性输出的任务上的能力

Method: 系统评估多种提示策略（CoT、Least-to-Most、PoT、SC）；训练CodeT5-small生成可执行程序；引入合成数据集

Result: 标准提示仅达中等准确率；CoT改进有限；Least-to-Most错误累积；PoT通过生成可执行代码达完美准确率；Self-Consistency虽改善鲁棒性但计算开销大；CodeT5-small在合成测试数据上实现完美准确率

Conclusion: LLMs模拟推理模式而非可靠执行确定性符号计算；确定性任务应结合外部工具或使用专用模型

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in natural language understanding and reasoning. However, their ability to perform exact, deterministic computation remains unclear. In this work, we systematically evaluate multiple prompting strategies, including Chain-of-Thought (CoT), Least-to-Most decomposition, Program-of-Thought (PoT), and Self-Consistency (SC), on tasks requiring precise and error-free outputs, including binary counting, longest substring detection, and arithmetic evaluation.
  To support this study, we introduce a synthetic dataset with diverse natural language instructions, enabling controlled evaluation of exact computation across multiple task types.
  Our results show that standard prompting methods achieve only moderate accuracy on sequence-based tasks. CoT provides limited improvement, while Least-to-Most suffers from error accumulation. In contrast, PoT achieves perfect accuracy by generating executable code and delegating computation to an external interpreter. Self-Consistency improves robustness through majority voting, but incurs substantial computational overhead.
  We further train a small domain-specific model (CodeT5-small) to generate executable programs, which achieves perfect accuracy on held-out synthetic test data across all tasks with minimal training cost.
  Overall, our findings suggest that LLMs may simulate reasoning patterns rather than reliably perform exact symbolic computation. For deterministic tasks, combining LLMs with external tools or using specialized models provides a more reliable and efficient solution.

</details>


### [58] [Enhancing Agent Safety Judgment: Controlled Benchmark Rewriting and Analogical Reasoning for Deceptive Out-of-Distribution Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03242)
*Zuoyu Zhang,Yancheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ROME是一个红队协同多智能体演化基准构建流程，通过重写已知不安全轨迹生成更具欺骗性的评估实例；ARISE是一种基于检索的推理时安全增强方法。实验表明这些挑战集显著降低了安全判断性能，即使是前沿模型在隐含风险场景下仍面临困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准仍强调显性风险，可能高估模型判断欺骗性或模糊轨迹的能力，与真实世界场景存在显著差距。

Method: ROME从100条不安全源轨迹生成300个挑战实例，覆盖上下文模糊性、隐含风险和捷径决策；ARISE通过从外部类比库检索ReAct风格的安全轨迹作为结构化推理示例来增强推理。

Result: 这些挑战集显著降低了安全判断性能，隐含风险案例对前沿模型仍具挑战性；ARISE无需重训练即可提升判断质量，但更适合作为任务特定的鲁棒性增强而非独立安全保障。

Conclusion: ROME和ARISE为欺骗性分布偏移下的智能体安全判断压力测试和改进提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Tool-using agent systems powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across web, app, operating-system, and transactional environments. Yet existing safety benchmarks still emphasize explicit risks, potentially overstating a model's ability to judge deceptive or ambiguous trajectories. To address this gap, we introduce ROME (Red-team Orchestrated Multi-agent Evolution), a controlled benchmark-construction pipeline that rewrites known unsafe trajectories into more deceptive evaluation instances while preserving their underlying risk labels. Starting from 100 unsafe source trajectories, ROME produces 300 challenge instances spanning contextual ambiguity, implicit risks, and shortcut decision-making. Experiments show that these challenge sets substantially degrade safety-judgment performance, with hidden-risk cases remaining particularly non-trivial even for recent frontier models. We further study ARISE (Analogical Reasoning for Inference-time Safety Enhancement), a retrieval-guided inference-time enhancement that retrieves ReAct-style analogical safety trajectories from an external analogical base and injects them as structured reasoning exemplars. ARISE improves judgment quality without retraining, but is best viewed as a task-specific robustness enhancement rather than a standalone safety guarantee. Together, ROME and ARISE provide practical tools for stress-testing and improving agent safety judgment under deceptive distribution shifts.

</details>


### [59] [Revisiting the Travel Planning Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03308)
*Bo-Wen Zhang,Jin Ye,Peng-Yu Hua,Jia-Wei Cao,Jie-Jing Shao,Yu-Feng Li,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将旅行规划任务分解为五个原子子能力（约束提取、工具使用、计划生成、错误识别、错误纠正），通过解耦评估协议隔离各组件性能，揭示LLM在隐式约束推理、计划结构偏差和自纠正能力方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM旅行规划评估采用端到端方式，缺乏可解释性，难以定位失败根因；需将复杂任务分解为原子子能力进行细粒度诊断。

Method: 将旅行规划解耦为五项原子子能力：约束提取、工具使用、计划生成、错误识别、错误纠正；利用Oracle中间上下文实现解耦评估，消除级联误差噪声。

Result: LLM擅长提取显式约束，但难以推理隐式开放世界需求；计划生成存在结构偏差；自纠正能力差，表现为过度敏感和错误固执。

Conclusion: 五项原子子能力分解评估可精确定位LLM推理与规划缺陷，为改进LLM长程推理能力提供明确方向。

Abstract: Travel planning serves as a critical task for long-horizon reasoning, exposing significant deficits in LLMs. However, existing benchmarks and evaluations primarily assess final plans in an end-to-end manner, which lacks interpretability and makes it difficult to analyze the root causes of failures. To bridge this gap, we decompose travel planning into five constituent atomic sub-capabilities, including \emph{Constraint Extraction}, \emph{Tool Use}, \emph{Plan Generation}, \emph{Error Identification}, and \emph{Error Correction}. We implement a decoupled evaluation protocol leveraging oracle intermediate contexts to rigorously isolate these components, thereby measuring the atomic performance boundary without the noise of cascading errors. Our results highlight a clear contrast in performance: while LLMs are proficient in extracting explicit constraints, they struggle to infer implicit, open-world requirements. Furthermore, they exhibit structural biases in plan generation and suffer from ineffective self-correction, characterized by excessive sensitivity and erroneous persistence. These findings offer precise directions for improving LLM reasoning and planning abilities.

</details>


### [60] [Automated Large-scale CVRP Solver Design via LLM-assisted Flexible MCTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03339)
*Tong Guo,Caishun Chen,Yew Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LaF-MCTS框架，利用LLM自动化设计大规模CVRP求解器。通过三层决策层次结构、语义剪枝和分支重生长机制，突破传统方法和现有LLM方法的瓶颈，实现超越现有最优求解器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模CVRP（数百至数千节点）求解困难，现有方法需要专家经验且配置耗时；LLM驱动的自动化方法受限于有限上下文窗口难以生成复杂搜索策略。

Method: 提出LLM辅助的灵活蒙特卡洛树搜索(LaF-MCTS)框架，包含三层决策层次结构以实现分解策略和子求解器的增量设计；引入语义剪枝消除冗余代码，以及分支重生长机制保持代码多样性。

Result: LaF-MCTS能够自主构建和优化分解增强型求解器，在CVRPLib上性能超越多种先进CVRP求解器。

Conclusion: LaF-MCTS successfully实现了自动化设计高性能LSCVRP求解器，在CVRPLib基准测试中超越了多种最先进的CVRP求解器，证明了LLM辅助自动化算法设计的可行性。

Abstract: Solving large-scale CVRP (LSCVRP) with hundreds to thousands of nodes remains difficult for even state-of-the-art solvers. Divide-and-conquer can scale by decomposing the instance into size-reduced subproblems, but designing decomposition logic and configuring sub-solvers is highly expertise- and labor-intensive. Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as promising tools for automated algorithm design. However, existing LLM-driven approaches struggle with LSCVRP primarily due to the difficulty in generating sophisticated search strategies within a limited context window. To bridge this gap, we propose the LLM-assisted Flexible Monte Carlo Tree Search (LaF-MCTS), a novel framework that automates the design of high-performance LSCVRP solvers. We develop a three-tier decision hierarchy to enable incremental design of decomposition policies and sub-solvers for LSCVRP. To enable efficient search within the algorithmic hypothesis space, we introduce semantic pruning to eliminate semantically and structurally redundant codes, and branch regrowth to regenerate codes and preserve diversity. Extensive experiments on CVRPLib demonstrate that LaF-MCTS autonomously composes and optimizes decomposition-enhanced solvers that surpasses various state-of-the-art CVRP solvers.

</details>


### [61] [ReasonAudio: A Benchmark for Evaluating Reasoning Beyond Matching in Text-Audio Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03361)
*Honglei Zhang,Yuting Chen,Chenpeng Hu,Siyue Zhang,Yilei Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReasonAudio是首个用于文本-音频检索的推理密集型基准测试，包含1000个查询和10000个复合音频片段，涵盖五个推理任务（否定、顺序、重叠、时长、混合）。对10个SOTA模型的评估表明，所有模型在推理密集型任务上表现不佳，尤其是否定和时长任务，而MLLM嵌入模型未能通过对比微调继承backbone的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频检索基准测试主要关注语义匹配，无法覆盖现实世界查询所需的高级推理能力（如否定理解、时序排序、并发事件识别、时长判别），导致当前模型在推理密集型任务上存在能力缺口。

Method: 构建ReasonAudio基准测试，包含1000个查询和10000个复合音频片段，设计五个基础推理任务：否定(Negation)、顺序(Order)、重叠(Overlap)、时长(Duration)和混合(Mix)。对10个SOTA模型进行系统评估。

Result: 所有模型在推理密集型音频检索任务上均表现困难，尤其在否定和时长任务上表现最差，而在重叠和顺序任务上相对较好。MLLM嵌入模型未能通过对比微调继承backbone的推理能力。

Conclusion: 当前训练范式不足以在检索设置中保持推理能力，需要新的方法来弥合多模态大语言模型推理能力与音频检索任务之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: As multimodal content continues to expand at a rapid pace, audio retrieval has emerged as a key enabling technology for media search, content organization, and intelligent assistants. However, most existing benchmarks concentrate on semantic matching and fail to capture the fact that real-world queries often demand advanced reasoning abilities, including negation understanding, temporal ordering, concurrent event recognition, and duration discrimination. To address this gap, we introduce ReasonAudio, the first reasoning-intensive benchmark for Text-Audio Retrieval, comprising 1,000 queries and 10,000 composite audio clips across five fundamental reasoning tasks: Negation, Order, Overlap, Duration, and Mix. Despite their intuitive nature for humans and straightforward construction, these tasks pose significant challenges to current models. Our evaluation of ten state-of-the-art models reveals the following findings: All models struggle with reasoning-intensive audio retrieval, performing particularly poorly on Negation and Duration while showing relatively better results on Overlap and Order. Moreover, Multimodal Large Language Model-based embedding models fail to inherit the reasoning capabilities of their backbones through contrastive fine-tuning, suggesting that current training paradigms are insufficient to preserve reasoning capacity in retrieval settings

</details>

*Jiahao Wang,Mingyue Cheng,Yitong Zhou,Qingyang Mao,Xiaoyu Tao,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Lithology classification aims to infer subsurface rock types from well-logging signals, supporting downstream applications like reservoir characterization. Despite substantial progress, most existing methods still treat lithology classification as a single-pass classification task. In contrast, practical experts incorporate geological principles, external knowledge, and tool-use capabilities to perform accurate classification. In this work, we propose GeoDecider, a coarse-to-fine agentic workflow that enables accurate and explainable lithology classification through training-free use of large language models (LLMs). GeoDecider reformulates lithology classification as an expert-like structured process and organizes it into a multi-stage workflow involving coarse-to-fine reasoning. Specifically, GeoDecider includes the following stages: (1) base classifier-guided coarse classification, which uses a pre-trained classifier to provide a rough reference for downstream tasks, thus reducing the overall cost of downstream reasoning, (2) tool-augmented reasoning, which utilizes several tools such as contextual analysis and neighbor retrieval to achieve finer and more precise classifications, (3) geological refinement, which post-processes the final results to enforce geological consistency. Experiments on four benchmarks show that GeoDecider outperforms representative baselines. Further analysis demonstrates that the proposed framework produces geologically interpretable predictions while achieving a better trade-off between classification performance and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [63] [Robust Agent Compensation (RAC): Teaching AI Agents to Compensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03409)
*Srinath Perera,Kaviru Hapuarachchi,Frank Leymann,Rania Khalaf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAC是一种基于日志的Agent恢复范式，通过架构扩展为Agent框架提供安全网，支持在不修改现有代码的情况下实现可靠的错误恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-based Agent在执行复杂任务时容易产生非预期的副作用（如API调用、资源消耗、状态变更等），现有错误恢复方法存在资源开销大、延迟高的问题。

Method: 通过架构扩展而非代码修改的方式，在现有Agent框架（如LangGraph）的扩展点中实现日志回滚恢复机制。

Result: 在τ-bench和REALM-Bench基准测试中，RAC在延迟和token消耗方面比现有SOTA方法提升1.5-8倍或以上。

Conclusion: RAC作为可扩展的架构扩展为现有Agent框架提供了经济可靠的错误恢复机制，在复杂问题解决场景中展现出显著的延迟和token消耗优势，是一种通用的Agent安全执行解决方案。

Abstract: We present Robust Agent Compensation (RAC), a log-based recovery paradigm (providing a safety net) implemented through an architectural extension that can be applied to most Agent frameworks to support reliable executions (avoiding unintended side effects). Users can choose to enable RAC without changing their current agent code (e.g., LangGraph agents). The proposed approach can be implemented in most existing agent frameworks via their existing extension points. We present an implementation based on LangChain, demonstrate its viability through the $τ$-bench and REALM-Bench, and show that when solving complex problems, RAC is 1.5-8X or more better in both latency and token economy compared to state-of-the-art LLM-based recovery approaches.

</details>


### [64] [Geometry over Density: Few-Shot Cross-Domain OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03410)
*Shawn Li,You Qin,Jiate Li,Charith Peris,Lisa Bauer,Roger Zimmermann,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UFCOD框架，通过分析扩散轨迹的信息几何特性，实现仅用约100个样本即可完成跨域OOD检测。核心创新在于将扩散噪声预测作为score函数，提取Path Energy和Dynamics Energy两种特征，形成"train-once, deploy-anywhere"的通用范式，在12个跨域基准上达到93.7% AUROC，样本效率提升500倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OOD检测器仅针对单一分布设计，无法处理跨域场景；实际应用中通常缺乏大量标注数据支撑模型训练。需要一种能够在任意新任务上快速部署的通用方法。

Method: UFCOD利用扩散模型噪声预测等于score函数这一特性，从扩散轨迹中提取Path Energy（积分score幅值）和Dynamics Energy（score平滑性），构成离散Sobolev范数。模型在单一数据集（如CelebA）上训练后，可作为通用特征提取器处理任意新任务，无需任何额外训练。

Result: 在12个跨域基准数据集上，使用约100个ID样本/任务，UFCOD达到93.7%平均AUROC，性能与使用50k-163k样本训练的方法相当，样本效率提升约500倍。

Conclusion: UFCOD成功实现了Few-shot跨域OOD检测，证明扩散模型可作为通用异常检测器，为高风险应用的安全部署提供了灵活高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection identifies test samples that fall outside a model's training distribution, a capability critical for safe deployment in high-stakes applications. Standard OOD detectors are trained on a specific in-distribution (ID) dataset and detect deviations from that single domain. In contrast, we study few-shot cross-domain OOD detection: given a \emph{single} pre-trained model, can we perform OOD detection on \emph{arbitrary} new ID-OOD task pairs using only a handful of ID samples at inference time, with no additional training? We propose \textbf{UFCOD}, a unified framework that achieves this goal through information-geometric analysis of diffusion trajectories. Our key insight is that diffusion noise predictions are score functions (gradients of log-density), and we extract two energy features: \emph{Path Energy} (integrated score magnitude) and \emph{Dynamics Energy} (score smoothness), that form a discrete Sobolev norm capturing how samples interact with the learned diffusion process. The central contribution is a \textbf{train-once, deploy-anywhere} paradigm: a diffusion model trained on a single dataset (e.g., CelebA) serves as a universal feature extractor for OOD detection across semantically unrelated domains (e.g., CIFAR-10, SVHN, Textures). At deployment, each new task requires only $\sim$100 unlabeled ID samples for inference: no retraining, no fine-tuning, no task-specific adaptation. Using 100 ID samples per task, UFCOD achieves 93.7\% average AUROC across 12 cross-domain benchmarks, competitive with methods trained on 50k--163k samples, demonstrating $\sim$500$\times$ improvement in sample efficiency. See our code in https://github.com/lili0415/UFCOD.

</details>


### [65] [Replacing Parameters with Preferences: Federated Alignment of Heterogeneous Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03426)
*Shule Lu,Yujing Wang,Hainan Zhang,Xiaoshan Yang,Hongwei Zheng,Yongxin Tong,Changsheng Xu,Zhiming Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MoR，一个面向异构视觉-语言模型(VLM)的联邦对齐框架，通过结合GRPO与奖励混合机制(Mixture-of-Rewards)，实现在不共享参数或数据前提下的隐私保护对齐训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗、金融等隐私敏感领域，视觉-语言模型(VLM)面临数据共享限制，中心化训练不可行；传统联邦学习虽支持去中心化训练，但在极端模型与数据异构性场景下面临挑战。参数聚合方式不再适用，需要一种无需直接交换参数或数据的协作机制。

Method: MoR框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 本地奖励模型：各客户端基于本地偏好标注训练奖励模型，捕获特定评估信号而不暴露原始数据；(2) 奖励混合机制：引入带学习路由的Mixture-of-Rewards，自适应融合客户端奖励模型，根据输入和对齐目标动态加权；服务端使用GRPO结合KL惩罚项优化基础VLM，实现偏好对齐而无需共享模型架构或参数。

Result: 在多个公开视觉-语言基准测试上的实验表明，MoR在泛化能力和跨客户端适应性方面一致优于联邦对齐基线方法。

Conclusion: MoR为联邦环境下异构VLM的隐私保护对齐提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have broad potential in privacy-sensitive domains such as healthcare and finance, yet strict data-sharing constraints render centralized training infeasible. Federated Learning mitigates this issue by enabling decentralized training, but practical deployments face challenges due to client heterogeneity in computational resources, application requirements, and model architectures. Under extreme model and data heterogeneity, replacing parameter aggregation with preference-based collaboration offers a more suitable interface, as it eliminates the need for direct parameter or data exchange. Motivated by this, we propose MoR, a federated alignment framework that combines GRPO with Mixture-of-Rewards for heterogeneous VLMs. In MoR, each client locally trains a reward model from local preference annotations, capturing specific evaluation signals without exposing raw data. To combine these heterogeneous supervision signals, MoR introduces a Mixture-of-Rewards mechanism with learned routing, which adaptively fuses client reward models according to the input and alignment objective. The server then optimizes a base VLM using GRPO with a KL penalty to a reference model, enabling preference alignment without requiring client models to share architectures or parameters. Experiments on diverse public vision-language benchmarks demonstrate that MoR consistently outperforms federated alignment baselines in generalization and cross-client adaptability. Our approach provides a scalable solution for privacy-preserving alignment of heterogeneous VLMs under federated settings.

</details>


### [66] [FinSTaR: Towards Financial Reasoning with Time Series Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03460)
*Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Jaehoon Lee,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinSTaR，一种金融时间序列思维推理方法，通过2x2能力分类体系和针对性设计的链式思维策略（Compute-in-CoT用于确定性评估，Scenario-Aware CoT用于随机性预测），在FinTSR-Bench基准上实现78.9%平均准确率，大幅超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列推理模型(TSRM)在通用领域表现良好，但在金融领域持续失效，因为金融数据具有独特特征（如随机性、噪声多、突发事件影响大），通用模型无法有效捕捉金融市场的复杂性。

Method: 1) 构建2x2能力分类体系：单实体vs多实体分析 × 当前状态评估vs未来行为预测；2) 针对金融领域特性设计两类CoT策略：确定性评估任务使用Compute-in-CoT（程序化链式推理直接从原始价格推导答案）；随机性预测任务使用Scenario-Aware CoT（生成多样场景后做判断，模拟分析师的不确定性推理）；3) 在FinTSR-Bench上进行差异化链式思维训练。

Result: 在基于S&P股票的FinTSR-Bench基准上，FinSTaR达到78.9%的平均准确率，显著优于LLM和TSRM基线；四种能力类别通过联合训练可互补增强；Scenario-Aware CoT始终优于标准CoT。

Conclusion: FinSTaR通过在FinTSR-Bench上的联合训练，实现了四种能力类别的互补增强；Scenario-Aware CoT相比标准CoT能持续提升预测准确率；在S&P股票金融推理任务上，FinSTaR以78.9%的平均准确率大幅超越LLM和TSRM基线模型。

Abstract: Time series (TS) reasoning models (TSRMs) have shown promising capabilities in general domains, yet they consistently fail on financial domain, which exhibit unique characteristics. We propose a general 2x2 capability taxonomy for TSRMs by crossing 1) single-entity vs. multi-entity analysis with 2) assessment of the current state vs. prediction of future behavior. We instantiate this taxonomy in the financial domain -- where the distinction between deterministic assessment and stochastic prediction is particularly critical -- as ten financial reasoning tasks, forming the FinTSR-Bench benchmark based on S&P stocks. To this end, we propose FinSTaR (Financial Time Series Thinking and Reasoning), trained on FinTSR-Bench with distinct chain-of-thought (CoT) strategies tailored to each category. For assessment, which is deterministic (i.e., computable from observable data), we employ Compute-in-CoT, a programmatic CoT that enables models to derive answers directly from raw prices. For prediction, which is inherently stochastic (i.e., subject to unobservable factors), we adopt Scenario-Aware CoT, which generates diverse scenarios before making a judgment, mirroring how financial analysts reason under uncertainty. The proposed method achieves 78.9% average accuracy on FinTSR-Bench, substantially outperforming LLM and TSRM baselines. Furthermore, we show that the four capability categories are complementary and mutually reinforcing through joint training, and that Scenario-Aware CoT consistently improves prediction accuracy over standard CoT. Code is publicly available at: https://github.com/seunghan96/FinSTaR.

</details>


### [67] [Real-Time Evaluation of Autonomous Systems under Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03491)
*Adithya Mohan,Xujun Xie,Venkatesh Thirugnana Sambandham,Torsten Schön*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于真实世界交叉口数据的离线轨迹学习与对抗鲁棒性评估框架，对比MLP-BC、Transformer-BC和GAIL-IRL三种范式。研究发现架构设计对对抗鲁棒性有决定性影响，PGD攻击可造成约8米的位移误差，为自动驾驶对抗鲁棒性研究建立了可扩展基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶策略在对抗条件下的评估主要在仿真环境中进行，但纯虚拟测试无法捕捉真实世界数据中存在的结构不一致性、监督约束和状态表示效应，这些因素从根本上决定了策略的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用真实世界交叉口驾驶数据，在受控数据契约下训练和比较三种轨迹学习范式：基于MLP的行为克隆(BC)、基于Transformer的目标令牌化BC，以及基于生成对抗模仿学习(GAIL)框架的逆强化学习(IRL)。使用平均位移误差(ADE)和最终位移误差(FDE)评估模型性能。通过对训练好的策略施加基于梯度的对抗性扰动（投影梯度下降PGD攻击）来评估推理时的鲁棒性。

Result: 尽管三种范式在标称预测精度上相当（ADE < 0.08），但状态结构设计和架构归纳偏置对对抗稳定性产生关键影响，导致显著的鲁棒性差异。推理时的PGD攻击可诱导高达约8米的最终位移误差。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一个基于真实世界数据的离线轨迹学习与对抗鲁棒性评估框架，建立了可扩展的基准测试方法。研究表明，状态结构设计和架构归纳偏置对对抗稳定性有决定性影响，导致模型在相近的标称性能下表现出显著不同的鲁棒性特征。PGD攻击可诱导高达约8米的最终位移误差，说明现有模型在对抗性扰动下存在严重的安全隐患。

Abstract: Most evaluations of autonomous driving policies under adversarial conditions are conducted in simulation, due to cost efficiency and the absence of physical risk. However, purely virtual testing fails to capture structural inconsistencies, supervision constraints, and state-representation effects that arise in real-world data and fundamentally shape policy robustness. This work presents an offline trajectory-learning and adversarial robustness evaluation framework grounded in real-world intersection driving data. Within a controlled data contract, we train and compare three trajectory-learning paradigms: Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP)-based Behavior Cloning (BC), Transformer-based object-tokenized BC, and inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) formulated within a Generative Adversarial Imitation Learning (GAIL) framework. Models are evaluated using Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE).
  Inference-time robustness is assessed by subjecting trained policies to gradient-based adversarial perturbations across multiple intersection scenarios, yielding a structured robustness evaluation matrix. Results show that state-structure design and architectural inductive biases critically influence adversarial stability, leading to markedly different robustness profiles despite comparable nominal prediction accuracy (ADE < 0.08). Inference-time Projected Gradient Descent (PGD) attacks induce final displacement errors of up to approximately 8 meters. The proposed framework establishes a scalable benchmark for studying offline trajectory learning and adversarial robustness in real-world autonomous driving settings.

</details>


### [68] [Where Paths Split: Localized, Calibrated Control of Moral Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03609)
*Chenchen Yuan,Zheyu Zhang,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CDR方法，通过识别Transformer中伦理框架分支的最小干预点并利用CSP提取框架判别方向，实现细粒度伦理偏好控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在不同场景下表现出异质性的道德偏好，需要在保留通用能力的前提下实现推理时向特定伦理框架的引导。

Method: 提出收敛-发散路由(CDR)方法，定位Transformer内伦理框架相关通路首次收敛后发散的最小分支点，通过门控非目标分支阻止下游传播；将共同空间模式(CSP)适配到残差流，提取区分功利主义与义务论框架的方向向量；引入双逻辑标定(DLC)，在二维子空间中进行最小ℓ2范数闭式更新。

Result: 实验表明CDR能有效实现偏好校准，在真实道德困境测试中显著提升目标伦理框架推理能力，同时较好保留模型通用功能。

Conclusion: CDR通过最小干预点识别与双向框架方向提取的结合，在保持模型通用能力的同时实现精确的伦理偏好校准，性能优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Large language models often display heterogeneous moral preferences across settings. We study inference-time steering toward a desired ethical framework while preserving general competence. We present Convergent-Divergent Routing, which traces and edits minimal branch points inside transformer blocks where ethical-framework-related pathways first converge and then diverge. Gating non-target branches at these loci blocks the downstream propagation while leaving upstream computations intact. We find that this intervention alone increases targeted ethical-framework reasoning. To achieve fine-grained control, we adapt Common Spatial Patterns to the residual stream and extract, for each branch-point layer, a pair of directions that discriminate between utilitarian and deontological frameworks. We then introduce Dual Logit Calibration, a closed-form, minimum-$\ell_2$-norm update that moves the residual within this two-dimensional subspace so the resulting directional projections align with user-specified preference weights. Experiments on real-life moral dilemmas show that our method reliably achieves preference calibration and largely preserves general capabilities, outperforming recent baselines while providing an interpretable mechanism.

</details>


### [69] [Self-Improvement for Fast, High-Quality Plan Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03625)
*Robert Gieselmann,Henrike von Huelsen,Mihai Samson,Marie-Christine Meyer,Dariusz Piotrowski,Oleksandr Radomskyi,Justin Okamoto,Turan Gojayev,Michael Painter,Gavin Brown,Federico Pecora,Jeremy L. Wyatt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生成模型的自改进方法，在子指数时间内为未见问题生成高质量规划解，在Blocksworld、Logistics、Labyrinth和Sokoban四个领域平均将规划长度缩短30%，超过80%的规划达到最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多聚焦于生成任意有效规划，而非高质量（短而优）规划。高质量规划生成本质上是计算困难问题，现有符号规划器在求解质量和效率上面临瓶颈，需要新方法突破。

Method: 1) 证明在最优数据条件下，仅解码器Transformer可生成未见问题的高质量规划；2) 提出自改进框架：多轮迭代中，将模型调用与图搜索结合生成改进规划用于微调模型。实验在Blocksworld、Logistics、Labyrinth、Sokoban四域进行。

Result: 相比源符号规划器，规划长度平均缩短30%；在已知最优解的情况下，超过80%的规划达到最优；推理时搜索进一步提升规划质量；模型延迟随问题规模呈亚指数增长。

Conclusion: 生成模型自改进为高质量规划生成提供了一条可扩展的途径，兼具优于符号规划器的推理效率和解题质量。

Abstract: Generative models trained on synthetic plan data are a promising approach to generalized planning. Recent work has focused on finding any valid plan, rather than a high-quality solution. We address the challenge of producing high-quality plans, a computationally hard problem, in sub-exponential time. First, we demonstrate that, given optimal data, a decoder-only transformer can generate high-quality plans for unseen problem instances. Second, we show how to self-improve an initial model trained on sub-optimal data. Each round of self-improvement combines multiple model calls with graph search to generate improved plans, used for model fine-tuning. An experimental study on four domains: Blocksworld, Logistics, Labyrinth, and Sokoban, shows on average a 30% reduction in plan length over the source symbolic planner, with over 80% of plans being optimal, where the optimum is known. Plan quality is further improved by inference-time search. The model's latency scales sub-exponentially in contrast to the satisficing and optimal symbolic planners to which we compare. Together, these results suggest that self-improvement with generative models offers a scalable approach for high-quality plan generation.

</details>


### [70] [AdapShot: Adaptive Many-Shot In-Context Learning with Semantic-Aware KV Cache Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03644)
*Jie Ou,Jinyu Guo,Shiyao Guo,Yuang Li,Ruiqi Wu,Zhaokun Wang,Wenyi Li,Wenhong Tian*

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Many-Shot In-Context Learning (ICL) has emerged as a promising paradigm, leveraging extensive examples to unlock the reasoning potential of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing methods typically rely on a predetermined, fixed number of shots. This static approach often fails to adapt to the varying difficulty of different queries, leading to either insufficient context or interference from noise. Furthermore, the prohibitive computational and memory costs of long contexts severely limit Many-Shot's feasibility. To address the above limitations, we propose AdapShot, which dynamically optimizes shot counts and leverages KV cache reuse for efficient inference. Specifically, we design a probe-based evaluation mechanism that utilizes output entropy to determine the optimal number of shots. To bypass the redundant prefilling computation during both the probing and inference phases, we incorporate a semantics-aware KV cache reuse strategy. Within this reuse strategy, to address positional encoding incompatibilities, we introduce a decoupling and re-encoding method that enables the flexible reordering of cached key-value pairs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AdapShot achieves an average performance gain of around 10% and a 4.64x speedup compared to state-of-the-art DBSA.

</details>


### [71] [OracleProto: A Reproducible Framework for Benchmarking LLM Native Forecasting via Knowledge Cutoff and Temporal Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03762)
*Yiding Ma,Chengyun Ruan,Kaibo Huang,Zhongliang Yang,Linna Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OracleProto是一个用于评估LLM原生预测能力的可复现框架，通过模型截止日期对齐、时间掩码、泄露检测等五项技术重构已解决事件为时间有界预测样本，解决了预测评估中实时基准不可复用与回溯基准无法区分真实预测与预训练知识的根本矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测能力评估面临困境：实时基准虽能量化真实预测能力但事件解决后失效；回溯基准虽可复现但无法区分真实预测与预训练已学知识。模型被提示"假装不知道"不能替代真实知识边界。

Method: 提出OracleProto框架，采用模型截止日期对齐采样准入、工具级时间掩码、内容级泄露检测、离散答案归一化和分层评分五项技术，重构已解决事件为时间有界预测样本，在FutureX-Past数据集上对6个LLM进行评估。

Result: OracleProto成功区分了6个LLM的预测质量、采样稳定性和成本效率，残泄露降至1%水平，较工具级时间过滤低一个数量级。

Conclusion: OracleProto将LLM预测评估从一次性评估转变为可审计、可复用、可训练的数据集级能力，提供公平跨模型比较的统一接口和下游SFT/RL的受控信号源。实验表明在6个当代LLM上，残泄露降至约1%水平，较纯工具级时间过滤低一个数量级。

Abstract: Large language models are moving from static text generators toward real-world decision-support systems, where forecasting is a composite capability that links information gathering, evidence integration, situational judgment, and action-oriented decision making. This capability is in broad demand across finance, policy, industry, and scientific research, yet its evaluation remains difficult: live benchmarks evaluate forecasts before answers exist, making them the cleanest way to measure forecasting ability, but they expire once events resolve; retrospective benchmarks are reproducible, but they cannot reliably distinguish genuine forecasting from facts a model may have already learned during pretraining. Prompting models to "pretend not to know" cannot replace a genuine knowledge boundary. We propose OracleProto, a reproducible framework for evaluating LLM native forecasting capability.
