OracleProto reconstructs resolved events into time-bounded forecasting samples by combining model-cutoff-aligned sample admission, tool-level temporal masking, content-level leakage detection, discrete answer normalization, and hierarchical scoring. Instantiated on a FutureX-Past-derived dataset with six contemporary LLMs, OracleProto distinguishes forecasting quality, sampling stability, and cost efficiency under controlled information boundaries, while reducing residual leakage to the $1\%$ level, an order of magnitude below tool-only temporal filtering. OracleProto turns LLM forecasting from one-off evaluation into an auditable, reusable, and trainable dataset-level capability, providing a unified interface for fair cross-model comparison and a controlled signal source for downstream SFT and RL. Code and data are available at https://github.com/MaYiding/OracleProto and https://huggingface.co/datasets/MaYiding/OracleProto.

</details>


### [72] [What You Think is What You See: Driving Exploration in VLM Agents via Visual-Linguistic Curiosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03782)
*Haoxi Li,Qinglin Hou,Jianfei Ma,Jinxiang Lai,Tao Han,Sikai Bai,Jingcai Guo,Jie Zhang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GLANCE introduces a curiosity-driven exploration framework for VLM agents, addressing the limitation of passive reasoning in sparse-reward tasks by using the prediction-reality discrepancy as an intrinsic reward signal to actively explore uncertain regions of the environment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current VLM agents rely on passive reasoning over visited states, which is insufficient for sparse-reward tasks as it lacks the epistemic drive to actively uncover "known unknowns" required for robust generalization.

Method: GLANCE bridges reasoning and exploration by grounding the agent's linguistic world model into stable visual representations of an evolving target network, and leverages the discrepancy between linguistic prediction and visual reality as an intrinsic curiosity signal within reinforcement learning to steer active exploration of uncertain areas.

Result: Extensive experiments across a series of agentic tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of GLANCE in solving complex or sparse agentic tasks.

Conclusion: Aligning "what the agent thinks" (linguistic prediction) with "what the agent sees" (visual reality) is key to solving complex or sparse agentic tasks, enabling robust generalization.

Abstract: To navigate partially observable visual environments, recent VLM agents increasingly internalize world modeling capabilities into their policies via explicit CoT reasoning, enabling them to mentally simulate futures before acting. However, relying solely on passive reasoning over visited states is insufficient for sparse-reward tasks, as it lacks the epistemic drive to actively uncover the ``known unknown'' required for robust generalization. We ask: Can VLM agents actively find signals that challenge and refine their internal world model through curiosity-driven exploration? In this work, we propose GLANCE, a unified framework that bridges reasoning and exploration by grounding the agent's linguistic world model into the stable visual representations of an evolving target network. Crucially, GLANCE leverages the discrepancy between linguistic prediction and visual reality as an intrinsic curiosity signal within reinforcement learning, steering the agent to actively explore areas where its internal model is uncertain. Extensive experiments across a series of agentic tasks show the effectiveness of GLANCE, and demonstrate that aligning ``what the agent thinks'' with ``what the agent sees'' is key to solving complex or sparse agentic tasks.

</details>


### [73] [ScrapMem: A Bio-inspired Framework for On-device Personalized Agent Memory via Optical Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03804)
*Jiale Chang,Yuxiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScrapMem是一个面向边缘设备的多模态LLM Agent长期记忆框架，通过"剪贴簿页面"整合多模态数据，采用光学遗忘机制压缩旧记忆分辨率，并构建情景记忆图(EM-Graph)维持语义一致性。在ATM-Bench上实现SOTA，Joint@10达51.0%，存储降低93%，Recall@10达70.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备资源受限，多模态LLM Agent的长期个性化记忆面临存储成本高和多模态复杂度两大挑战。现有方法难以在有限资源下实现高效、准确的记忆管理。

Method: 提出ScrapMem框架，核心包括：(1) 多模态数据整合为"剪贴簿页面"；(2) 光学遗忘(Optical Forgetting)机制，对旧记忆渐进降低分辨率以降低存储成本；(3) 情景记忆图(EM-Graph)，将关键事件组织为因果-时序结构以保持语义一致性。

Result: 在ATM-Bench多模态基准上取得SOTA：Joint@10为51.0%，存储降低达93%，Recall@10为70.3%。

Conclusion: ScrapMem为多模态LLM Agent的设备端长期记忆提供了一种高效、存储友好的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-term personalized memory for LLM agents is challenging on resource-limited edge devices due to high storage costs and multimodal complexity. To address this, we propose ScrapMem, a framework that integrates multimodal data into "Scrapbook Page." ScrapMem introduces Optical Forgetting, an optical compression mechanism that progressively reduces the resolution of older memories, lowering storage cost while suppressing low-value details. To maintain semantic consistency, we construct an Episodic Memory Graph (EM-Graph) that organizes key events into a causal-temporal structure. Extensive experiments on the multimodal ATM-Bench showcase that ScrapMem provides three main benefits: (1) strong performance, achieving a new state-of-the-art with a 51.0% Joint@10 score; (2) high storage efficiency, reducing memory usage by up to 93% via optical forgetting; and (3) improved recall, increasing Recall@10 to 70.3% through structured aggregation. ScrapMem offers an effective and storage-efficient solution for on-device long-term memory in multimodal LLM agents.

</details>


### [74] [SOAR: Real-Time Joint Optimization of Order Allocation and Robot Scheduling in Robotic Mobile Fulfillment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03842)
*Yibang Tang,Yifan Yang,Jingyuan Wang,Junhua Chen,Zhen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SOAR框架，采用深度强化学习统一优化机器人移动履约系统（RMFS）中的订单分配和机器人调度问题，通过事件驱动的马尔可夫决策过程和异构图Transformer实现实时联合决策，有效降低全局完工时间7.5%和平均订单完成时间15.4%，并在真实工业环境（Geekplus）中验证了sim-to-real部署的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RMFS优化面临严格实时性约束和多阶段决策强耦合的挑战。现有方法要么分解问题牺牲全局最优性以保证响应速度，要么采用计算开销大的全局优化模型，无法适应动态工业环境。

Method: 提出SOAR统一DRL框架：1）将订单分配和机器人调度统一建模为事件驱动的马尔可夫决策过程（Event-Driven MDP）；2）采用异构图Transformer编码仓库状态并整合分阶段领域知识；3）引入奖励塑形策略解决长时域任务的稀疏反馈问题。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实工业数据集（与极智嘉合作）上，SOAR将全局完工时间降低7.5%，平均订单完成时间降低15.4%，推理延迟低于100ms。Sim-to-real部署验证了其在生产环境中的实用性和显著性能提升。

Conclusion: SOAR通过统一深度强化学习框架有效解决了RMFS中订单分配与机器人调度的联合优化问题，兼顾实时性和全局最优性，为智能仓储系统提供了一种可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic Mobile Fulfillment Systems (RMFS) rely on mobile robots for automated inventory transportation, coordinating order allocation and robot scheduling to enhance warehousing efficiency. However, optimizing RMFS is challenging due to strict real-time constraints and the strong coupling of multi-phase decisions. Existing methods either decompose the problem into isolated sub-tasks to guarantee responsiveness at the cost of global optimality, or rely on computationally expensive global optimization models that are unsuitable for dynamic industrial environments. To bridge this gap, we propose SOAR, a unified Deep Reinforcement Learning framework for real-time joint optimization. SOAR transforms order allocation and robot scheduling into a unified process by utilizing soft order allocations as observations. We formulate this as an Event-Driven Markov Decision Process, enabling the agent to perform simultaneous scheduling in response to asynchronous system events. Technically, we employ a Heterogeneous Graph Transformer to encode the warehouse state and integrate phased domain knowledge. Additionally, we incorporate a reward shaping strategy to address sparse feedback in long-horizon tasks. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world industrial datasets, in collaboration with Geekplus, demonstrate that SOAR reduces global makespan by 7.5\% and average order completion time by 15.4\% with sub-100ms latency. Furthermore, sim-to-real deployment confirms its practical viability and significant performance gains in production environments. The code is available at https://github.com/200815147/SOAR.

</details>


### [75] [Mechanical Conscience: A Mathematical Framework for Dependability of Machine Intelligenc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03847)
*Munkhdegerekh Batzorig,Purevbaatar Ganbold,Kyungbin Park,Pilkong Jeong,Kangbin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式协同智能(DCI)系统中轨迹级规范调控的空白，提出"机械良心"(MC)概念框架，通过监督过滤器对基线策略进行最小修正，在考虑认知不确定性下减少累积偏离规范可接受区域的程度，并引入良心分数、机械内疚、共振可靠性等可解释的治理信号，建立了该框架的核心理论性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DCI系统（包含边缘计算、联邦学习、迁移学习和群体系统）中，局部正确的个体决策在不确定性下会组合成全局不可接受的轨迹级风险。现有方法（约束优化、安全强化学习、运行时保证）仅评估个体行动层面，无法应对DCI多参与方、不确定性负载的特性，导致轨迹级规范调控存在理论空白。

Method: 提出机械良心(MC)作为监督过滤器，对基线策略动作进行最小修正以减少累积偏离规范可接受区域，同时考虑认知不确定性。引入良心分数(conscience score)、机械内疚(mechanical guilt)、共振可靠性(resonant dependability)等可计算治理信号。

Result: 理论证明：可容性等价性、最优调控的存在性、偏离的单调递减性。实验表明MC调控的智能体维持轨迹级规范可接受性而传统控制器偏离可接受范围，且框架可自然扩展到多智能体DCI场景以抑制交互诱发的emergent risk。

Conclusion: 机械良心框架为单智能体和分布式智能系统提供了轨迹级规范调控的可操作化数学框架，通过最小修正原则和不确定性感知设计，填补了DCI场景下规范治理的理论空白。

Abstract: Distributed collaborative intelligence (DCI), encompassing edge-to-edge architectures, federated learning, transfer learning, and swarm systems, creates environments in which emergent risk is structurally unavoidable: locally correct decisions by individual agents compose into globally unacceptable behavioral trajectories under uncertainty. Existing approaches such as constrained optimization, safe reinforcement learning, and runtime assurance evaluate acceptability at the level of individual actions rather than across behavioral trajectories, and none addresses the multi-participant, uncertainty-laden nature of DCI deployments. This paper introduces mechanical conscience (MC), a novel concept and simplified mathematical framework that operationalizes trajectory-level normative regulation for both single-agent and distributed intelligent systems. Mechanical conscience is defined as a supervisory filter that minimally corrects a baseline policy's actions to reduce cumulative deviation from a normatively admissible region, while accounting for epistemic uncertainty. We introduce associated constructs, conscience score, mechanical guilt, and resonant dependability, that provide an interpretable vocabulary and computable governance signals for this emerging field. Core theoretical properties are established: admissibility equivalence, existence of optimal regulation, and monotonic deviation reduction. Illustrative results demonstrate that MC-regulated agents maintain trajectory-level normative acceptability where conventional controllers drift outside admissible bounds, and that the framework naturally extends to suppress interaction-induced emergent risk in multi-agent DCI settings.

</details>


### [76] [Correct Is Not Enough: Training Reasoning Planners with Executor-Grounded Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03862)
*Tianyang Han,Hengyu Shi,Junjie Hu,Xu Yang,Zhiling Wang,Junhao Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceLift框架，通过引入executor-grounded reward机制，在训练过程中同时评估推理轨迹的质量和实用性，解决传统强化学习方法仅关注最终答案正确性而忽视推理过程可靠性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法仅关注最终答案正确性，无法揭示推理过程是否忠实、可靠或有用。这种仅关注结果的信号可能导致模型学习到"正确但理由错误"的推理，夸大推理收益，并传播有缺陷的中间状态。

Method: 提出TraceLift planner-executor训练框架：1) 规划器(planner)生成带标签的推理过程；2) 冻结的执行器(executor)将推理转化为最终产物供验证器反馈；3) 引入executor-grounded reward，将基于评分标准的推理奖励模型分数乘以在冻结执行器上的提升度量，从而奖励高质量且有用的推理轨迹。此外，构建TRACELIFT-GROUPS数据集，包含数学和代码问题的同题组，每组含高质量参考轨迹和多个有局部扰动的缺陷轨迹。

Result: 在代码和数学基准测试上的广泛实验表明，executor-grounded reasoning reward能够改进两阶段planner-executor系统，优于仅基于执行的训练方法。

Conclusion: 推理监督应不仅评估推理轨迹"看起来如何"，还应评估它对消费它的模型"是否有帮助"。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become a common way to improve explicit reasoning in large language models, but final-answer correctness alone does not reveal whether the reasoning trace is faithful, reliable, or useful to the model that consumes it. This outcome-only signal can reinforce traces that are right for the wrong reasons, overstate reasoning gains by rewarding shortcuts, and propagate flawed intermediate states in multi-step systems. To this end, we propose TraceLift, a planner-executor training framework that treats reasoning as a consumable intermediate artifact. During planner training, the planner emits tagged reasoning. A frozen executor turns this reasoning into the final artifact for verifier feedback, while an executor-grounded reward shapes the intermediate trace. This reward multiplies a rubric-based Reasoning Reward Model (RM) score by measured uplift on the same frozen executor, crediting traces that are both high-quality and useful. To make reasoning quality directly learnable, we introduce TRACELIFT-GROUPS, a rubric-annotated reason-only dataset built from math and code seed problems. Each example is a same-problem group containing a high-quality reference trace and multiple plausible flawed traces with localized perturbations that reduce reasoning quality or solution support while preserving task relevance. Extensive experiments on code and math benchmarks show that this executor-grounded reasoning reward improves the two-stage planner-executor system over execution-only training, suggesting that reasoning supervision should evaluate not only whether a trace looks good, but also whether it helps the model that consumes it.

</details>


### [77] [EvoLM: Self-Evolving Language Models through Co-Evolved Discriminative Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03871)
*Shuyue Stella Li,Rui Xin,Teng Xiao,Yike Wang,Rulin Shao,Zoey Hao,Melanie Sclar,Sewoong Oh,Faeze Brahman,Pang Wei Koh,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Language models encode substantial evaluative knowledge from pretraining, yet current post-training methods rely on external supervision (human annotations, proprietary models, or scalar reward models) to produce reward signals. Each imposes a ceiling. Human judgment cannot supervise capabilities beyond its own, proprietary APIs create dependencies, and verifiable rewards cover only domains with ground-truth answers. Self-improvement from a model's own evaluative capacity is a reward source that scales with the model itself, yet remains largely untapped by current methods. We introduce EVOLM, a post-training method that structures this capacity into explicit discriminative rubrics and uses them as training signal. EVOLM trains two capabilities within a single language model in alternation: (1) a rubric generator producing instance-specific evaluation criteria optimized for discriminative utility, which maximizes a small frozen judge's ability to distinguish preferred from dispreferred responses; and (2) a policy trained using those rubric-conditioned scores as reward. All preference signals are constructed from the policy's own outputs via temporal contrast with earlier checkpoints, requiring no human annotation or external supervision. EVOLM trains a Qwen3-8B model to generate rubrics that outperform GPT-4.1 on RewardBench-2 by 25.7%. The co-trained policy achieves 69.3% average on the OLMo3-Adapt suite, outperforming policies trained with GPT-4.1 prompted rubrics by 3.9% and with the state-of-the-art 8B reward model SkyWork-RM by 16%. Overall, EVOLM demonstrates that structuring a model's evaluative capacity into co-evolving discriminative rubrics enables self-improvement without external supervision.

</details>


### [78] [Contextual Multi-Objective Optimization: Rethinking Objectives in Frontier AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03900)
*Jie Zhou,Qin Chen,Liang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文识别了前沿AI系统在开放性场景中失败的根本原因——"目标选择"失败而非能力不足，并提出"情境化多目标优化"框架。该框架要求系统动态识别和平衡多个上下文相关目标（有用性、真实性、安全性、隐私等），区分软偏好与硬约束，并提供九维实施路径以实现更可靠的AI行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI系统在明确目标场景（如代码生成、数学推理）中表现优异，但在开放性场景（如科学辅助、长周期任务）中可靠性不足。作者认为核心问题不在于规模或能力不足，而在于"目标选择"失败——系统优化了局部可见信号，却忽略了应该主导交互的真正目标。

Method: 将AI行为建模为基于上下文的选择规则，涵盖候选行动、目标估计、活跃约束、利益相关方、不确定性和冲突解决程序等多个维度。方法强调在动态情境中识别和平衡多个目标的重要性，而非依赖固定的目标函数。

Result: 文章建立了"情境化多目标优化"(Contextual Multi-Objective Optimization)框架，将AI行为建模为上下文依赖的选择规则，系统需考虑多个情境相关目标（有用性、真实性、安全性、隐私、校准性、非操纵性、用户偏好、可逆性、利益相关方影响等），并动态判断哪些是活跃目标、软偏好或硬约束。

Conclusion: 文章提出了一个包含分解目标表示、上下文目标路由、分层约束、审议策略推理、受控个性化、工具使用控制、诊断评估、审计和部署后修订等九个维度的实施路径。该框架旨在使AI系统能够在开放性环境中有效处理多目标冲突，而非仅追求单一性能指标。

Abstract: Frontier AI systems perform best in settings with clear, stable, and verifiable objectives, such as code generation, mathematical reasoning, games, and unit-test-driven tasks. They remain less reliable in open-ended settings, including scientific assistance, long-horizon agents, high-stakes advice, personalization, and tool use, where the relevant objective is ambiguous, context-dependent, delayed, or only partially observable. We argue that many such failures are not merely failures of scale or capability, but failures of objective selection: the system optimizes a locally visible signal while missing which objectives should govern the interaction. We formulate this problem as \emph{contextual multi-objective optimization}. In this setting, systems must consider multiple, context-dependent objectives, such as helpfulness, truthfulness, safety, privacy, calibration, non-manipulation, user preference, reversibility, and stakeholder impact, while determining which objectives are active, which are soft preferences, and which must function as hard or quasi-hard constraints. These examples are not intended as an exhaustive taxonomy: different domains and deployment settings may activate different objective dimensions and different conflict-resolution procedures. Our framework models AI behavior as a context-dependent choice rule over candidate actions, objective estimates, active constraints, stakeholders, uncertainty, and conflict-resolution procedures. We outline an implementation pathway based on decomposed objective representations, context-to-objective routing, hierarchical constraints, deliberative policy reasoning, controlled personalization, tool-use control, diagnostic evaluation, auditing, and post-deployment revision.

</details>


### [79] [From Intent to Execution: Composing Agentic Workflows with Agent Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03986)
*Kishan Athrey,Ramin Pishehvar,Brian Riordan,Mahesh Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化创建多智能体系统的框架，通过LLM规划器、两阶段信息检索智能体推荐器和批判智能体等模块，替代了传统的手动组合、选择和创建过程。实验结果表明，该方法在召回率、鲁棒性和可扩展性方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体系统（MAS）的构建需要手动编排计划、手动选择智能体、手动创建执行图，这一过程费时费力且缺乏自动化支持。

Method: 提出了一个自动化框架，包含以下模块：LLM驱动的规划器、自然语言任务描述、动态调用图、智能体任务映射编排器、以及智能体推荐器（采用两阶段信息检索系统，包括快速检索器和基于LLM的重排序器）。此外，还引入了批判智能体对智能体和工具推荐进行整体性重新评估。

Result: 该方法在召回率上优于现有最优方法，且具有更好的鲁棒性和可扩展性。批判智能体的加入进一步提升了召回率得分。

Conclusion: 综合审查和修订基于任务的智能体选择是构建端到端多智能体系统的重要步骤。批判智能体的加入进一步提升了召回率，证明了对智能体和工具推荐进行整体性重新评估的必要性。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built using AI agents fulfill a variety of user intents that may be used to design and build a family of related applications. However, the creation of such MAS currently involves manual composition of the plan, manual selection of appropriate agents, and manual creation of execution graphs. This paper introduces a framework for the automated creation of multi-agent systems which replaces multiple manual steps with an automated framework. The proposed framework consists of software modules and a workflow to orchestrate the requisite task- specific application. The modules include: an LLM-derived planner, a set of tasks described in natural language, a dynamic call graph, an orchestrator for map agents to tasks, and an agent recommender that finds the most suitable agent(s) from local and global agent registries. The agent recommender uses a two-stage information retrieval (IR) system comprising a fast retriever and an LLM-based re-ranker. We implemented a series of experiments exploring the choice of embedders, re- rankers, agent description enrichment, and supervising critique agent. We benchmarked this system end-to-end, evaluating the combination of planning, agent selection, and task completion, with our proposed approach. Our experimental results show that our approach outperforms the state-of-the- art in terms of the recall rate and is more robust and scalable compared to previous approaches. The critique agent holistically reevaluates both agent and tool recommendations against the overall plan. We show that the inclusion of the critique agent further enhances the recall score, proving that the comprehensive review and revision of task-based agent selection is an essential step in building end-to-end multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [80] [An Agent-Oriented Pluggable Experience-RAG Skill for Experience-Driven Retrieval Strategy Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03989)
*Dutao Zhang,Tian Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Experience-RAG Skill，通过将检索策略选择封装为可复用的智能体技能，实现动态适配不同任务的检索需求，在多个基准数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成系统假设单一固定检索流程足以应对所有异构任务，但事实问答、多跳推理、科学验证等不同任务表现出不同的检索偏好需求。

Method: 提出Experience-RAG Skill，一个位于智能体与检索器池之间的面向智能体的可插拔检索编排层。该技能通过分析当前场景、查询经验记忆、选择合适的检索策略，并向智能体返回结构化证据来实现检索编排。

Result: 在固定候选池条件下，于BeIR/nq、BeIR/hotpotqa和BeIR/scifact三个数据集上达到0.8924的整体nDCG@10，优于固定单检索器基线，并与自适应RAG风格路由方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 检索策略选择可以被封装为可复用的智能体技能，而非硬编码到上层工作流中。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation systems often assume that one fixed retrieval pipeline is sufficient across heterogeneous tasks, yet factoid question answering, multi-hop reasoning, and scientific verification exhibit different retrieval preferences. We present Experience-RAG Skill, an agent-oriented pluggable retrieval orchestration layer positioned between the agent and the retriever pool. The proposed skill analyzes the current scene, consults an experience memory, selects an appropriate retrieval strategy, and returns structured evidence to the agent. Under a fixed candidate pool, Experience-RAG Skill achieves an overall nDCG@10 of 0.8924 on BeIR/nq, BeIR/hotpotqa, and BeIR/scifact, outperforming fixed single-retriever baselines and remaining competitive with Adaptive-RAG-style routing. The results suggest that retrieval strategy selection can be productively encapsulated as a reusable agent skill rather than being hard-coded in the upper workflow.

</details>


### [81] [SymptomAI: Towards a Conversational AI Agent for Everyday Symptom Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04012)
*Joseph Breda,Fadi Yousif,Beszel Hawkins,Marinela Cotoi,Miao Liu,Ray Luo,Po-Hsuan Cameron Chen,Mike Schaekermann,Samuel Schmidgall,Xin Liu,Girish Narayanswamy,Samuel Solomon,Maxwell A. Xu,Xiaoran Fan,Longfei Shangguan,Anran Wang,Bhavna Daryani,Buddy Herkenham,Cara Tan,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,John B. Hernandez,Quang Duong,Yun Liu,Zach Wasson,Dimitrios Antos,Bob Lou,Matthew Thompson,Jonathan Richina,Anupam Pathak,Nichole Young-Lin,Jake Sunshine,Daniel McDuff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SymptomAI是对话式AI患者访谈系统，在约1.4万人的Fitbit用户中验证，其诊断准确率显著超过临床医生，并证明系统化的症状采集优于用户自主描述。该研究还揭示了可穿戴设备生理数据与疾病状态的强关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦于精心策划的医学案例研究，但这些复杂场景难以反映患者在日常生活中报告症状的真实情况。需要评估语言模型在真实世界人群中的表现。

Method: 通过Fitbit应用部署SymptomAI对话式AI智能体，采用随机对照设计（N=13,917）。子集中1,228名参与者提供临床诊断标签，517名参与者的对话由临床医生小组进行注释（共250多小时）。在盲法随机比较中评估AI的DDx准确性，并与给定相同对话的独立临床医生进行对比。同时分析了超过500,000天的可穿戴设备数据与约400种疾病的相关性。

Result: SymptomAI的DDx准确性显著优于独立临床医生（OR=2.47, p<0.001）；采用专门症状访谈的智能体策略表现显著优于基线用户引导对话（p<0.001）；急性感染与生理指标变化存在强相关性（如流感OR>7）。

Conclusion: 尽管受限于自我报告的真实标签，但研究结果表明，专门且完整的症状访谈优于用户引导的症状讨论（这是大多数消费者LLM的默认模式）。

Abstract: Language models excel at diagnostic assessments on currated medical case-studies and vignettes, performing on par with, or better than, clinical professionals. However, existing studies focus on complex scenarios with rich context making it difficult to draw conclusions about how these systems perform for patients reporting symptoms in everyday life. We deployed SymptomAI, a set of conversational AI agents for end-to-end patient interviewing and differential diagnosis (DDx), via the Fitbit app in a study that randomized participants (N=13,917) to interact with five AI agents. This corpus captures diverse communication and a realistic distribution of illnesses from a real world population. A subset of 1,228 participants reported a clinician-provided diagnosis, and 517 of these were further evaluated by a panel of clinicians during over 250 hours of annotation. SymptomAI DDx were significantly more accurate (OR = 2.47, p < 0.001) than those from independent clinicians given the same dialogue in a blinded randomized comparison. Moreover, agentic strategies which conduct a dedicated symptom interview that elicit additional symptom information before providing a diagnosis, perform substantially better than baseline, user-guided conversations (p < 0.001). An auxiliary analysis on 1,509 conversations from a general US population panel validated that these results generalize beyond wearable device users. We used SymptomAI diagnoses as labels for all 13,917 participants to analyze over 500,000 days of wearable metrics across nearly 400 unique conditions. We identified strong associations between acute infections and physiological shifts (e.g., OR > 7 for influenza). While limited by self-reported ground truth, these results demonstrate the benefits of a dedicated and complete symptom interview compared to a user-guided symptom discussion, which is the default of most consumer LLMs.

</details>


### [82] [Redefining AI Red Teaming in the Agentic Era: From Weeks to Hours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04019)
*Raja Sekhar Rao Dheekonda,Will Pearce,Nick Landers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍基于Dreadnode SDK的AI红队智能体系统，通过自然语言接口将红队工作从周级压缩到小时级。该系统统一了传统ML模型对抗样本测试和生成式AI越狱测试的框架，涵盖45+攻击方法、450+转换操作和130+评分器。在Meta Llama Scout上验证实现85%攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI红队方法需要操作者手动编写特定库的代码，工作流程繁琐（需数周时间构建），且结果不满意时需重建流程，导致实际探测目标的时间远少于构建工作流的时间。

Method: 构建基于45+攻击方法、450+转换操作和130+评分器的统一框架；通过自然语言接口让操作者描述目标，系统自动完成攻击选择、转换组合、执行和报告生成。

Result: 在Meta Llama Scout上实现85%攻击成功率，严重度达1.0，且无需人工开发代码。

Conclusion: 通过Dreadnode SDK和AI智能体架构，将AI红队攻击从周级工作压缩到小时级；在Llama Scout上实现85%攻击成功率，证明框架有效性。

Abstract: AI systems are entering critical domains like healthcare, finance, and defense, yet remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. While AI red teaming is a primary defense, current approaches force operators into manual, library-specific workflows. Operators spend weeks hand-crafting workflows - assembling attacks, transforms, and scorers. When results fall short, workflows must be rebuilt. As a result, operators spend more time constructing workflows than probing targets for security and safety vulnerabilities.
  We introduce an AI red teaming agent built on the open-source Dreadnode SDK. The agent creates workflows grounded in 45+ adversarial attacks, 450+ transforms, and 130+ scorers. Operators can probe multi-agent systems, multilingual, and multimodal targets, focusing on what to probe rather than how to implement it.
  We make three contributions: 1. Agentic interface. Operators describe goals in natural language via the Dreadnode TUI (Terminal User Interface). The agent handles attack selection, transform composition, execution, and reporting, letting operators focus on red teaming. Weeks compress to hours. 2. Unified framework. A single framework for probing traditional ML models (adversarial examples) and generative AI systems (jailbreaks), removing the need for separate libraries. 3. Llama Scout case study. We red team Meta Llama Scout and achieve an 85% attack success rate with severity up to 1.0, using zero human-developed code

</details>


### [83] [OpenSeeker-v2: Pushing the Limits of Search Agents with Informative and High-Difficulty Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04036)
*Yuwen Du,Rui Ye,Shuo Tang,Keduan Huang,Xinyu Zhu,Yuzhu Cai,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文表明，仅通过监督微调（SFT）配合高质量训练数据，学术团队即可训练出超越工业级大厂的搜索智能体。通过三项数据合成改进（扩大知识图谱、扩展工具集、严格低步数过滤），使用仅10.6k数据点训练的OpenSeeker-v2在4个基准上超越Tongyi DeepResearch。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业界训练前沿搜索智能体需要大量资源（预训练+持续预训练+SFT+强化学习），门槛过高。学术团队缺乏资源跟进。本文旨在探索是否可以通过简化的SFT方法+高质量轨迹数据达到同等甚至更好的效果。

Method: 三项简单数据合成改进：(1) 扩大知识图谱规模以支持更丰富的探索；(2) 扩展工具集规模以提供更广泛的功能；(3) 严格低步数过滤以确保数据质量。仅使用10.6k数据点进行SFT训练。

Result: OpenSeeker-v2（30B ReAct范式）在4个基准上达到SOTA：BrowseComp 46.0%、BrowseComp-ZH 58.1%、Humanity's Last Exam 34.6%、xbench 78.0%，全面超越Tongyi DeepResearch（43.4%、46.7%、32.9%、75.0%）。

Conclusion: 高质量、高难度的信息性轨迹配合简单SFT可达到出人意料的强大效果。OpenSeeker-v2是首个在同等模型规模和范式下、由纯学术团队仅用SFT训练达到SOTA的搜索智能体。研究表明前沿搜索智能体研究可以被更多社区 접근。

Abstract: Deep search capabilities have become an indispensable competency for frontier Large Language Model (LLM) agents, yet their development remains dominated by industrial giants. The typical industry recipe involves a highly resource-intensive pipeline spanning pre-training, continual pre-training (CPT), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL). In this report, we show that when fueled with informative and high-difficulty trajectories, a simple SFT approach could be surprisingly powerful for training frontier search agents. By introducing three simple data synthesis modifications: scaling knowledge graph size for richer exploration, expanding the tool set size for broader functionality, and strict low-step filtering, we establish a stronger baseline. Trained on merely 10.6k data points, our OpenSeeker-v2 achieves state-of-the-art performance across 4 benchmarks (30B-sized agents with ReAct paradigm): 46.0% on BrowseComp, 58.1% on BrowseComp-ZH, 34.6% on Humanity's Last Exam, and 78.0% on xbench, surpassing even Tongyi DeepResearch trained with heavy CPT+SFT+RL pipeline, which achieves 43.4%, 46.7%, 32.9%, and 75.0%, respectively. Notably, OpenSeeker-v2 represents the first state-of-the-art search agent within its model scale and paradigm to be developed by a purely academic team using only SFT. We are excited to open-source the OpenSeeker-v2 model weights and share our simple yet effective findings to make frontier search agent research more accessible to the community.

</details>


### [84] [The Kerimov-Alekberli Model: An Information-Geometric Framework for Real-Time System Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24083)
*Hikmat Karimov,Rahid Zahid Alekberli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Kerimov-Alekberli模型，通过建立非平衡热力学与随机控制之间的形式同构，将AI安全重新定义为信息几何框架，利用KL散度和Fisher信息度量量系统异常，并基于Landauer原理证明对抗扰动通过增加信息熵做功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI安全研究依赖启发式规则，缺乏严格的物理基础。现有方法无法量化伦理违规的物理本质，需要从热力学角度建立可量化的安全范式。

Method: 1) 建立非平衡热力学与随机控制的形式同构；2) 定义系统异常为黎曼流形上的偏差；3) 使用KL散度作为主度量，Fisher信息度量作为动态阈值；4) 基于Landauer原理证明对抗扰动的物理做功机制。

Result: 在NSL-KDD数据集和无人机轨迹仿真中，基于FPT触发器实现实时检测，获得高准确率、低FPR等良好性能指标。

Conclusion: 该研究为AI安全提供了严格的热力学基础，将伦理违规锚定在可量化的物理做功和熵信息上，实现从启发式规则向热力学稳定性范式的转变。

Abstract: This study introduces the Kerimov-Alekberli model, a novel information-geometric framework that redefines AI safety by formally linking non-equilibrium thermodynamics to stochastic control for the ethical alignment of autonomous systems. By establishing a formal isomorphism between non-equilibrium thermodynamics and stochastic control, we define systemic anomalies as deviations from a Riemannian manifold. The model utilizes the Kullback-Leibler divergence as the primary metric, governed by a dynamic threshold derived from the Fisher Information Metric. We further ground this framework in the Landauer Principle, proving that adversarial perturbations perform measurable physical work by increasing the system's informational entropy. Validation on the NSL-KDD dataset and unmanned aerial vehicle trajectory simulations demonstrated that our model achieves effective real-time detection via the FPT trigger, with strong performance metrics (e.g., high accuracy and low FPR) on benchmark datasets. This study provides a rigorous physical foundation for AI safety, transitioning from heuristic, rule-based ethical frameworks to a thermodynamics-based stability paradigm by grounding ethical violations in quantifiable physical work and entropic information.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [Beyond Similarity Search: A Unified Data Layer for Production RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03275)
*Venkata Krishna Prasanth Budigi,Siri Chandana Sirigiri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于PostgreSQL+pgvector的统一数据层解决方案，解决RAG生产环境中的数据陈旧、租户泄漏和查询组合爆炸三大核心问题，实验表明可降低92%查询延迟并消除跨租户数据泄漏风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在生产环境中暴露出原型性能与实际可靠性之间的差距，根源在于传统分离式数据层存在的三个核心问题：数据陈旧、租户数据泄漏和查询组合爆炸。

Method: 使用PostgreSQL内置向量搜索(pgvector)和HNSW索引构建统一数据层，在50,000份文档上进行受控基准测试，对比传统分离式系统架构。

Result: 统一数据层实现了显著改进：日期过滤查询延迟降低92%，租户范围查询降低74%，零同步不一致，跨租户数据泄漏完全消除，同步代码减少93%。

Conclusion: 研究证明PostgreSQL+pgvector的统一数据层可有效解决RAG系统的三个核心问题，建议采用混合分层架构来平衡性能与成本。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems have become the standard architecture for grounding large language models in organizational knowledge. Yet production deployments consistently expose a gap between clean prototype performance and real-world reliability. This paper identifies three root causes of that gap: data staleness, tenant data leakage, and query composition explosion. All three trace back to the conventional split-system data layer. We propose and evaluate a unified data layer built on PostgreSQL with native vector search (pgvector) and HNSW indexing. Controlled benchmarks on 50,000 documents show 92% latency reduction for date-filtered queries, 74% for tenant-scoped queries, zero synchronization inconsistency, and complete elimination of cross-tenant data leakage with 93% less synchronization code. We additionally discuss a recommended hybrid tier architecture

</details>


### [86] [RAG over Thinking Traces Can Improve Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03344)
*Negar Arabzadeh,Wenjie Ma,Sewon Min,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究证明检索思考轨迹（问题解决过程中生成的中间思维轨迹）可显著提升推理任务性能，提出的T3方法将其结构化后作为检索语料，在多个强模型和基准测试中超越非RAG基线和标准网页检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为RAG对推理密集型任务（如数学和代码生成）收益有限，本研究挑战这一假设，认为局限在于语料选择而非RAG本身。

Method: 提出检索思考轨迹（thinking traces）而非传统文档，并开发T3方法将思考轨迹转换为结构化、易检索的表示形式，形成retrieve-then-generate管道。

Result: 在AIME 2025-2026、LiveCodeBench、GPQA-Diamond等基准上均取得一致提升，例如Gemini-2.5-Flash在AIME上相对提升+56.3%；同时可降低最多15%的推理成本。

Conclusion: Thinking traces是一种有效的推理任务检索语料，T3方法通过结构化、紧凑化或诊断性表示可进一步解锁更强性能提升，且几乎不增加额外推理成本。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has proven effective for knowledge-intensive tasks, but is widely believed to offer limited benefit for reasoning-intensive problems such as math and code generation. We challenge this assumption by showing that the limitation lies not in RAG itself, but in the choice of corpus. Instead of retrieving documents, we propose retrieving thinking traces, i.e., intermediate thinking trajectories generated during problem solving attempts. We show that thinking traces are already a strong retrieval source, and further introduce T3, an offline method that transforms them into structured, retrieval-friendly representations, to improve usability. Using these traces as a corpus, a simple retrieve-then-generate pipeline consistently improves reasoning performance across strong models and benchmarks such as AIME 2025--2026, LiveCodeBench, and GPQA-Diamond, outperforming both non-RAG baselines and retrieval over standard web corpora. For instance, on AIME, RAG with traces generated by Gemini-2-thinking achieves relative gains of +56.3%, +8.6%, and +7.6% for Gemini-2.5-Flash, GPT-OSS-120B, and GPT-5, respectively, even though these are more recent models. Interestingly, RAG on T3 also incurs little or no extra inference cost, and can even reduce inference cost by up to $15%$. Overall, our results suggest that thinking traces are an effective retrieval corpus for reasoning tasks, and transforming them into structured, compact, or diagnostic representations unlocks even stronger gains. Code available at https://github.com/Narabzad/t3.

</details>


### [87] [Revisiting General Map Search via Generative Point-of-Interest Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03397)
*Dong Chen,Shuai Zheng,Haoyang Shao,Hongsheng Wu,Muhao Xu,Yeyu Yan,Ruifang Li,Zhenfeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GenPOI是一个创新的生成式POI检索框架，旨在解决通用地图搜索中用户查询信息不足的问题。该框架将异构搜索上下文和POI统一为结构化序列，利用LLM进行空间感知的候选生成，并创新性地提出了Geo-Semantic POI Tokenization和proximity-aware constrained generation策略，在千万级POI数据集上验证了其卓越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统POI检索方法过度依赖浅层语义匹配，难以处理用户查询中信息不足的问题；同时现有检索范式难以有效整合异构上下文信息来推断复杂的搜索意图。

Method: 提出Geo-Semantic POI Tokenization方法，将每个POI表示为包含语义和地理上下文信息的紧凑token序列，以增强LLM的空间理解能力；同时采用proximity-aware constrained generation策略约束LLM的解码空间，确保生成结果的有效性和地理相关性。

Result: 在腾讯地图超过千万量级的工业级数据集上验证了该框架的优越性能。

Conclusion: GenPOI通过在腾讯地图超过千万量级的POI数据集上的实验验证了其优越性。该框架利用LLM强大的上下文建模能力，有效解决了传统方法难以处理的上下文依赖复杂查询问题，同时通过约束解码策略确保了生成结果的有效性和地理空间相关性。

Abstract: Point-of-Interest (POI) retrieval aims to identify relevant candidates from massive-scale POI databases, serving as a cornerstone for diverse location-based services. However, in general map search scenarios, conventional POI retrieval methods are increasingly challenged by underspecified user queries due to their excessive reliance on surface-level semantic matching. Meanwhile, such queries are often highly context-dependent and personalized, yet existing retrieval paradigms struggle to effectively synergize heterogeneous contexts for complex search intent inference. To address these limitations, we revisit general map search from a generative perspective and propose GenPOI, an innovative Generative POI retrieval framework tailored for general search on maps. It seamlessly unifies heterogeneous search contexts and POIs into structured sequences, leveraging the powerful contextual modeling of Large Language Models (LLMs) for spatial-aware candidate generation. Consequently, this generative paradigm effectively solves more challenging queries through profound context dependency modeling and search intent reasoning. Specifically, accounting for the unique geospatial nature of map scenarios, GenPOI introduces a novel Geo-Semantic POI Tokenization to represent each POI as a compact token sequence encoding both semantic and geographic context, thus grounding the LLM's spatial understanding. Additionally, a proximity-aware constrained generation strategy is employed to restrict the decoding space of the LLM, ensuring the validity and geospatial relevance of the generated results. Extensive experiments on large-scale industrial datasets from Tencent Map, comprising POIs at the scale of over 10 million, demonstrate the superior performance of GenPOI.

</details>


### [88] [Domain-Adaptive Dense Retrieval for Brazilian Legal Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04005)
*Jayr Pereira,Roberto Lotufo,Luiz Bonifacio*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究巴西法律检索的异构性问题，比较Qwen3-Embedding-4B模型在三种训练设置下的表现：基础模型、法律数据微调、混合训练（法律+SQuAD-pt）。混合训练在保持法律检索性能的同时显著提升了跨类型检索的鲁棒性，尤其在问答类检索任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西法律检索任务具有异构性特点，涵盖案例法、立法文本和问答型检索，这使得训练密集检索器时存在领域专业化与泛化能力之间的权衡困境。

Method: 基于Qwen3-Embedding-4B模型，采用三种训练设置进行对比：1）无微调的基础模型；2）仅使用法律数据训练；3）混合训练（法律数据+SQuAD-pt葡萄牙语监督数据集）。评估在JUÁ排行榜的五个法律数据集及Quati数据集上进行。

Result: 法律专用模型在专业化法律任务上表现最佳；混合训练设置在保持法律检索能力的同时实现了更好的整体平衡，在六个数据集上NDCG@10从0.414提升至0.447，MRR@10从0.586提升至0.595，MAP@10从0.270提升至0.308。Quati数据集上混合模型明显优于法律专用模型。

Conclusion: 法律专用训练与混合训练各具优势：前者更适合领域专业化，后者在跨检索类型（尤其是问答型检索）上更具鲁棒性。研究表明针对异构检索任务，混合训练是更平衡的选择。

Abstract: Brazilian legal retrieval is heterogeneous, covering case law, legislation, and question-based search. This makes training dense retrievers a trade-off between stronger domain specialization and broader robustness across retrieval types of search. In this paper, we explore this trade-off using three training setups based on Qwen3-Embedding-4B: a base model with no fine-tuning, a version trained only on legal data, and a mixed setup that combines legal data with SQuAD-pt supervised dataset. We evaluate these models on five legal datasets from the JUÁ leaderboard, along with Quati dataset as an extra Portuguese retrieval benchmark to test out-of-domain generalization. The legal-only model performs best on the most specialized legal tasks. The mixed setup keeps strong performance on legal data while offering a better overall balance, improving average NDCG@10 from 0.414 to 0.447, MRR@10 from 0.586 to 0.595, and MAP@10 from 0.270 to 0.308 across all six datasets. The biggest improvement appears on Quati, where the mixed model clearly outperforms the legal-only one. Overall, the results show that legal-only and mixed training lead to different strengths: the first is better for specialization, while the second is more robust across different types of search, especially question-based ones. Both adapted models are available on Hugging Face

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [eOptShrinkQ: Near-Lossless KV Cache Compression Through Optimal Spectral Denoising and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02905)
*Pei-Chun Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出eOptShrinkQ，一种基于随机矩阵理论的KV缓存压缩方法。通过奇异值收缩提取共享结构并对残差进行TurboQuant量化，在~2.2 bits/entry下实现优于现有方法的压缩质量，且谱去噪可作为检索任务的有效正则化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到transformer注意力头中的KV缓存可自然分解为低秩共享上下文分量与满秩每token残差，该结构符合尖峰随机矩阵模型(spiked random matrix model)，为压缩提供了理论基础。

Method: 提出两阶段压缩管道eOptShrinkQ：1) 使用最优奇异值收缩(eOptShrink)自动提取低秩共享上下文分量；2) 对满足薄壳性质且坐标局部化的残差使用TurboQuant标量量化。通过谱去噪恢复等距性，避免离群值处理和内积偏差校正的开销。

Result: 理论层面：BBP相变实现自动秩选择，残差内积偏差接近零，坐标去局部化保证近乎最优量化失真。实验验证：在Llama-3.1-8B和Ministral-8B上，eOptShrinkQ以约2.2 bits/entry节省近1 bit compared to TurboQuant；LongBench 16任务中以2.2 bits超越TurboQuant 3.0 bits；多针检索中以2.2 bits接近或超越FP16。

Conclusion: eOptShrinkQ通过将KV cache分解为低秩共享结构与残差两部分，结合最优奇异值收缩与TurboQuant量化，在约2.2 bits/entry下实现优于TurboQuant 3.0 bits的压缩效果，且在多针检索任务中可匹配甚至超越FP16原始精度，表明谱去噪可作为检索密集型任务的有效正则化手段。

Abstract: We show that the key-value (KV) cache in transformer attention heads admits a natural decomposition into a low-rank \emph{shared context} component and a full-rank \emph{per-token} residual, well described by the spiked random matrix model. This observation leads to eOptShrinkQ, a two-stage compression pipeline: optimal singular value shrinkage (eOptShrink) automatically extracts the shared structure, and the residual -- which satisfies the \emph{thin shell property} with delocalized coordinates -- is quantized by TurboQuant~\citep{zandieh2025turboquant}, a recently proposed per-vector scalar quantizer with near-optimal distortion guarantees. By restoring the isotropy that scalar quantization assumes, spectral denoising eliminates the need for both outlier handling and dedicated inner product bias correction, freeing those bits for improved reconstruction.
  The theoretical grounding in random matrix theory provides three guarantees: automatic rank selection via the BBP phase transition, provably near-zero inner product bias on the residual, and coordinate delocalization ensuring near-optimal quantization distortion. Experimentally, we validate eOptShrinkQ on Llama-3.1-8B and Ministral-8B across three levels: per-head MSE and inner product fidelity, where eOptShrinkQ saves nearly one bit per entry over TurboQuant at equivalent quality; end-to-end on LongBench (16 tasks), where eOptShrinkQ at $\sim$2.2 bits per entry outperforms TurboQuant at 3.0 bits; and multi-needle retrieval, where eOptShrinkQ at 2.2 bits closely matches or exceeds uncompressed FP16, suggesting that spectral denoising can act as a beneficial regularizer for retrieval-intensive tasks.

</details>


### [90] [An End-to-End Framework for Building Large Language Models for Software Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02906)
*Jingkai He,Pengfei Chen,Chenghui Wu,Shuang Liang,Ye Li,Gou Tan,Xiadao Wen,Chuanfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OpsLLM是一个面向软件运维领域的专用大语言模型，支持知识问答和根因分析任务。通过人类反馈机制构建高质量微调数据集，结合监督微调和领域过程奖励模型进行优化，在QA任务上提升0.2%~5.7%，在RCA任务上提升2.7%~70.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究因数据质量低、知识碎片化、学习不充分等问题，尚未实现高效有效的端到端智能运维。

Method: 1) 引入Human-in-the-Loop机制从原始运维数据中筛选高质量数据构建微调数据集；2) 基于数据开展监督微调获得基础模型；3) 在强化学习阶段引入领域过程奖励模型(DPRM)优化RCA任务准确性。

Result: OpsLLM在QA任务上提升0.2%~5.7%，在RCA任务上提升2.7%~70.3%，展现出强迁移能力。

Conclusion: 将开源7B、14B、32B三个版本的OpsLLM及15K微调数据集。

Abstract: In the field of software operations, Large Language Models (LLMs) have attracted increasing attention. However, existing research has not yet achieved efficient and effective end-to-end intelligent operations due to low-quality data, fragmented knowledge and insufficient learning. To explore the potential of LLMs in software operations, we propose OpsLLM, a domain-specific LLM that supports both knowledge-based question answering (QA) and root cause analysis (RCA). Moreover, we disclose the detailed workflow for building LLMs specifically in the software operations domain. First, a Human-in-the-Loop mechanism is introduced to curate highquality data from a large collection of operational raw data and construct a fine-tuning dataset. Then, based on the data, supervised fine-tuning is conducted to achieve a base model. Furthermore, we introduce a domain process reward model (DPRM) during the reinforcement learning stage to optimize the accuracy and reliability of the fine-tuned model on RCA tasks. Experimental results on the tasks with diverse difficulties demonstrate that OpsLLMs effectively learns and aligns with the operational domain knowledge infused, outperforming existing open-source and closed-source LLMs in accuracy with improvements of 0.2%~5.7% on QA tasks and 2.7% ~70.3% on RCA tasks, while exhibiting strong transferability. Moreover, we will open-source three versions of OpsLLM with 7B, 14B and 32B parameters, along with a 15K fine-tuning dataset.

</details>


### [91] [On the Invariants of Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02907)
*Wonsuk Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Transformer中softmax注意力机制的数学不变量：定义了能量场概念，发现其具有机制层面的秩边界和零和约束等代数性质，以及模型层面的关键不相干性导致的方差去中心化分布，这些发现可用于模型诊断和训练监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax注意力机制将每个query-key交互映射为概率分布，但其底层数学结构尚未得到充分探索。该研究旨在揭示注意力机制中普遍存在的不变量结构。

Method: 定义了能量场（行中心化的注意力logit）概念，理论分析了其代数性质（零和约束、秩边界、谱特征），并通过多种架构（GPT-2、LLaMA、Mistral等）、不同上下文长度和输入文本进行实验验证。

Result: 发现了两类不变量：(1) 机制层面的不变量来自softmax的代数结构，包括每行零和约束、由头维度决定的秩边界及谱特征；(2) 模型层面的规律性包括能量场的方差分布去中心化特性（关键不相干性）。

Conclusion: 发现softmax注意力具有可预测的代数结构（能量场），其中能量场被限制在由头维度决定的低维子空间中；模型层面的关键不相干性可作为训练监控指标。这些不变量为理解和诊断Transformer模型提供了理论框架和实践工具。

Abstract: Softmax attention maps every query--key interaction into a probability distribution, but the underlying structure remains largely unexplored. We define the \emph{energy field}, the row-centered attention logit, and show that it exhibits invariant properties across models, architectures, and inputs.
  Two classes of invariants emerge. \emph{Mechanism-level} invariants follow from the algebraic structure of softmax attention. They include a per-row zero-sum constraint, a rank bound determined by the head dimension, and spectral signatures that follow from them. \emph{Model-level} regularities are not required by the mechanism, yet hold in every autoregressive language model we test, spanning several architecture families. The energy field distributes its variance over key positions without concentrating at a few. This delocalization traces to a property of the key matrix we call \emph{key incoherence}.
  These invariants have practical consequences. The rank bound confines the energy field to a low-dimensional subspace. Key incoherence yields a per-head training monitor. All results are verified at multiple context lengths and input texts.

</details>


### [92] [Delay, Plateau, or Collapse: Evaluating the Impact of Systematic Verification Error on RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02909)
*Kazuki Egashira,Mark Vero,Jasper Dekoninck,Florian E. Dorner,Robin Staab,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验表明，RLVR中系统性验证错误（尤其是false positives）会导致训练失败，且失败模式取决于错误的具体分布而非整体错误率，挑战了此前关于验证误差影响有限的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior work assumes verification errors are random and independent, but practical verifiers tend to exhibit systematic errors that may cause models to learn incorrect consistent behaviors from structurally flawed reward signals.

Method: Controlled experiments on arithmetic tasks studying the impact of systematic verification errors (false positives vs. false negatives) on RLVR training dynamics and final performance.

Result: Systematic false positives cause diverse failure modes (sub-optimal plateaus to performance collapse), while false negatives behave like random noise. Critically, outcomes depend on error pattern rather than overall error rate, making pre-hoc mitigation challenging.

Conclusion: Verifier quality must be understood beyond sample-level error rates; realistic systematic errors can critically shape RLVR outcomes in ways that prior work underestimates.

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a powerful approach for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). While RLVR is designed for tasks with verifiable ground-truth answers, real-world verifiers (e.g., static code checkers) can introduce errors into the reward signal. Prior analyses have largely treated such errors as random and independent across samples, concluding that errors merely slow training with limited effect on final performance. However, practical verifiers tend to exhibit systematic errors. This introduces a risk of models learning unwanted consistent behavior from a structurally incorrect reward signal. In this work, we study the impact of such systematic verification errors on RLVR. Through controlled experiments on arithmetic tasks, we show that systematic false negatives lead to similar effects as random noise. On the other hand, systematic false positives can cause a wide range of behaviors from sub-optimal plateaus to performance collapse. Crucially, these outcomes are not determined by the overall error rate but by the specific pattern of introduced errors, making pre-hoc mitigation difficult. Our results show that, in contrast to prior conclusions, realistic verification errors can critically shape RLVR outcomes and that verifier quality has to be understood beyond its sample-level error rate.

</details>


### [93] [Moral Sensitivity in LLMs: A Tiered Evaluation of Contextual Bias via Behavioral Profiling and Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03217)
*Yash Aggarwal,Atmika Gorti,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Krishnaprasad Thirunarayan,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Moral Sensitivity Index (MSI)指标来量化LLM中的偏见问题，通过行为分析和机制验证两阶段方法，揭示了偏见随模型能力变化呈现U型曲线：小型模型偏见最强，指令微调消除偏见，但推理蒸馏又重新引入偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏见评估采用简单的"有偏见/无偏见"二元框架，无法捕捉偏见在实际场景中逐渐显现、依赖上下文的特点，需要更精细的评估方法和机制解释。

Method: 行为层面：构建七层压力测试的MSI指标，从抽象数值问题到历史社会经济不公场景；机制层面：使用logit lens、注意力分析、激活修补和语义探测等可解释性技术。

Result: Gemini 1.5在社会经济框架下Tier 5达到72.7% MSI；Claude表现出基于身份的安全训练导致的抑制效应。机制分析发现偏见呈现U型曲线：SLM强偏见→指令微调消除→推理蒸馏重新引入（达SLM水平）。

Conclusion: 推理蒸馏过程会压缩推理痕迹，可能重新激活浅层统计关联；社会负载线索能激活与机制分析识别的相同偏见驱动回路，实现跨阶段验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings that require nuanced ethical reasoning, yet existing bias evaluations treat model outputs as simply "biased" or "unbiased." This binary framing misses the gradual, context-sensitive way bias actually emerges. We address this gap in two stages: behavioral profiling and mechanistic validation. In the behavioral stage, we introduce the Moral Sensitivity Index (MSI), a metric that quantifies the probability of biased output across a graduated, seven-tier stress test ranging from abstract numerical problems to scenarios rooted in historical and socioeconomic injustice. Evaluating four leading models (Claude 3.5, Qwen 3.5, Llama 3, and Gemini 1.5), we identify distinct behavioral signatures shaped by alignment design: for instance, Gemini 1.5 reaches 72.7% MSI by Tier 5 under socioeconomic framing, while Claude exhibits sharp suppression consistent with identity-based safety training. We then verify these behavioral patterns mechanistically. We select criminal-bias scenarios, which produced the highest MSI scores across models, as probes and apply logit lens, attention analysis, activation patching, and semantic probing to a controlled set of six models spanning three capability tiers: small language models (SLMs), instruction-tuned base models, and reasoning-distilled variants. Circuit-level analysis reveals a U-curve of bias: SLMs exhibit strong criminal bias; scaling to instruction-tuned models eliminates it; reasoning distillation reintroduces bias to SLM-like levels despite identical parameter counts, suggesting distillation compresses reasoning traces in ways that reactivate shallow statistical associations. Critically, the socially loaded cues that drive high MSI scores activate the same bias-driving circuits identified mechanistically, providing cross-stage validation.

</details>


### [94] [Generate, Filter, Control, Replay: A Comprehensive Survey of Rollout Strategies for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02913)
*Rohan Surana,Gagan Mundada,Xunyi Jiang,Chuhan Wang,Zhenwei Tang,Difan Jiao,Zihan Huang,Yuxin Xiong,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Xintong Li,Raghav Jain,Nikki Kuang,Sizhe Zhou,Bowen Jin,Zhendong Chu,Tong Yu,Ryan Rossi,Kuan-Hao Huang,Jingbo Shang,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是首个从优化器无关视角全面审视推理LLM的RL后训练rollout策略的综述，提出GFCR四阶段_taxonomy（生成-过滤-控制-重放）和可靠性-覆盖率-成本敏感性权衡框架，涵盖方法综述、应用案例及诊断索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rollout（从prompt到终止的轨迹采样，包括推理步骤和工具交互）决定了RL优化器的学习数据，但rollout设计在研究中往往报道不足，方法间缺乏统一术语和系统分类。

Method: 提出GFCR生命周期_taxonomy：Generate（生成候选轨迹和拓扑）、Filter（通过验证器/判官/批评者构建中间信号）、Control（在预算约束下分配计算并做出继续/分支/停止决策）、Replay（跨rollout保留和复用数据，包括自进化课程）。并引入可靠性、覆盖率、成本敏感性三维标准来表征rollout权衡。

Result: 系统综述了涵盖可验证奖励RL、过程监督、判官门控、引导式/树状/分段rollout、自适应计算分配、早期退出与部分rollout、吞吐量优化、重放/重组自改进等方法；并在数学、代码/SQL、多模态推理、工具使用智能体、技能归纳与跨任务迁移等基准上进行了案例验证。

Conclusion: 该框架为构建可复现、计算高效、可信赖的推理LLM的RL rollout系统提供了系统性指导，并通过诊断索引映射常见病理学问题至GFCR模块，揭示了开放挑战。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central post-training tool for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs). In these systems, the rollout, the trajectory sampled from a prompt to termination, including intermediate reasoning steps and optional tool or environment interactions, determines the data the optimizer learns from, yet rollout design is often underreported. This survey provides an optimizer-agnostic view of rollout strategies for RL-based post-training of reasoning LLMs. We formalize rollout pipelines with unified notation and introduce Generate-Filter-Control-Replay (GFCR), a lifecycle taxonomy that decomposes rollout pipelines into four modular stages: Generate proposes candidate trajectories and topologies; Filter constructs intermediate signals via verifiers, judges, critics; Control allocates compute and makes continuation/branching/stopping decisions under budgets; and Replay retains and reuses artifacts across rollouts without weight updates, including self-evolving curricula that autonomously generate new training tasks. We complement GFCR with a criterion taxonomy of reliability, coverage, and cost sensitivity that characterizes rollout trade-offs. Using this framework, we synthesize methods spanning RL with verifiable rewards, process supervision, judge-based gating, guided and tree/segment rollouts, adaptive compute allocation, early-exit and partial rollouts, throughput optimization, and replay/recomposition for self-improvement. We ground the framework with case studies in math, code/SQL, multimodal reasoning, tool-using agents, and agentic skill benchmarks that evaluate skill induction, reuse, and cross-task transfer. Finally, we provide a diagnostic index that maps common rollout pathologies to GFCR modules and mitigation levers, alongside open challenges for building reproducible, compute-efficient, and trustworthy rollout pipelines.

</details>


### [95] [When Safety Geometry Collapses: Fine-Tuning Vulnerabilities in Agentic Guard Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02914)
*Ismail Hossain,Sai Puppala,Jannatul Ferdaus,Md Jahangir Alam,Yoonpyo Lee,Syed Bahauddin Alam,Sajedul Talukder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了安全guard模型通过标准领域特化而非对抗攻击丧失安全对齐的现象，并提出FW-SSR方法通过恢复安全子空间几何结构来有效缓解此问题，证明表征几何监测是guard模型安全评估的必要组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guard模型在仅使用良性数据进行标准微调时会丧失安全对齐能力，而非通过对抗性操纵；这对于agentic AI管道中的保护层构成严重安全威胁。

Method: 提取每层安全子空间：通过对类别条件激活差异进行SVD分解；追踪良性微调下安全边界的演化；提出FW-SSR（Fisher加权安全子空间正则化），结合对角Fisher信息的曲率感知方向权重与随任务-安全梯度冲突自适应调整的λ_t。

Result: Granite Guardian完全崩溃（拒绝率从85%降至0%，CKA归零）；FW-SSR在Granite Guardian上恢复75%拒绝率（CKA=0.983），将WildGuard攻击成功率降至3.6%（低于未修改基线）。

Conclusion: Safety表示几何（以CKA和Fisher评分衡量）比绝对位移指标更能可靠地预测安全行为；几何基监控应成为guard模型评估的必要组成部分。集中化的安全表示高效但具有灾难性脆弱性（特化假说）。

Abstract: A guard model fine-tuned on entirely benign data can lose all safety alignment -- not through adversarial manipulation, but through standard domain specialization. We demonstrate this failure across three purpose-built safety classifiers -- LlamaGuard, WildGuard, and Granite Guardian -- deployed as protection layers in agentic AI pipelines, and show that it originates in the destruction of latent safety geometry: the structured harmful -- benign representational boundary that guides classification. We extract per-layer safety subspaces via SVD on class-conditional activation differences and track how this boundary evolves under benign fine-tuning. Granite Guardian undergoes complete collapse --  refusal rate drops from 85\% to 0\%, CKA falls to zero, and 100\% of outputs become ambiguous -- a severity exceeding prior findings on general-purpose LLMs, explained by the specialization hypothesis: concentrated safety representations are efficient but catastrophically brittle. To mitigate this, we propose Fisher-Weighted Safety Subspace Regularization (FW-SSR), a training-time penalty combining (i) curvature-aware direction weights derived from diagonal Fisher information and (ii) an adaptive $λ_t$ that scales with task-safety gradient conflict. FW-SSR recovers 75\% refusal on Granite Guardian (CKA = 0.983) and reduces WildGuard's Attack Success Rate to 3.6\% -- below the unmodified baseline -- by actively sharpening the safety subspace rather than merely anchoring it. Across all three models, structural representational geometry (CKA, Fisher score) predicts safety behavior more reliably than absolute displacement metrics, establishing geometry-based monitoring as a necessary component of guard model evaluation in agentic deployments.

</details>


### [96] [AutoRAGTuner: A Declarative Framework for Automatic Optimization of RAG Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02967)
*Xintan Zeng,Yongchao Liu,Yice Luo,Jiajun Zhen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoRAGTuner是一个声明式、配置驱动的RAG自动化框架，通过模块化架构、领域元素模型(DEM)和自适应贝叶斯优化引擎，实现RAG全生命周期自动化。实验证明其在多种RAG流程中优于基线，并显著降低工程开销（代码变更减少95%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG enhances LLMs but performance is highly sensitive to complex architecture designs and hyper-parameter configurations, which currently rely on inefficient manual tuning.

Method: 1) Modular architecture with component registration mechanism to decouple pipeline stages; 2) Domain-Element Model (DEM) with bidirectional pointers for unified representation of heterogeneous data (nodes, edges, hyperedges); 3) Adaptive Bayesian optimization engine for end-to-end hyper-parameter tuning.

Result: AutoRAGTuner consistently outperforms default baselines across diverse RAG pipelines (vanilla to graph-based). It significantly reduces engineering overhead with declarative configuration language achieving up to 95% code churn reduction for architectural adjustments.

Conclusion: AutoRAGTuner provides a systematically optimizable foundation for building evolvable and reusable RAG systems, with up to 95% code churn reduction for architectural adjustments.

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances LLMs, but performance is highly sensitive to complex architecture designs and hyper-parameter configurations, which currently rely on inefficient manual tuning. We present AutoRAGTuner, a declarative, configuration-driven framework that automates the RAG life cycle: construction, execution,evaluation, and optimization. AutoRAGTuner employs a modular architecture to decouple pipeline stages through a component registration mechanism. To unify heterogeneous data, we introduce the Domain-Element Model (DEM), representing objects as atomic elements with bidirectional pointers to support nodes, edges, and hyperedges. Furthermore, AutoRAGTuner integrates an adaptive Bayesian optimization engine for end-to-end hyper-parameter tuning. Experimental results demonstrate AutoRAGTuner's architectural generality: across diverse RAG pipelines, ranging from vanilla to graph-based, the framework consistently outperforms default baselines. Notably, AutoRAGTuner significantly mitigates engineering overhead, where its declarative configuration language enables a up to 95\% reduction in code churn for architectural adjustments. Overall, AutoRAGTuner provides a systematically optimizable foundation for building evolvable and reusable RAG systems.

</details>


### [97] [From Synthesis to Clinical Assistance: A Strategy-Aware Agent Framework for Autism Intervention based on Real Clinical Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02916)
*Junhong Lai,Shuzhong Lai,Yanhao Yu,Wanlin Chen,Chenyu Yan,Haifeng Li,Lin Yao,Yueming Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASDAgent是一个策略感知框架，通过DoctorAgent的O-T-A-C推理机制和ChildAgent的概率行为建模，解决了ABA干预中数据稀缺与LLM策略不一致的双重挑战，并在对话质量和真实干预中验证了有效性，同时证明了合成数据蒸馏小模型的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助早期密集行为干预（EIBI）面临数据稀缺困境；同时，现有通用大语言模型（LLM）虽语言流畅，但难以严格遵循ABA标准化流程，存在策略不一致问题。

Method: 1) DoctorAgent：引入O-T-A-C（Observe-Think-Act-Correct）推理循环，使ABA执行过程显式可控，解决策略崩溃问题；2) ChildAgent：采用概率行为建模，模拟ASD儿童的多样化、非确定性响应模式。实验采用KL散度衡量策略分布相似性，并以合成数据蒸馏小语言模型（SLM）验证应用价值。

Result: 1) 生成的对话在策略分布上与人类治疗师高度接近（KL散度：0.083）；2) 实际干预中策略一致性达近80%；3) 合成数据能有效将专业知识蒸馏到小模型，显著提升其治疗能力。

Conclusion: ASDAgent通过整合策略感知的DoctorAgent和概率建模的ChildAgent，有效解决了ABA领域数据稀缺和LLM策略不一致的核心问题，证明了合成数据可成功蒸馏到小模型中。

Abstract: The development of AI-assisted Early Intensive Behavioral Intervention (EIBI) for Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) is severely constrained by data scarcity. Furthermore, while Applied Behavior Analysis (ABA) serves as the gold standard for clinical intervention, general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to strictly adhere to its standardized procedures, often resulting in interactions that are linguistically fluent but strategically inconsistent. To address these challenges, we introduce \textsc{ASDAgent}, a strategy-aware framework designed to unify high-fidelity intervention dialogue synthesis and clinical decision support. \textsc{ASDAgent} incorporates two specialized components to solve distinct problems: (i) a \textsc{DoctorAgent} equipped with an Observe-Think-Act-Correct (O-T-A-C) reasoning loop, which resolves the issue of strategy collapse in LLMs by making ABA execution explicit and controllable; and (ii) a \textsc{ChildAgent} that utilizes probabilistic behavior modeling to mitigate data homogeneity, simulating diverse and non-deterministic ASD response patterns. Experiments demonstrate that dialogues generated by \textsc{ASDAgent} closely mirror the strategy distribution of human therapists (KL divergence: 0.083). In real autism intervention, \textsc{ASDAgent} achieves nearly 80\% strategic consistency with human experts. Moreover, we show that synthetic data produced by \textsc{ASDAgent} effectively distills professional clinical knowledge into small language models (SLMs), significantly enhancing their therapeutic capabilities.

</details>


### [98] [Mitigating the reconstruction-detection trade-off in VAE-based unsupervised anomaly detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02918)
*Agathe Senellart,Maëlys Solal,Stéphanie Allassonnière,Ninon Burgos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究β-VAE模型在无监督异常检测中的模型选择问题，揭示了重建质量与异常检测之间存在权衡关系，并提出beta调度和稀疏VAE两种方法来缓解这一矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分自编码器(VAE)在无监督异常检测中应用广泛，但模型选择仍是一个开放问题。在完全无监督的情况下，超参数通常通过最小化正常样本的重建误差来选择，这忽略了异常检测性能与重建质量之间的潜在冲突。

Method: 1) 分析β-VAE模型中重建质量与异常检测性能之间的权衡；2) 研究随机种子带来的性能变异性，发现其与正常/异常样本潜空间分布距离相关；3) 提出两种缓解方法：beta调度和稀疏VAE。

Result: 约束潜空间的模型能获得更高的检测指标，但重建质量较低；性能变异性与正常和异常潜空间分布距离相关。

Conclusion: 稀疏VAE特别有效，在保持高重建质量的同时提升了异常检测性能，可有效缓解重建-检测权衡问题。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders are widely used for unsupervised anomaly detection. Model selection however remains an open-question: to remain fully unsupervised, hyperparameters are often chosen to minimize the reconstruction error on normal samples. In this paper, we reveal a trade-off between reconstruction quality and anomaly detection among $β$-VAE models. Models with constrained latent space reach higher detection metrics but lower reconstruction quality. We also assess the performance variability across random seeds and show it is linked to the distance between normal and abnormal latent distributions. From this analysis, we justify and investigate two methods to mitigate the reconstructiondetection tradeoff: beta-scheduling and the Sparse VAE. The latter especially shows an improvement in detection while maintaining high reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [99] [DeRelayL: Sustainable Decentralized Relay Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02935)
*Haihan Duan,Tengfei Ma,Yuyang Qin,Runhao Zeng,Wei Cai,Victor C. M. Leung,Xiping Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In the era of big data, large-scale machine learning models have revolutionized various fields, driving significant advancements. However, large-scale model training demands high financial and computational resources, which are only affordable by a few technological giants and well-funded institutions. In this case, common users like mobile users, the real creators of valuable data, are often excluded from fully benefiting due to the barriers, while the current methods for accessing large-scale models either limit user ownership or lack sustainability. This growing gap highlights the urgent need for a collaborative model training approach, allowing common users to train and share models. However, existing collaborative model training paradigms, especially federated learning (FL), primarily focus on data privacy and group-based model aggregation. To this end, this paper intends to address this issue by proposing a novel training paradigm named decentralized relay learning (DeRelayL), a sustainable learning system where permissionless participants can contribute to model training in a relay-like manner and share the model. In detail, this paper presents the architecture and workflow of DeRelayL, designs incentive mechanisms to ensure sustainability, and conducts theoretical analysis and numerical simulations to demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [100] [Proteo-R1: Reasoning Foundation Models for De Novo Protein Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02937)
*Fang Wu,Weihao Xuan,Heli Qi,Hanqun Cao,Heng-Jui Chang,Zeqi Zhou,Haokai Zhao,Ma Jian,Carl Ma,Yu-Chi Cheng,Kuan Pang,Xiangru Tang,Zehong Wang,Guanlue Li,Hanchen Wang,Kejun Ying,Pan Lu,Chiho Im,Seungju Han,Peng Xia,Tinson Xu,Yinxi Li,Deyao Zhu,Pheng-Ann Heng,Naoto Yokoya,Masashi Sugiyama,Li Erran Li,Jure Leskovec,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Proteo-R1是一个推理引导的蛋白设计框架，通过双专家架构将LLM的分子理解能力与扩散模型的生成能力解耦。MLLM识别关键功能残基作为硬约束，扩散模型在约束下进行条件协同设计，从而提升蛋白设计的可解释性、可控性和模块化程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习蛋白设计模型多为"非深思熟虑"型：直接合成分子几何结构，而不显式推理哪些残基或相互作用在功能上至关重要。这导致设计决策与连续采样动态纠缠，限制了模型的可解释性、可控性和生化知识的系统性复用。

Method: 采用双专家架构：多模态大语言模型(MLLM)作为"理解专家"，分析蛋白质序列、结构及文本上下文以识别关键功能残基；扩散模型作为"生成专家"，在约束条件下执行条件协同设计。通过将推理操作化为显式残基级承诺而非潜在文本指导，实现推理与生成的解耦。

Result: 该框架通过因子化设计——将分子理解与几何生成显式解耦——实现了稳定的LLM推理与先进几何生成模型的集成。

Conclusion: Proteo-R1实现了LLM推理与先进几何生成模型之间的稳定、可解释和模块化集成，其设计理念模拟了人类专家的分子工程思路：先推理关键相互作用，再优化几何结构。

Abstract: Deep learning in \emph{de novo} protein design has achieved atomic-level fidelity. However, existing models remain largely non-deliberative: they directly synthesize molecular geometries without explicitly reasoning about which residues or interactions are functionally essential. As a result, design decisions are entangled with continuous sampling dynamics, limiting interpretability, controllability, and systematic reuse of biochemical knowledge. We introduce \textbf{Proteo-R1}, a reasoning-guided protein design framework that explicitly decouples \emph{molecular understanding} from \emph{geometric generation}. Proteo-R1 adopts a dual-expert architecture in which a multimodal large language model (MLLM) serves as an \emph{understanding expert}, analyzing protein sequences, structures, and textual context to identify key functional residues that govern binding and specificity. These residue-level decisions are then passed as hard constraints to a separate diffusion-based \emph{generation expert}, which performs conditional co-design while respecting the fixed interaction anchors. This factorization mirrors how human experts approach molecular engineering: first, reasoning about critical interactions, then optimizing geometry subject to those constraints. By operationalizing reasoning as explicit residue-level commitments rather than latent textual guidance, Proteo-R1 achieves stable, interpretable, and modular integration of LLM reasoning with state-of-the-art geometric generative models. Code, data, and demos are available at https://smiles724.github.io/r1/.

</details>


### [101] [From Static Analysis to Audience Dissemination: A Training-Free Multimodal Controversy Detection Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02939)
*Zihan Ding,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuDisAgent，一个无需训练的多智能体框架，将多模态争议检测(MCD)从静态表示学习任务重新定义为动态传播过程。通过 Screening Agents（视频、评论、交互）和 Viewing Panel Agent 模拟不同受众群体的观点差异，解决冷启动问题。在公开数据集上显著优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将MCD视为静态表示学习任务，直接从视频和评论中提取特征，忽视了不同受众群体对同一内容可能持有不同观点和评价这一关键问题。现实中，内容在传播过程中会因受众背景差异而产生争议，但现有方法未能建模这一动态传播机制。

Method: 提出AuDisAgent框架，包含：(1) 三个 Screening Agents（Video Agent处理视觉、Comment Agent处理文本、Interaction Agent处理跨模态）分别从不同角度进行初步评估；(2) 当三 Agent 无法达成共识时，激活 Viewing Panel Agent 模拟不同背景和立场受众的讨论；(3) Arbitration Agent 根据完整推理链做出最终判断。此外，设计 Comment Bootstrapping Strategy 利用语义相似视频的历史评论解决冷启动问题。

Result: 在公开数据集上的广泛实验表明，该框架在富评论和有限评论两种场景下均显著优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 通过将MCD重新定义为动态传播过程并引入多智能体协作机制，AuDisAgent能够有效捕捉受众观点差异，发现潜在争议内容。该框架为社交视频平台的风险管理提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Multimodal controversy detection (MCD) identifies controversial content in videos and their associated user comments, to support risk management for social video platforms.Prior research frames MCD as a static representation learning task, where features are directly extracted from videos and their accompanying comments. However, these methods fail to capture the diverse perspectives and evaluations from different audience groups. Inspired by the real-world process of content dissemination among audiences, we propose AuDisAgent, a training-free multi-agent framework that reformulates MCD as a dynamic propagation process.Our framework explicitly models audience dissemination through a structured multi-agent system. First, three specialized Screening Agents (Video Agent, Comment Agent, and Interaction Agent) conduct initial assessments from visual, textual, and cross-modal perspectives, respectively. For samples where the three agents cannot reach a consensus, a Viewing Panel Agent is activated to simulate post-screening discussions among audiences with diverse backgrounds and stances. This mechanism models how different audience groups interpret and react to the same content, uncovering latent controversial content that may emerge during the dissemination process. Finally, an Arbitration Agent renders the final judgment based on the complete reasoning chain from the preceding steps.In addition, to address the "cold-start" scenario where newly released videos have few or no comments, we design a Comment Bootstrapping Strategy that leverages historical public comments from semantically similar videos as the initial comment context. Extensive experiments on a public dataset demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in both rich-comment and limited-comment scenarios.

</details>


### [102] [PrismAgent: Illuminating Harm in Memes via a Zero-Shot Interpretable Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02940)
*Zihan Ding,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PrismAgent，一个零样本、多智能体、可解释的模因（meme）有害内容检测框架。该框架将检测任务类比为刑事案件调查，通过分析员、调查员、检察官和法官四个专业智能体的协作流程完成零样本条件下的模因检测，在三个公共数据集上显著优于现有零样本检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有害模因检测方法高度依赖大规模标注数据，导致训练成本高昂且泛化能力有限，难以应对模因内容的快速传播和多样化演变。

Method: PrismAgent采用多智能体协作框架，包含四个专业智能体：1）分析员智能体从善恶意两个角度对模因进行改写以探测其潜在意图；2）调查员智能体从未标注数据集中检索支持证据并构建上下文解释；3）检察官智能体将原始模因与三种解释两两配对进行独立预判；4）法官智能体综合所有证据做出最终裁决。该框架的核心创新在于将检测任务类比为刑事案件调查流程，通过结构化协作实现零样本检测。

Result: 在三个公共数据集上的广泛实验表明，PrismAgent显著优于现有零样本检测方法。

Conclusion: PrismAgent成功实现了无需大量标注数据的模因有害内容检测，其多阶段推理链设计使框架具有内在可解释性，每个中间步骤均被明确解释而非仅输出最终检测结果。

Abstract: The rapid spread of memes makes harmful content detection increasingly crucial, as effective identification can curb the circulation of misinformation. However, existing methods rely heavily on high-volume annotated data, which leads to substantial training costs and limited generalization. To address these challenges, we propose PrismAgent, a zero-shot, multi-agent, interpretable framework. PrismAgent conceptualizes this task as a criminal case investigation, employing four specialized agents responsible for the analysis, investigation, prosecution, and judgment stages within a structured collaborative workflow. In the first stage, the analyst agent paraphrases each meme under benevolent and malicious assumptions to probe its underlying intent. The investigator agent then retrieves supporting evidence from an unannotated dataset and constructs contextual interpretations for the meme and its variants. Next, the prosecutor agent performs three independent preliminary judgments by pairing the original meme with each of the three interpretations. Finally, the judge agent deliberates across all evidence to render a final verdict. Moreover, PrismAgent's explicit multi-stage reasoning chain makes the model inherently interpretable, as every intermediate step is explicitly explained rather than only producing a final detection result. Extensive experiments on three public datasets show that PrismAgent significantly outperforms existing zero-shot detection methods.

</details>


### [103] [Healthcare AI GYM for Medical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02943)
*Minbyul Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出gym环境用于医学AI智能体的多轮强化学习训练，发现多轮交互结构容易退化为单轮冗长独白，提出TT-OPD方法通过结果感知KL正则化提升训练效率和稳定性，在18个基准中10个取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床推理需要多轮交互（收集病史、订购检测、解释结果、做出治疗决策），但缺乏统一且覆盖广泛临床领域和专用工具的训练环境来通过强化学习训练可泛化的医学AI智能体。

Method: 构建gymnasium兼容环境gym，包含10个临床领域、3.6K+任务、135个领域专用工具和828K医学知识库；提出TT-OPD方法，使用无梯度EMA教师利用结果特权信息在每轮对话提供密集的结果感知KL正则化。

Result: TT-OPD在18个基准中的10个取得最佳性能，平均提升+3.9pp；收敛更快，响应长度可控，多轮工具使用得以维持。

Conclusion: 稀疏终端奖励与顺序临床轨迹的对齐不当是导致多轮结构退化和蒸馏不稳定的主要原因；TT-OPD通过密集的结果感知正则化有效解决了训练不稳定问题，提升了医学AI智能体的训练效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Clinical reasoning demands multi-step interactions -- gathering patient history, ordering tests, interpreting results, and making safe treatment decisions -- yet a unified training environment provides the breadth of clinical domains and specialized tools to train generalizable medical AI agents through reinforcement learning remains elusive. We present a comprehensive empirical study of multi-turn agentic RL for medical AI, built on \gym{}, a gymnasium-compatible environment spanning 10 clinical domains with 3.6K+ tasks, 135 domain-specific tools, and a knowledge base of 828K medical passages. Our analysis reveals that agentic multi-turn structure degrades into verbose single-turn monologues, characterized by monotonic length explosion and a simultaneous erosion of tool-use frequency. We characterize how this collapse, alongside distillation instability, stems from the misalignment of sparse terminal rewards with sequential clinical trajectories. We find that vanilla GRPO achieves strong final accuracy on some benchmarks but suffers from training instability, evidenced by significant oscillations in response length and prolonged convergence periods. To improve training efficiency and stability, we propose Turn-level Truncated On-Policy Distillation (TT-OPD), a self-distillation framework where a gradient-free EMA teacher leverages outcome-privileged information to provide dense, outcome-aware KL regularization at every conversation turn. TT-OPD achieves the best performance on 10 of 18 benchmarks with an average +3.9~pp improvement over the non-RL baseline with faster early convergence, controlled response length, and sustained multi-turn tool use.

</details>


### [104] [RouteHijack: Routing-Aware Attack on Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02946)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RouteHijack是一种针对MoE大语言模型的路由感知越狱攻击方法，通过识别安全关键专家并优化输入后缀来操控路由决策，从而绕过安全对齐机制。该方法在多种MoE架构上展现出高攻击成功率和强迁移性，揭示了稀疏专家架构的根本性安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击方法存在显著局限：基于提示词的越狱攻击依赖启发式搜索且迁移性差；模型干预方法需要特权访问内部表征；优化型输入攻击因MoE不可微分的路由机制而受限。因此，需要一种专门针对MoE架构路由特性的新型攻击方法。

Method: 1) 响应驱动的专家定位：通过对比安全拒绝响应与有害完成响应的激活模式，识别安全关键专家(safety-critical experts)和有害专家(harmful experts)；2) 构建对抗后缀：设计路由感知(routing-aware)的优化目标，包含三个核心组件——抑制安全专家激活、促进有害专家激活、阻止生成早期的拒绝行为；3) 推理时将优化后的对抗后缀附加到恶意提示词，仅需输入级访问权限即可实施攻击。

Result: 在7个MoE LLM上，RouteHijack平均攻击成功率达69.3%，相比先前优化攻击方法提升3.2倍；在5个同源MoE变体间实现零样本迁移，ASR从27.7%提升至61.2%；在3个基于MoE的多模态大语言模型(VLM)上同样有效，ASR从2.47%提升至38.7%。

Conclusion: RouteHijack揭示了稀疏专家架构的根本性安全漏洞，表明仅在输出层面进行对齐远远不够，需要设计针对路由机制的新型防御措施。该研究对MoE模型的安全性评估和防护具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Safety alignment is critical for the responsible deployment of large language models (LLMs). As Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are increasingly adopted to scale model capacity, understanding their safety robustness becomes essential. Existing adversarial attacks, however, have notable limitations. Prompt-based jailbreaks rely on heuristic search and transfer poorly, model intervention methods require privileged access to internal representations, and optimization-based input attacks remain output-centric and are fundamentally limited to MoE models due to the non-differentiable routing mechanism.
  In this paper, we present RouteHijack, a routing-aware jailbreak for MoE LLMs. Our key insight is that safety behavior is concentrated in a small subset of experts, creating an opportunity to steer model behavior by influencing routing decisions through input optimization. Building on this observation, RouteHijack first performs response-driven expert localization to identify safety-critical and harmful experts by contrasting activations under safe refusals and harmful completions.
