<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造（AM）和熔丝制造（FFF）虽已普及，但将工艺规划责任转移到缺乏制造专业知识的用户手中，存在因G代码错误导致的热力学或几何缺陷风险。需要在打印前进行G代码筛选以避免浪费材料和机时。

Method: 提出LLM-ADAM框架，采用三角色分解架构：Extractor-LLM将G代码映射为结构化工艺参数模式；Reference-LLM将打印机和材料文档转换为对齐的运行范围；Judge-LLM解读确定性偏差表和G代码证据，判定零件是否无缺陷或属于特定异常类别。该框架可适配不同打印机、材料和LLM后端。

Result: 在N=200的FFF G代码语料库上评估，涵盖两个桌面打印机系列、两种材料、五种类别（非缺陷、欠挤出、过挤出、翘曲、搭桥）。最佳配置达到87.5%准确率，比最强单LLM基线（59.5%）提升28个百分点。结构化分解是主要改进来源，缺陷类别识别接近上限，残余误差集中在非缺陷样本的保守误报。

Conclusion: 结构化任务分解（多角色LLM协作）比单纯依赖更强 backbone 模型更能提升性能。该框架具有良好的通用性，可适应不同打印机、材料和LLM后端，为工业3D打印质量控制提供了可扩展的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Additive manufacturing (AM) continues to transform modern manufacturing by enabling flexible, on-demand production of complex geometries across diverse industries. Fused filament fabrication (FFF) has extended AM to laboratories, classrooms, and small production environments, but this accessibility shifts process-planning responsibility to users who may lack manufacturing expertise. A syntactically valid slicer profile can still encode thermally or geometrically harmful settings, and subtle G-code edits can alter extrusion, cooling, or adhesion before a print begins. Pre-print G-code screening catches accidental or adversarial machine-program errors before material or machine time is wasted. This paper proposes LLM-ADAM as a generalizable LLM framework for pre-print anomaly detection in AM. The framework decomposes the task into three roles: Extractor-LLM maps a G-code file to a structured process-parameter schema; Reference-LLM converts printer and material documentation into aligned operating ranges; and Judge-LLM interprets a deterministic deviation table and G-code evidence to decide whether a part is non-defective or belongs to an anomaly class. Printers, materials, and LLM backbones are interchangeable test conditions, not fixed assumptions. We evaluate the framework on an N=200 FFF G-code corpus spanning two desktop printer families, two materials, and five classes including non-defective, under-extrusion, over-extrusion, warping, and stringing. The best framework configuration reaches 87.5% accuracy, compared with 59.5% for the strongest engineered single-LLM baseline. The results show that structured decomposition, rather than backbone strength alone, is the dominant source of improvement, with defect classes identified at or near ceiling for leading configurations while residual errors concentrate on conservative false alarms for non-defective samples.

</details>


### [142] [Toward Structural Multimodal Representations: Specialization, Selection, and Sparsification via Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03348)
*Hahyeon Choi,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S3框架通过结构化视角重新思考多模态学习，将多模态输入分解为语义专家并进行选择性路由，在MultiBench四个基准上提升准确率，发现稀疏性与性能呈倒U型关系，峰值出现在中等稀疏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态学习方法通常将所有信号编码到固定嵌入中，或依赖对比学习和InfoMax驱动方法，存在灵活性不足和冗余信息处理的问题。

Method: 提出S3框架，包含三个核心机制：(1) Specialization专业化，在共享潜在空间形成概念级专家；(2) Selection选择，为任务特定需求自适应路由；(3) Sparsification稀疏化，修剪低效用路径以产生紧凑的信息最小化表示。

Result: 在MultiBench四个基准上提升准确率；发现一致的倒U型稀疏性-性能趋势，峰值性能出现在中等稀疏度。

Conclusion: 将多模态表示结构化为可选择的语义组件，为对比学习或InfoMax驱动方法提供了实用且有原则的替代方案。

Abstract: We propose S3 (Specialization, Selection, Sparsification), a framework that rethinks multimodal learning through a structural perspective. Instead of encoding all signals into a fixed embedding, S3 decomposes multimodal inputs into semantic experts and selectively routes them for each task. Specialization forms concept-level experts in a shared latent space, Selection adapts routing for task-specific needs, and Sparsification prunes low-utility paths to yield compact, information-minimal representations. Across four MultiBench benchmarks, S3 improves accuracy and shows a consistent reverse U-shaped sparsity-performance trend, with peak performance at intermediate sparsity. These results suggest that structuring multimodal representations as selectable semantic components provides a practical and principled alternative to contrastive learning or InfoMax-driven approaches.

</details>


### [143] [Fully Automatic Trace Gas Plume Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03372)
*Vít Růžička,David R. Thompson,Jay E. Fahlen,Amanda M. Lopez,Steven Lu,Chuchu Xiang,Holly Bender,Daniel Jensen,Philip G. Brodrick,Jake Lee,Brian Bue,Daniel H. Cusworth,Luis Guanter,Adam Chlus,Andrew Thorpe,Robert O. Green*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 开发了一个全自动框架，结合机器学习形态分析与物理光谱拟合，用于自动检测EMIT成像光谱仪数据中的痕量气体点源。日常分析模式可自动检测最大羽流，回溯分析发现至少25%的羽流被先前人工审查遗漏。此外还将检测范围扩展到NH3、NO2和CO（首次在EMIT图像中观测到）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来成像光谱仪将产生海量数据，传统人工检测无法应对，需要开发自动化痕量气体点源检测系统。

Method: 结合机器学习形态分析与物理光谱拟合的全自动框架，采用双模式运行：日常摘要模式（自动处理所有下行数据）和回溯分析模式（识别人工审查遗漏的羽流）。

Result: 日常摘要模式可自动检测最大羽流且假阳性极低；回溯分析表明至少25%的羽流曾被遗漏；首次在EMIT图像中实现对NH3、NO2和CO羽流的检测。

Conclusion: 该自动化框架能显著提升痕量气体点源检测效率，回溯分析揭示现有检测方法存在较大漏洞，同时证明了EMIT数据在多种痕量气体检测中的潜力。

Abstract: Future imaging spectrometers will increase data volumes by orders of magnitude, requiring automated detection of trace gas point sources. We present a fully automated framework that combines machine learning-based morphological analysis with physics-based spectroscopic fitting to detect plumes without human participation. Applied to EMIT imaging spectrometer data, the system operates in two modes: "daily digest" that runs automatically on all downlinked data, flagging the largest events for immediate response, and a retrospective analysis that identifies plumes missed by prior human review. The daily digest demonstrates that a significant fraction of the largest plumes can be detected automatically with negligible false positives, while retrospective analysis suggests at least 25% of plumes may have been overlooked. In addition to the previously observed methane point sources, we extend detection to three understudied trace gases: NH3, NO2 and the first observations of carbon monoxide (CO) plume in EMIT imagery.

</details>


### [144] [Learning Dynamics of Zeroth-Order Optimization: A Kernel Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03373)
*Zhe Li,Bicheng Ying,Zidong Liu,Haibo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了经典优化理论预测与LLM微调实践之间的悖论：通过推导ZO-SGD的单步学习动态，证明经验神经切向核(eNTK)的近似保真度由扰动数量决定而非模型参数维度，从而为ZO方法在大规模LLM微调中的成功提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决经典优化理论与实际应用之间的矛盾：理论上ZO算法存在维度依赖的 slowdown，但在大规模LLM微调中却表现成功。

Method: 推导ZO-SGD的单步学习动态，通过Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理分析ZO-eNTK中每个元素的结构（随机低维子空间上的神经切向向量内积），建立近似保真度与扰动数量而非参数维度的关系。

Result: ZO-eNTK的近似误差取决于模型输出大小而非海量参数维度，证明了ZO方法的维度无关性质，解释了ZO算法在LLM微调中的可扩展性。

Conclusion: ZO方法的扩展性问题可以通过eNTK的维度无关性质得到理论解释，为LLM微调中ZO方法的有效性提供了理论依据，并提出了基于核的框架来理解ZO方法的学习动态。

Abstract: Classical optimization theory establishes that zeroth-order (ZO) algorithms suffer from a dimension-dependent slowdown, with convergence rates typically scaling with the model dimension compared to first-order methods. However, in contrast to these theoretical expectations, a growing body of recent work demonstrates the successful application of ZO methods to fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) with billions of parameters. To explain this paradox, we derive the one-step learning dynamics of ZO SGD, where the empirical Neural Tangent Kernel (eNTK) naturally emerges as the key term governing the learning behavior. Inspection of the eNTK produced by ZO SGD reveals that each element corresponds to the inner product of neural tangent vectors projected onto a random low-dimensional subspace. Thus, by invoking the Johnson-Lindenstrauss Lemma, our analysis shows that the fidelity of the ZO eNTK is governed primarily by the number of perturbations. Crucially, the approximation error depends on the model output size rather than the massive parameter dimension. This dimension-free property provides a theoretical justification for the scalability of ZO methods to LLMs finetuning tasks. We believe that this kernel-based framework offers a novel perspective for understanding ZO methods within the context of learning dynamics.

</details>


### [145] [GRAFT: Auditing Graph Neural Networks via Global Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03377)
*Rishi Raj Sahoo,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAFT是一种事后全局解释框架，通过整合多样化示例选择、积分梯度归因和大语言模型，为GNNs生成类别级特征重要性配置文件和可解释自然语言规则，弥合了定量归因与人类可理解解释之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN在节点分类任务中表现优异，但难以解释，尤其是输入特征如何驱动预测的问题。现有全局解释器仅在结构层面识别子图模式，缺少从输入节点属性层面全局解释模型行为的能力。

Method: 提出GRAFT框架，融合多样性引导的示例选择、基于Integrated Gradients的归因和聚合方法，构建各类别的全局特征影响力视图，并利用大语言模型结合自优化生成简洁自然语言规则。

Result: 有效捕获模型相关特征、支持偏差分析、实现特征高效迁移学习，并引入结构化人工评估协议验证生成规则的准确性和有用性。

Conclusion: GRAFT为分析GNNs特征级行为提供了实用且可解释的方法，定量归因与人类可理解解释之间架起桥梁。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) achieve strong performance on node classification tasks but remain difficult to interpret, particularly with respect to which input features drive their predictions. Existing global GNN explainers operate at the structural level identifying recurring subgraph motifs, but none explain model behaviour globally at the level of input node attributes. We propose GRAFT, a posthoc global explanation framework that identifies class-level feature importance profiles for GNNs. The method combines diversity-guided exemplar selection, Integrated Gradients-based attribution, and aggregation to construct a global view of feature influence for each class, which can be further expressed as concise natural language rules using a large language model with self-refinement. We evaluate GRAFT across multiple datasets, architectures, and experimental settings, demonstrating its effectiveness in capturing model-relevant features, supporting bias analysis, and enabling feature-efficient transfer learning. In addition, we introduce a structured human evaluation protocol to assess the interpretability of generated rules along dimensions such as accuracy and usefulness. Our results suggest that GRAFT provides a practical and interpretable approach for analysing feature-level behaviour in GNNs, bridging quantitative attribution with human-understandable explanations.

</details>


### [146] [Local Truncation Error-Guided Neural ODEs for Large Scale Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03386)
*Xiao Zhang,Yafei Li,Ruixiang Wang,Wei Wei,Shuo He,Mingliang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LTE-ODE解决时空预测中连续演化和离散冲击的双重动力学建模难题。核心创新是将局部截断误差(LTE)从需消除的误差重新定位为监督前向归纳偏置，通过动态注意力掩码实现自适应的模型切换，在保持高精度连续演化的同时敏感捕获异常冲击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理时空预测任务时面临连续-冲击两难困境：神经ODE的Lipschitz连续约束导致面对突变异常时严重过度平滑；物理信息方法通过惩罚数值积分误差强制流形平滑，但作者数学上证明这会引发梯度冲突和"注意力崩溃"，丧失对异常的敏感性。

Method: LTE-ODE 将局部截断误差(LTE)映射为动态空间注意力掩码，架构在稳定区域保留高精度连续ODE演化，而在冲击点处自适应触发离散补偿分支。模型完全端到端训练，不依赖流形惩罚。

Result: 在多个大规模基准测试中达到最先进性能，对高度非线性波动展现出卓越鲁棒性，且具有高部署灵活性，可适应不同硬件内存约束。

Conclusion: LTE-ODE 通过创新性地将LTE重新定位为前向归纳偏置，在连续演化和离散冲击之间实现了优雅的平衡。该方法无需流形惩罚即可端到端训练，在多个大规模基准测试中达到了最先进性能，展现出对高度非线性波动的卓越鲁棒性。此外，在积分步数上的消融实验表明该模型具有高部署灵活性，能够适应不同硬件内存约束。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal forecasting in physical systems, such as large-scale traffic networks, requires modeling a dual dynamic: continuous macroscopic rhythms and discrete, unpredictable microscopic shocks. While Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) excel at capturing smooth evolution, their inherent Lipschitz continuity constraints inevitably cause severe over-smoothing when confronting abrupt anomalies. Recent physics-informed methods attempt to bypass this by penalizing numerical integration errors to enforce manifold smoothness. However, we mathematically reveal that such rigid regularization inherently triggers gradient conflicts and ``attention collapse,'' stripping the model of its sensitivity to anomalies. To resolve this continuity-shock dilemma, we propose Local Truncation Error-Guided Neural ODEs (LTE-ODE). Rather than treating numerical error as a nuisance to be eliminated, we innovatively repurpose the Local Truncation Error (LTE) as an unsupervised forward inductive bias. By mapping the LTE into a dynamic spatial attention mask, our architecture gracefully preserves high-precision continuous ODE evolution in stable regions, while adaptively triggering a discrete compensation branch exclusively at shock points. Trained purely end-to-end without manifold penalties, LTE-ODE achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple large-scale benchmarks, exhibiting exceptional robustness against highly non-linear fluctuations. Furthermore, our ablation on integration steps demonstrates high deployment flexibility, allowing the model to seamlessly adapt to varying hardware memory constraints in real-world applications.

</details>


### [147] [PODiff: Latent Diffusion in Proper Orthogonal Decomposition Space for Scientific Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03399)
*Onkar Jadhav,Tim French,Matthew Rayson,Nicole L. Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PODiff是一种在POD系数空间中进行扩散的条件生成框架，用于高维空间场的概率超分辨率重建，相比像素空间扩散具有更低的计算成本和内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维空间场的概率超分辨率任务中，直接在像素空间进行扩散模型计算成本高昂，难以实际应用。

Method: 提出PODiff框架，在方差排序的POD（主成分分析）系数空间中进行条件扩散生成，利用POD模式的正交性建立可解释的方差排序潜在几何结构，实现高效的条件生成和不确定性量化。

Result: PODiff在保持与像素空间扩散相当的重构精度的同时，显著降低了内存占用，并产生了更可靠的不确定性估计。在西澳大利亚海岸海表温度降尺度和受控平流扩散基准上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过在结构化POD系数空间中进行扩散，PODiff实现了计算效率与生成质量的良好平衡，为高维空间场超分辨率提供了一种可解释、计算高效的生成式方法。

Abstract: Probabilistic super-resolution of high-dimensional spatial fields using diffusion models is often computationally prohibitive due to the cost of operating directly in pixel space. We propose PODiff, a structured conditional generative framework that performs diffusion in a fixed, variance-ordered Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) coefficient space, exploiting the orthogonality of POD modes to impose an interpretable, variance-ordered latent geometry. This design enables efficient ensemble generation, preserves dominant spatial structure, and yields spatially interpretable, well-calibrated uncertainty at substantially lower computational cost. We evaluate PODiff on sea surface temperature downscaling over the West Australian coast and on a controlled advection-diffusion benchmark. PODiff achieves reconstruction accuracy comparable to pixel-space diffusion while requiring significantly less memory and producing more reliable uncertainty estimates than deterministic and Monte Carlo Dropout baselines.

</details>


### [148] [Discovering Reinforcement Learning Interfaces with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03408)
*Akshat Singh Jaswal,Ashish Baghel,Paras Chopra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LIMEN是一个基于大语言模型(LLM)引导的进化框架,能从原始模拟器状态自动合成RL任务接口(包括观测映射和奖励函数),通过策略训练反馈迭代优化候选接口。实验表明,在仅需轨迹级成功指标的情况下,该方法能在离散网格世界和连续控制领域(运动与操作)中发现有效接口,且联合优化观测和奖励的效果优于单独优化任一组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习系统依赖环境接口(观测映射和奖励函数),但为新任务构建这些接口需要大量人工工作。现有方法虽用LLM自动设计奖励,但假设观测固定,且未解决完整任务接口合成的挑战。

Method: 提出LIMEN框架:1)LLM引导的进化框架,将候选接口表示为可执行程序;2)利用策略训练反馈迭代优化接口;3)对观测映射和奖励函数进行联合进化优化。

Result: 在离散网格世界任务和连续控制领域(运动、操纵)中进行评估。实验表明,联合进化观测和奖励能在仅给定轨迹级成功指标的情况下发现有效接口;单独优化任一组件在至少一个领域上失败。

Conclusion: 从原始状态自动构建RL接口可显著减少人工工程;观测和奖励组件通常需要协同设计,因为单一组件优化在评估套件中至少一个领域上会严重失败。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning systems rely on environment interfaces that specify observations and reward functions, yet constructing these interfaces for new tasks often requires substantial manual effort. While recent work has automated reward design using large language models (LLMs), these approaches assume fixed observations and do not address the broader challenge of synthesizing complete task interfaces. We study RL task interface discovery from raw simulator state, where both observation mappings and reward functions must be generated. We propose LIMEN (Code available at https://github.com/Lossfunk/LIMEN), a LLM guided evolutionary framework that produces candidate interfaces as executable programs and iteratively refines them using policy training feedback. Across novel discrete gridworld tasks and continuous control domains spanning locomotion and manipulation, joint evolution of observations and rewards discovers effective interfaces given only a trajectory-level success metric, while optimizing either component alone fails on at least one domain. These results demonstrate that automatic construction of RL interfaces from raw state can substantially reduce manual engineering and that observation and reward components often benefit from co-design, as single-component optimization fails catastrophically on at least one domain in our evaluation suite.

</details>


### [149] [FIBER: A Differentially Private Optimizer with Filter-Aware Innovation Bias Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03425)
*Duc Dm,Thao Do,Minh Son Hoang,Anh Le Duc Tran,Daeyoung Kim,Huy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FiBeR是一种专为时序滤波私有梯度设计的差分隐私优化器，通过创新空间去噪、几何解耦和滤波器感知校准解决了滤波与DP噪声统计错配问题，在多种任务上实现SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP训练中，向梯度注入的噪声与自适应优化器存在复杂交互；虽有时序滤波方法可降低方差，但滤波会改变AdamW二阶矩累积器观测到的DP噪声统计特性，导致基于未滤波DP噪声推导的偏置校正（如减去sigma_w²）出现错配。

Method: FiBeR采用三项核心设计：(1)在创新空间中进行去噪，通过过滤残差流并积分形成滤波梯度估计；(2)解除双点观测几何与创新增益的耦合，实现独立调参；(3)引入滤波器感知的二阶矩校准，从sigma_w²中减去衰减的DP噪声贡献A(ω)sigma_w²，其中A(ω)对创新滤波器以闭式形式推导，对一般稳定线性滤波器也可计算。

Result: 在图像和语言基准测试中，FiBeR持续显著提升DP优化器性能，在多个任务的等隐私约束下超越了当前最优结果。

Conclusion: FiBeR通过在创新空间进行去噪、解除双点观测几何与创新增益的耦合、引入滤波器感知的二阶矩校准等创新设计，在图像和语言基准上实现了显著的SOTA性能提升。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) training protects individual examples by adding noise to gradients, but the injected noise interacts nontrivially with adaptive optimizers. Recent DP methods temporally filter privatized gradients to reduce variance; however, filtering also changes the DP noise statistics seen by AdamW's second-moment accumulator. As a result, bias corrections derived for unfiltered DP noise, such as subtracting sigma_w squared, can become miscalibrated when filtering is present.
  We propose FiBeR, a DP optimizer designed for temporally filtered privatized gradients. FiBeR (i) performs denoising in innovation space by filtering the residual stream and integrating it to form the filtered gradient estimate, (ii) decouples the two-point observation geometry from the innovation gain to enable independent tuning, and (iii) introduces a filter-aware second-moment calibration that subtracts the attenuated DP noise contribution A(omega) sigma_w squared, where A(omega) is derived in closed form for the innovation filter and can be computed for general stable linear filters.
  Across vision and language benchmarks, FiBeR consistently demonstrates substantial improvements in the performance of DP optimizers, surpassing state-of-the-art results under equivalent privacy constraints on multiple tasks.

</details>


### [150] [DynaTab: Dynamic Feature Ordering as Neural Rewiring for High-Dimensional Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03430)
*Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib,Gianfranco Doretto,Donald A. Adjeroh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DynaTab通过动态特征排序机制和神经重连算法处理高维表格数据，结合位置嵌入、重要性门控和掩码注意力层，在36个真实数据集上相比45个SOTA方法取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维表格数据缺乏天然的特征顺序，限制了排列敏感型深度学习模型的应用；现有方法无法自适应判断是否需要特征重排序。

Method: 1) 设计轻量级准则量化数据集内在复杂度，预测特征排序是否有益；2) 通过神经重连算法动态重排序特征；3) 采用位置嵌入+重要性门控+掩码注意力的组合架构；4) 端到端训练结合DFO损失和分散损失。

Result: 在36个真实数据集上与45个SOTA基线相比取得统计学显著提升，尤其在高维数据集上优势明显。

Conclusion: DynaTab为高维表格深度学习提供了新的范式，动态特征排序机制有效解决了表格数据无自然顺序的核心问题。

Abstract: High-dimensional tabular data lacks a natural feature order, limiting the applicability of permutation-sensitive deep learning models. We propose DynaTab, a dynamic feature ordering-enabled architecture inspired by neural rewiring. We introduce a lightweight criterion that predicts when feature permutation will benefit a dataset by quantifying its intrinsic complexity. DynaTab dynamically reorders features via a neural rewiring algorithm and processes them through a compact, dynamic order-aware combination of separate learned positional embedding, importance-based gating, and masked attention layers, compatible with any sequence-sensitive backbone. Trained end-to-end with bespoke dynamic feature ordering (DFO) and dispersion losses, DynaTab achieves statistically significant gains, particularly on high-dimensional datasets, where it is benchmarked against 45 state-of-the-art baselines across 36 different real-world tabular datasets. Our results position DynaTab as a compelling new paradigm for high-dimensional tabular deep learning.

</details>


### [151] [Quantum Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning via Variational Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03434)
*Yu-Ting Lee,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Fu-Chieh Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将变分量子电路引入选项-评论家架构，开发了一种混合分层强化学习智能体。量子特征提取器可在减少66%可训练参数的同时超越经典基线性能，但量子选项值估计存在架构瓶颈导致性能下降。研究建立了参数高效混合分层智能体的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习是极具挑战性的学习范式，效率和效能的提升至关重要。分层强化学习通过时间抽象结构化决策过程。参数化量子计算在非分层强化学习中已展现优势，但这些优势能否迁移到分层决策场景仍是关键开放问题。

Method: 基于选项-评论家架构开发混合分层智能体，用变分量子电路替换经典组件，包括：特征提取器、选项值函数、终止函数和选项内策略。采用标准基准环境进行评估。

Result: 使用量子特征提取器的混合智能体可超越经典基线，同时节省最多66%的可训练参数。但发现架构瓶颈：量子选项值估计会严重降级性能。消融研究揭示了量子电路架构选择对性能的影响。

Conclusion: 研究建立了参数高效混合分层智能体的设计原则，为量子增强分层决策系统提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is one of the most challenging learning paradigms where efficacy and efficiency gains are extremely valuable. Hierarchical reinforcement learning is a variant that leverages temporal abstraction to structure decision-making. While parametrized quantum computations have shown success in non-hierarchical reinforcement learning, whether these advantages adapt to hierarchical decision-making remains a critical open question. In this work, we develop a hybrid hierarchical agent based on the option-critic architecture. This hybrid agent substitutes classical components with variational quantum circuits for feature extractors, option-value functions, termination functions, and intra-option policies. Evaluated on standard benchmarking environments, results show that a hybrid agent utilizing a quantum feature extractor can outperform classical baselines while saving up to 66\% trainable parameters. We also identify an architectural bottleneck that quantum option-value estimation severely degrades performance. Further ablation studies reveal how architectural choices of the quantum circuits affect performance. Our work establishes design principles for parameter-efficient hybrid hierarchical agents.

</details>


### [152] [Learning Generalizable Action Representations via Pre-training AEMG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03462)
*Zhenghao Huang,Huilin Yao,Kaikai Wang,Lin Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个大规模自监督表征学习框架AEMG，用于肌电图(EMG)信号处理。该框架创新性地将神经肌肉动态过程语言化，通过神经肌肉收缩分词器(NCT)将肌肉收缩转化为结构化词汇，将时序激活模式转化为连贯语句，并构建了迄今最大的跨设备EMG信号词汇库。实验表明，AEMG在零样本留一受试者交叉验证中优于6个先进基线模型5.79-9.25%，在仅使用5%目标用户数据时即可达到90%以上的少样本适应性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMG plays a fundamental role in decoding human motor intent and enabling intuitive human-computer interaction, but its generalization capability across subjects, devices, and tasks remains substantially limited by data heterogeneity, label scarcity, and the lack of a unified representational framework.

Method: AEMG reconceptualizes neuromuscular dynamics linguistically, utilizing a novel Neuromuscular Contraction Tokenizer (NCT) to translate discrete muscle contractions into structural words and temporal activation patterns into coherent sentences. Additionally, the largest cross-device EMG signal vocabulary to date is compiled to enable seamless transfer across arbitrary channel topologies and sampling rates.

Result: Experiments demonstrate that AEMG improves the zero-shot leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) accuracy by 5.79-9.25% compared to six state-of-the-art baselines, and achieves more than 90% few-shot adaptation performance with only 5% of target user data.

Conclusion: AEMG proposes the concept of EMG signals as a cross-device physiological language, learns their grammar from massive amounts of data, and lays the groundwork for a single-training, universally applicable EMG foundation model.

Abstract: A fundamental role in decoding human motor intent and enabling intuitive human-computer interaction is played by electromyography (EMG). However, its generalization capability across subjects, devices, and tasks remains substantially limited by data heterogeneity, label scarcity, and the lack of a unified representational framework. To bridge this gap, we propose Any Electromyography (AEMG), the first large-scale, self-supervised representation learning framework for EMG. AEMG reconceptualizes neuromuscular dynamics linguistically, utilizing a novel Neuromuscular Contraction Tokenizer (NCT) to translate discrete muscle contractions into structural words and temporal activation patterns into coherent sentences. Furthermore, we compile the largest cross-device EMG signal vocabulary to date, enabling seamless transfer across arbitrary channel topologies and sampling rates. Experiments demonstrate that AEMG improves the zero-shot leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) accuracy by 5.79-9.25% compared to six state-of-the-art baselines, and achieves more than 90% few-shot adaptation performance with only 5% of target user data. Our work has proposed the concept of EMG signals as a cross-device physiological language, learned their grammar from massive amounts of data, and laid the groundwork for a single-training, universally applicable EMG foundation model.

</details>


### [153] [Bandits on graphs and structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03493)
*Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The goal of this thesis is to investigate the structural properties of certain sequential problems in order to bring the solutions closer to a practical use. In the first part, we put a special emphasis on structures that can be represented as graphs on actions. In the second part, we study the large action spaces that can be of exponential size in the number of base actions or even infinite. For graph bandits, we consider the settings of smoothness of rewards (spectral bandits), side observations, and influence maximization. For large structured domains, we cover kernel bandits, polymatroid bandits, bandits for function optimization (including unknown smoothness), and infinitely many-arms bandits. The thesis aspires to be a survey of the author's contributions on graph and structured bandits.

</details>


### [154] [Adaptive graph-based algorithms for conditional anomaly detection and semi-supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03495)
*Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种图学习方法：一是针对大规模数据的快速近似在线半监督学习算法，通过局部代表点压缩和正则化技术提升效率和稳定性；二是用于临床异常检测的条件异常检测框架，采用图连通性分析和软调和解解决边缘点问题，并经专家验证可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当数据丰富或以流式到达时，传统图方法面临计算和存储瓶颈；同时，条件异常检测相比标准无条件异常框架面临新的挑战（边缘点和孤立点问题），需要新方法识别医院中异常临床行为以发现潜在错误。

Method: 提出基于数据相似性图的半监督标签传播方法，开发快速近似在线算法求解近似图上的调和解；通过将邻近点压缩为一组局部代表点以最小化畸变，并引入正则化提升稳定性；针对条件异常检测，设计基于图连通性分析和软调和解的非参数图方法解决边缘点和孤立点问题。

Result: 实验和理论分析表明该方法可取得良好效果；非参数图方法有效解决了边缘点和孤立点问题；经15位重症监护专家进行人体评估验证，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过将邻近点聚类为局部代表点并结合正则化技术，可以有效解决大规模数据下的图方法计算问题；在临床异常检测中，基于图连通性分析和软调和解的条件异常检测方法能够有效识别异常临床行为，并经专家验证具有实用价值。

Abstract: We develop graph-based methods for semi-supervised learning based on label propagation on a data similarity graph. When data is abundant or arrive in a stream, the problems of computation and data storage arise for any graph-based method. We propose a fast approximate online algorithm that solves for the harmonic solution on an approximate graph. We show, both empirically and theoretically, that good behavior can be achieved by collapsing nearby points into a set of local representative points that minimize distortion. Moreover, we regularize the harmonic solution to achieve better stability properties. We also present graph-based methods for detecting conditional anomalies and apply them to the identification of unusual clinical actions in hospitals. Our hypothesis is that patient-management actions that are unusual with respect to the past patients may be due to errors and that it is worthwhile to raise an alert if such a condition is encountered. Conditional anomaly detection extends standard unconditional anomaly framework but also faces new problems known as fringe and isolated points. We devise novel nonparametric graph-based methods to tackle these problems. Our methods rely on graph connectivity analysis and soft harmonic solution. Finally, we conduct an extensive human evaluation study of our conditional anomaly methods by 15 experts in critical care.

</details>


### [155] [Bandits attack function optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03496)
*Philippe Preux,Rémi Munos,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将函数优化建模为预算约束下的序列决策问题，提出SOO（同步乐观优化）算法。该算法受连续多臂老虎机问题启发，通过确定性域划分策略平衡探索与利用，在保证解质量的前提下具有较高的数值效率，并在CEC'2014基准测试集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数优化问题面临预算约束，即允许的评估次数有限，需在此约束下做出序列决策，核心挑战在于平衡探索（全面搜索定义域）与利用（局部精细搜索）之间的关系。

Method: 该算法通过域划分（domain partitioning）策略进行确定性搜索，同时处理探索与利用的权衡：初期进行准均匀搜索（exploration），后期在潜在全局最优解附近进行局部优化（exploitation）。

Result: 实验表明SOO算法在CEC'2014单目标实参数数值优化竞赛测试套件上具有良好的优化性能。

Conclusion: SOO通过确定性域划分方法，在保证解质量的同时实现了数值高效性，并在CEC'2014基准测试集上得到了经验验证。

Abstract: We consider function optimization as a sequential decision making problem under budget constraint. This constraint limits the number of objective function evaluations allowed during the optimization. We consider an algorithm inspired by a continuous version of a multi-armed bandit problem which attacks this optimization problem by solving the tradeoff between exploration (initial quasi-uniform search of the domain) and exploitation (local optimization around the potentially global maxima). We introduce the so-called Simultaneous Optimistic Optimization (SOO), a deterministic algorithm that works by domain partitioning. The benefit of such approach are the guarantees on the returned solution and the numerical efficiency of the algorithm. We present this machine learning approach to optimization, and provide the empirical assessment of SOO on the CEC'2014 competition on single objective real-parameter numerical optimization test-suite.

</details>


### [156] [GRIFDIR: Graph Resolution-Invariant FEM Diffusion Models in Function Spaces over Irregular Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03497)
*James Rowbottom,Elizabeth L. Baker,Nick Huang,Ben Adcock,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Alexander Denker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于有限元函数的图卷积核架构，用于无限维函数空间的扩散模型，能够自然处理非结构化网格和复杂几何，在非凸和多连通域上实现分辨率不变的高保真采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于傅里叶神经算子的函数空间扩散模型存在固有缺陷：倾向于正则网格，对复杂域拓扑的泛化能力不足，限制了其在不规则离散化场景下的应用。

Method: 将广义图卷积核表示为有限元函数，设计一种新颖的架构来处理函数值数据。该方法天然支持非结构化网格和复杂几何形状。

Result: 所提方法在多种几何形状（包括非凸和多连通域）上的无条件及条件采样实验中表现出色，保持了分辨率不变性，并在非平凡几何上实现了高保真度。

Conclusion: 该架构有效结合了扩散模型的生成能力与有限元方法的灵活性，为复杂几何上函数值数据的建模提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models in infinite-dimensional function spaces provide a mathematically principled framework for modelling function-valued data, offering key advantages such as resolution invariance and the ability to handle irregular discretisations. However, practical implementations have struggled to fully realise these benefits. Existing backbones like Fourier neural operators are often biased towards regular grids and fail to generalise to complex domain topologies. We propose a novel architecture for function-space diffusion models that represents generalised graph convolutional kernels as finite element functions, enabling the model to naturally handle unstructured meshes and complex geometries. We demonstrate the efficacy of our network architecture through a series of unconditional and conditional sampling experiments across diverse geometries, including non-convex and multiply-connected domains. Our results show that the proposed method maintains resolution invariance and achieves high fidelity in capturing functional distributions on non-trivial geometries.

</details>


### [157] [A Hierarchical Sampling Framework for bounding the Generalization Error of Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03499)
*Dario Filatrella,Ragnar Thobaben,Mikael Skoglund*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了层次联邦学习(HFL)的期望泛化界，采用Wasserstein距离度量，通过多层树结构建模客户数据集的依赖关系，通过超样本构造和Lipschitz假设推导出泛化界，并证明该界可恢复CMI界和差分隐私下的泛化界，在高斯位置模型上验证了渐近收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习(FL)中数据分布在各客户端，现有泛化界难以刻画客户端间的层次依赖关系，条件互信息(CMI)方法难以处理无界损失函数，需要更一般的泛化分析框架。

Method: 1) 采用多层树结构建模HFL中的层次采样过程和数据依赖；2) 利用Wasserstein距离和超样本构造度量算法对单节点变化的敏感性；3) 在Lipschitz损失假设下推导泛化界。

Result: 1) 所提界在有界损失情况下可退化为现有CMI界；2) 可与差分隐私结合恢复基于隐私的泛化界；3) 在高斯位置模型(GLM)上恢复了泛化误差的实际渐近速率。

Conclusion: Wasserstein距离为HFL泛化分析提供了更一般的框架，适用范围更广且可导出已有方法，在GLM上的渐近匹配表明界具有紧性。

Abstract: We study expected generalization bounds for the Hierarchical Federated Learning (HFL) setup using Wasserstein distance. We introduce a generalized framework in which data is sampled hierarchically, and we model it with a multi-layered tree structure that induces dependencies among the clients' datasets. We derive generalization bounds in terms of Wasserstein distance under the Lipschitz assumption on the loss function, by applying a supersample construction that allows us to measure the sensitivity of the algorithm to the change of a single node in the sampling tree. By leveraging the FL structure, we recover and strictly imply existing state-of-the-art conditional mutual information (CMI) bounds in the case of bounded losses. We also show that our bound can be applied together with Differential Privacy assumptions, to recover generalization bounds based on algorithmic privacy. To assess the tightness of our bounds, we study the Gaussian Location Model (GLM) and show that we recover the actual asymptotic rate of the generalization error.

</details>


### [158] [Meta-Inverse Physics-Informed Neural Networks for High-Dimensional Ordinary Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03511)
*Zhao Wei,Kenneth Hor Cheng Koh,Sheng Yuan Chin,James Chun Yip Chan,Chin Chun Ooi,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MI-PINN，通过两阶段元学习框架解决高维耦合ODE系统中的逆建模问题，显著降低参数搜索维度提高采样效率，并引入自适应聚类多分支学习处理多尺度动力学。该方法在全body PBPK模型（含33个ODE）上验证了其在稀疏临床观测下恢复动力学参数和重建未知机理的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维耦合ODE系统的逆问题在科学机器学习中普遍存在，面临参数未知、部分物理特征和稀疏观测等挑战。现有PINNs依赖任务特定的联合优化，存在优化困难和泛化能力差的问题。

Method: 提出MI-PINN框架，将逆建模问题重构为两阶段元学习问题：第一阶段跨任务学习物理感知表示，第二阶段固定表示优化任务特定未知量。同时引入自适应聚类的多分支学习方案处理多尺度动力学问题。

Result: 在含33个耦合ODE的全body PBPK模型上，MI-PINN在仅有限临床观测条件下成功恢复了被掩蔽的代谢动力学参数，并重建了缺失的机理项。

Conclusion: MI-PINN通过元学习框架和两阶段优化策略，有效解决了高维耦合ODE系统中的逆建模问题，在PBPK模型上验证了其准确恢复掩蔽动力学参数和重建缺失机理项的能力。

Abstract: Solving inverse problems in dynamical systems governed by high-dimensional coupled ordinary differential equations (ODEs) is a ubiquitous challenge in scientific machine learning. In many real-world applications, researchers seek to uncover unknown parameters or model unknown dynamics even as the underlying physics is only partially characterized, and observations are sparse and limited to specific measurable channels. While physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) are ideal for inverse inference under partial observability, existing PINNs typically rely on task-specific joint optimization, which suffers from optimization difficulties and poor generalization. In this paper, we propose a meta-inverse physics-informed neural network (MI-PINN) that reformulates inverse modeling as a two-stage meta-learning problem. MI-PINN first learns a physics-aware representation across multiple tasks, and then performs inverse modeling by optimizing task-specific unknowns while keeping the learned representation fixed. This two-stage formulation significantly reduces the parameter search dimension, thereby improving sample efficiency and enabling accurate inference. To handle multi-scale dynamics common in these high-dimensional ODE systems, we further introduce an adaptive clustering-based multi-branch learning scheme. We demonstrate the effectiveness of MI-PINN on whole-body physiologically based pharmacokinetic (PBPK) models with up to 33 coupled ODEs, using paracetamol and theophylline under intravenous and oral dosing scenarios. Experimental results show that MI-PINN enables accurate recovery of masked kinetic parameters and reconstruction of missing mechanistic terms despite limited clinical observations.

</details>


### [159] [Understanding Self-Supervised Learning via Latent Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03517)
*Fabian A Mikulasch,Friedemann Zenke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在分布匹配（LDM）作为自监督学习的统一框架，通过同时最大化表示的对数概率（对齐）和潜在熵（均匀性）来学习表示。该框架成功统一了ICA、对比学习、非对比学习和预测性SSL等多种方法，并能推导出基于卡尔曼滤波的时序预测模型，证明了方法的理论可辨识性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法缺乏统一理论框架，难以解释不同方法间的内在联系，也无法为新方法设计提供指导。

Method: 将SSL形式化为潜在分布匹配：学习在假设潜在模型下最大化对数概率（对齐），同时最大化潜在熵以防止崩溃（均匀性）。基于LDM，推导了非线性、无采样的贝叶斯滤波模型及基于卡尔曼的高维时序预测器。

Result: 该框架统一了独立成分分析、对比学习、非对比学习和预测性SSL方法（包括stop gradient方法），并证明了预测性LDM在温和假设下具有可辨识性。

Conclusion: LDM框架为SSL提供了统一的理论视角，阐明了现有方法的隐含假设，并为设计新方法提供了原则性指导；同时证明了预测性LDM在温和假设下可获得可识别的潜在表示。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) excels at finding general-purpose latent representations from complex data, yet lacks a unifying theoretical framework that explains the diverse existing methods and guides the design of new ones. We cast SSL as latent distribution matching (LDM): learning representations that maximize their log-probability under an assumed latent model (alignment), while maximizing latent entropy to prevent collapse (uniformity). This view unifies independent component analysis with contrastive, non-contrastive, and predictive SSL methods, including stop gradient approaches. Leveraging LDM, we derive a nonlinear, sampling-free Bayesian filtering model with a Kalman-based predictor for high-dimensional timeseries. We further prove that predictive LDM yields identifiable latent representations under mild assumptions, even with nonlinear predictors. Overall, LDM clarifies the assumptions behind established SSL methods and provides principled guidance for developing new approaches.

</details>


### [160] [Disentangling Shared and Task-Specific Representations from Multi-Modal Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03570)
*He Lyu,Huolin Zeng,Junren Wang,Huazhen Yang,Linchao He,Yong Chen,Zhirui Li,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer,Huan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态临床数据的多结局预测问题，提出OrthTD框架，通过Transformer融合多模态信息并利用正交任务分解隔离共享与任务特异性表征，有效提升预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在平衡共享表征学习与任务特异性建模方面存在不足：硬参数共享引发负迁移，灵活共享方法仍混杂共享与任务特异性信号

Method: 提出OrthTD框架：基于Transformer实现多模态融合，引入正交任务分解将患者表征分离为共享子空间与任务特异性子空间，并施加几何正交性约束以减少冗余

Result: 在12,430名外科患者4项结局预测任务中，平均AUC达87.5%，AUPRC达37.2%，一致优于先进表格数据和多任务学习方法，尤其在稀有事件识别上表现突出

Conclusion: 强制分离非冗余的共享与任务特异性表征可有效提升多模态临床数据的多结局预测性能

Abstract: Real-world clinical data is inherently multimodal, providing complementary evidence that mirrors the practical necessity of jointly assessing multiple related outcomes. Although multi-task learning can improve efficiency by sharing information across outcomes, existing approaches often fail to balance shared representation learning with outcome-specific modeling. Hard parameter sharing can trigger negative transfer when task gradients conflict, while flexible sharing may still entangle shared and task-specific signals. To address this, we propose a multi-task framework built on a unified Transformer for multimodal fusion, augmented with Orthogonal Task Decomposition (OrthTD) to split patient representations into shared and task-specific subspaces and impose a geometric orthogonality constraint to reduce redundancy and isolate task-specific signals. We evaluated OrthTD on a real-world cohort of 12,430 surgical patients for predicting four outcomes. OrthTD achieved average AUC (area under the receiver operating characteristic curve) of 87.5% and average AUPRC (area under the precision-recall curve) of 37.2%, consistently outperformed advanced tabular and multi-task methods. Notably, OrthTD achieves substantial gains in AUPRC, indicating superior performance in identifying rare events within imbalanced clinical data. These results suggest that enforcing non-redundant shared and task-specific representations can improve multi-outcome prediction from multimodal clinical data.

</details>


### [161] [Flow Matching on Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03588)
*Francesco Ruscelli,Ferdinando Zanchetta,Rita Fioresi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在黎曼对称空间上训练流匹配模型的一般性框架，通过利用等距群的代数结构将问题线性化，简化了测地线计算，并在实Grassmannians上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型通常针对欧几里得空间设计，将其推广到黎曼流形面临挑战。黎曼对称空间（包括球面、双曲空间和Grassmannians）是一类重要的流形，需要专门的方法来处理。

Method: 利用黎曼对称空间的代数结构，将对称空间上的流匹配问题重新表述为等距群李代数子空间上的流匹配问题，从而实现问题线性化，并简化了测地线的处理。

Result: 成功实现了黎曼对称空间上的流匹配训练，在实Grassmannians（SO(n)/SO(k)×SO(n-k)）上进行了验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为黎曼流形上的生成建模提供了一般性方法，通过利用等距群的代数结构，成功将流匹配推广到了实Grassmannians等对称空间。

Abstract: We introduce a general framework for training flow matching models on Riemannian symmetric spaces, a large class of manifolds that includes the sphere, hyperbolic space and Grassmannians. We exploit their algebraic structure to reformulate flow matching on symmetric spaces as flow matching on a subspace of the Lie algebra of their isometry group, thus linearizing the problem and greatly simplifying the handling of geodesics. As an application, we showcase our framework on the real Grassmannians $\operatorname{SO}(n) / \operatorname{SO}(k) \times \operatorname{SO}(n-k)$.

</details>


### [162] [A Few-Step Generative Model on Cumulative Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03623)
*Zhiqi Li,Duowen Chen,Yuchen Sun,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于累积流图的统一少步生成建模框架，通过累积传输和累积参数化将局部瞬时更新与有限时间传输相连接。该框架可广泛应用于现有的扩散模型和流模型，能够在保持生成质量的前提下实现少步甚至单步生成，无需增加模型容量，仅需对时间嵌入和训练目标进行最小修改。在图像生成、几何分布建模、联合预测和SDF生成等任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型和流模型在概率空间的长程传输计算上存在效率问题，缺乏一种统一的方法来连接局部更新与全局状态转换

Method: 核心是累积流图抽象，将局部瞬时更新与有限时间传输联系起来；基于累积传输和累积参数化构建统一的少步生成框架

Result: 在图像生成、几何分布建模、联合预测和SDF生成等任务上验证了方法有效性，同时降低了推理成本

Conclusion: 提出一种基于累积流图的统一少步生成框架，可应用于扩散模型和流模型，无需增加模型容量，仅需对时间嵌入和训练目标进行最小修改，即可在保持生成质量的同时实现少步甚至单步生成。

Abstract: We propose a unified, few-step generative modeling framework based on \emph{cumulative flow maps} for long-range transport in probability space, inspired by flow-map techniques for physical transport and dynamics. At its core is a cumulative-flow abstraction that connects local, instantaneous updates with finite-time transport, enabling generative models to reason about global state transitions. This perspective yields a unified few-step framework built on cumulative transport and \revise{cumulative} parameterization that applies broadly to existing diffusion- and flow-based models without being tied to a specific prediction \revise{instantiation}. Our formulation supports few-step and even one-step generation while preserving synthesis quality, requiring only minimal changes to time embeddings and training objectives, and no increase in model capacity. We demonstrate its effectiveness across diverse tasks, including image generation, geometric distribution modeling, joint prediction, and SDF generation, with reduced inference cost.

</details>


### [163] [Uni-OPD: Unifying On-Policy Distillation with a Dual-Perspective Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03677)
*Wenjin Hou,Shangpin Peng,Weinong Wang,Zheng Ruan,Yue Zhang,Zhenglin Zhou,Mingqi Gao,Yifei Chen,Kaiqi Wang,Hongming Yang,Chengquan Zhang,Zhuotao Tian,Han Hu,Yi Yang,Fei Wu,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Uni-OPD，一个统一的双视角同策略蒸馏框架。针对同策略蒸馏中信息状态探索不足和教师监督不可靠两个瓶颈，从学生视角采用数据平衡策略促进探索，从教师视角提出结果引导的边界校准机制恢复正确与错误轨迹间的顺序一致性。在16个基准测试上的实验证明了该方法在大小语言模型和多模态大小语言模型中的有效性和通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation (OPD) has shown empirical success as a post-training paradigm for consolidating specialized expert models into a single student model, but the conditions under which OPD yields reliable improvement remain poorly understood. Identifying the fundamental bottlenecks is crucial for advancing the technology.

Method: Uni-OPD proposes a dual-perspective optimization strategy: (1) From the student's perspective, two data balancing strategies are adopted to promote exploration of informative student-generated states; (2) From the teacher's perspective, an outcome-guided margin calibration mechanism is developed to restore order consistency between correct and incorrect trajectories by ensuring aggregated token-level guidance aligns with outcome rewards.

Result: Experiments across 5 domains and 16 benchmarks covering diverse settings—including single-teacher/multi-teacher distillation, strong-to-weak distillation, and cross-modal distillation—verify the effectiveness and versatility of Uni-OPD.

Conclusion: Uni-OPD provides a unified OPD framework that generalizes across LLMs and MLLMs, with extensive experiments on 5 domains and 16 benchmarks demonstrating its effectiveness and versatility. The work offers practical insights for reliable on-policy distillation, addressing both exploration (student-side) and supervision quality (teacher-side) challenges.

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as an effective post-training paradigm for consolidating the capabilities of specialized expert models into a single student model. Despite its empirical success, the conditions under which OPD yields reliable improvement remain poorly understood. In this work, we identify two fundamental bottlenecks that limit effective OPD: insufficient exploration of informative states and unreliable teacher supervision for student rollouts. Building on this insight, we propose Uni-OPD, a unified OPD framework that generalizes across Large Language Models (LLMs) and Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), centered on a dual-perspective optimization strategy. Specifically, from the student's perspective, we adopt two data balancing strategies to promote exploration of informative student-generated states during training. From the teacher's perspective, we show that reliable supervision hinges on whether aggregated token-level guidance remains order-consistent with the outcome reward. To this end, we develop an outcome-guided margin calibration mechanism to restore order consistency between correct and incorrect trajectories. We conduct extensive experiments on 5 domains and 16 benchmarks covering diverse settings, including single-teacher and multi-teacher distillation across LLMs and MLLMs, strong-to-weak distillation, and cross-modal distillation. Our results verify the effectiveness and versatility of Uni-OPD and provide practical insights into reliable OPD.

</details>


### [164] [Rethinking the Rank Threshold for LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03724)
*Juneyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将LoRA微调中保证无虚假局部极小值的充分条件从r≥12降低到r=1，仅限于二分类任务。通过用非对称LoRA流形维度替代对称Sard形式计数、引入Polyak-Łojasiewicz不等式分析交叉熵损失、以及Rademacher复杂度界分析，证明了二分类场景下秩一LoRA的充分性，并在GLUE四项二分类任务、三种编码器架构及RoBERTa-large上验证了该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究建立了LoRA微调中避免虚假局部极小值的充分条件r(r+1)/2 > KN，要求在少样本RoBERTa设置下r≥12。但该条件对特定场景的尖锐性以及对于实际使用的交叉熵损失的有效性尚不明确，需要进一步的理论分析和实证验证。

Method: 本文提出三个理论改进：(1) 用非对称LoRA流形维度替代对称Sard形式计数，得到更弱的容量要求r(m+n)-r² > C*·KN (C*≈1.35)；(2) 在交叉熵设置下利用Polyak-Łojasiewicz不等式消除秩阈值要求；(3) 通过Rademacher复杂度界预测秩一方差最优性，精确对应偏置项饱和的条件（仅适用于二分类）。

Result: 在二分类设置下，理论条件在r=1时即可满足；经验结果表明，在四个GLUE二分类任务、三种编码器架构及规模化RoBERTa-large上，秩一LoRA与r=12性能相当；多分类MNLI任务中，最优秩确实高于一，符合理论预测。

Conclusion: 本文将二分类场景下的LoRA秩要求从r≥12降至r=1，证明了低秩适应在此场景下的充分性和有效性。多分类扩展的条件保证依赖于标准NTK假设，相关工作留待未来研究。

Abstract: A recent landscape analysis of LoRA fine-tuning in the neural tangent kernel regime establishes a sufficient condition $r(r+1)/2 > KN$ on the LoRA rank $r$ for the absence of spurious local minima under squared-error loss, prescribing $r \geq 12$ on canonical few-shot RoBERTa setups. The condition is stated for general output dimension $K$, so its sharpness in any particular regime, and its practical implication for the cross-entropy loss actually used in fine-tuning, are open. We give three results that together reduce the prescribed rank to $r = 1$ for binary classification in this regime. First, replacing the symmetric Sard-form count with the non-symmetric LoRA manifold dimension yields a strictly weaker capacity requirement, $r(m+n) - r^2 > C^* \cdot KN$ with $C^* \approx 1.35$ under Gaussian-iid features, satisfied at $r = 1$ on canonical setups. Second, in the cross-entropy setting the Polyak--Łojasiewicz inequality removes the rank threshold entirely. Third, a Rademacher-complexity bound predicts rank-one variance optimality precisely when the bias term is saturated, which is the case for binary classification but not for $K > 2$. Empirically, across four GLUE-style binary tasks, three encoder architectures, and at scale on RoBERTa-large, rank one is competitive with the existing prescription $r = 12$; on multi-class MNLI the optimal rank shifts above one, also as predicted. The binary-regime guarantees are conditional on standard NTK assumptions; the multi-class extension is left to future work.

</details>


### [165] [GEM-FI: Gated Evidential Mixtures with Fisher Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03750)
*Marco Mustafa Mohammed,Fatemeh Daneshfar,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM是一种改进的证据深度学习方法，通过学习能量信号门控证据输出，实现单次推理下的多模态认知不确定性估计，同时改善校准性能和ID/OOD分离能力。在CIFAR-10上，GEM-FI将准确率从91.11%提升至93.75%，Brier分数降低7.46，误分类检测AUPR达99.94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) 虽然能实现单次推理的不确定性估计，但存在三个主要缺陷：过度自信、校准性差、以及无法有效表示多模态认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）。

Method: 提出GEM模型家族，包含三个变体：1) GEM-CORE：学习特征级能量信号并映射为有界门控，在支持度低时平滑抑制证据；2) GEM-MIX：添加轻量级证据头混合模块和学习路由权重，无需多通道集成即可捕获多模态认知不确定性；3) GEM-FI：引入Fisher信息正则化稳定混合分配，减少头部坍缩，产生更平滑的边界不确定性。所有模型均保持单次推理特性。

Result: 在图像分类和OOD检测基准测试中，GEM显著优于基线方法。CIFAR-10分类：准确率+2.64pp (91.11→93.75)，Brier×100降低7.46 (14.27→6.81)，误分类检测AUPR +0.86 (99.08→99.94)。认知不确定性OOD检测：CIFAR-10→SVHN达AUPR/AUROC 92.59/95.09，CIFAR-10→CIFAR-100达90.20/89.06，均大幅领先DAEDL的85.54/89.30和88.19/86.10。

Conclusion: GEM通过距离感知的能量门控机制和混合证据头设计，成功解决了EDL的过度自信和校准问题，同时在单次推理框架内有效捕获多模态认知不确定性，显著提升了分布内分类精度和分布外检测性能。

Abstract: Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) enables single-pass uncertainty estimation by predicting Dirichlet evidence, but it can remain overconfident and poorly calibrated, and it often fails to represent multi-modal epistemic uncertainty. We introduce Gated Evidential Mixtures (GEM), a family of models that learns an in-model energy signal and uses it to gate evidential outputs end-to-end in a distance-informed manner. GEM-CORE learns a feature-level energy and maps it to a bounded gate that smoothly suppresses evidence when support is low. To capture epistemic multi-modality without multi-pass ensembling, GEM-MIX adds a lightweight mixture of evidential heads with learned routing weights while preserving single-pass inference. Finally, GEM-FI stabilizes mixture allocations via a Fisher-informed regularizer, reducing head collapse and producing smoother boundary uncertainty. Across image classification and OOD detection benchmarks, GEM improves calibration and ID/OOD separation with single-pass inference. On CIFAR-10, GEM-FI vs. DAEDL improves accuracy from 91.11 to 93.75 (+2.64 pp), reduces Brier x100 from 14.27 to 6.81 (-7.46), and also improves misclassification-detection AUPR from 99.08 to 99.94 (+0.86). For epistemic OOD detection, GEM-FI achieves AUPR/AUROC of 92.59/95.09 on CIFAR-10 to SVHN and 90.20/89.06 on CIFAR-10 to CIFAR-100, compared with 85.54/89.30 and 88.19/86.10 for DAEDL.

</details>


### [166] [Vanishing L2 regularization for the softmax Multi Armed Bandit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03752)
*Stefana-Lucia Anita,Gabriel Turinici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究L2正则化softmax策略梯度在多臂老虎机（MAB）算法中的收敛性。通过凸性分析框架证明了当正则化参数趋于零时的理论收敛性，并在标准基准测试中验证了其数值优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用凸性的研究未能为分析L2正则化softmax策略梯度在正则化参数消失时的收敛性提供合适的理论框架。

Method: 通过挖掘凸性框架证明了L2正则化softmax策略梯度的理论收敛性，填补了此前研究的空白。

Result: 实验结果表明，该正则化机制在标准基准测试中具有数值优势。

Conclusion: 本研究为L2正则化softmax策略梯度提供了理论收敛保证，填补了正则化参数消失时收敛性分析的空白，并通过实验验证了该方法在标准基准测试中的数值优势。

Abstract: Multi Armed Bandit (MAB) algorithms are a cornerstone of reinforcement learning and have been studied both theoretically and numerically. One of the most commonly used implementation uses a softmax mapping to prescribe the optimal policy and served as the foundation for downstream algorithms, including REINFORCE. Distinct from vanilla approaches, we consider here the L2 regularized softmax policy gradient where a quadratic term is subtracted from the mean reward. Previous studies exploiting convexity failed to identify a suitable theoretical framework to analyze its convergence when the regularization parameter vanishes. We prove here theoretical convergence results and confirm empirically that this regime makes the L2 regularization numerically advantageous on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [167] [Nora: Normalized Orthogonal Row Alignment for Scalable Matrix Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03769)
*Jinghui Yuan,Jiaxuan Zou,Shuo Wang,Yong Liu,Feiping Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Nora is a novel optimizer for LLM training that unifies three core requirements—efficiency (Muon-like preconditioning), stability (scale-invariance adherence), and speed (O(mn) complexity)—using row-wise momentum projection and block-diagonal Hessian approximation, with a simple 2-line implementation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing optimizers fail to unify efficiency, stability, and speed: Muon achieves efficient preconditioning but has prohibitive computational cost; RMNP maintains speed but allows radial jitters compromising stability. An optimizer satisfying all three requirements is needed.

Method: Nora stabilizes weight norms and angular velocities via row-wise momentum projection onto the orthogonal complement of weights, and leverages block-diagonal dominance of Transformer Hessian for structured preconditioning with O(mn) complexity.

Result: Preliminary experiments validate Nora as an efficient and promising optimizer for large-scale training, with proven scalability through established scaling theorems.

Conclusion: Nora achieves all three desiderata (efficiency, stability, speed) through row-wise momentum projection and block-diagonal Hessian approximation, with a streamlined 2-line implementation and proven scalability via scaling theorems.

Abstract: Matrix-based optimizers have demonstrated immense potential in training Large Language Models (LLMs), however, designing an ideal optimizer remains a formidable challenge. A superior optimizer must satisfy three core desiderata: efficiency, achieving Muon-like preconditioning to accelerate optimization; stability, strictly adhering to the scale-invariance inherent in neural networks; and speed, minimizing computational overhead. While existing methods address these aspects to varying degrees, they often fail to unify them, either incurring prohibitive computational costs like Muon, or allowing radial jitters that compromise stability like RMNP. To bridge this gap, we propose Nora, an optimizer that rigorously satisfies all three requirements. Nora achieves training stability by explicitly stabilizing weight norms and angular velocities through row-wise momentum projection onto the orthogonal complement of the weights. Simultaneously, by leveraging the block-diagonal dominance of the Transformer Hessian, Nora effectively approximates structured preconditioning while maintaining an optimal computational complexity of $\mathcal{O}(mn)$. Furthermore, we prove that Nora is a scalable optimizer and establish its corresponding scaling theorems. With a streamlined implementation requiring only two lines of code, our preliminary experiments validate Nora as an efficient and highly promising optimizer for large-scale training.

</details>


### [168] [Graph Convolutional Support Vector Regression for Robust Spatiotemporal Forecasting of Urban Air Pollution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03795)
*Nourin Jahan,Madhurima Panja,Muhammed Navas T,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图卷积支持向量回归(GCSVR)框架用于城市空气污染时空预测，结合图卷积学习捕捉站点间空间依赖性和SVR建模非线性时间动态，同时降低异常值敏感性。在印度德里(37站)和孟买(18站)的数据集上验证了该方法的优越性和稳健性，并集成了保形预测生成校准预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空气质量预测面临污染物浓度非线性、非平稳、时空依赖及异常观测(交通拥堵、工业排放、季节性气象变化等)等多重挑战，现有方法在同时处理空间依赖和时间动态方面存在不足，且对异常值敏感。

Method: 提出GCSVR框架，核心创新包括：(1)图卷积层捕捉站点间空间依赖关系；(2)支持向量回归建模非线性时间动态并提供异常值鲁棒性；(3)集成保形预测(conformal prediction)生成校准预测区间，实现不确定性感知预测。

Result: GCSVR在德里和孟买两个城市的多预测周期实验中一致提升预测精度，在不同季节和异常污染事件中保持稳定性能。统计检验证实了方法在两个城市的可靠性。

Conclusion: GCSVR框架有效融合图卷积与SVR的优势，实现了鲁棒的城市空气污染时空预测，保形预测的集成增强了其在空气质量监测和公共健康决策中的实用价值。

Abstract: Urban air quality forecasting is challenging because pollutant concentrations are nonlinear, nonstationary, spatiotemporally dependent, and often affected by anomalous observations caused by traffic congestion, industrial emissions, and seasonal meteorological variability. This study proposes a Graph Convolutional Support Vector Regression (GCSVR) framework for robust spatiotemporal forecasting of urban air pollution. The model combines graph convolutional learning to capture inter-station spatial dependence with support vector regression to model nonlinear temporal dynamics while reducing sensitivity to outlier observations. The proposed framework is evaluated using air quality records from 37 monitoring stations in Delhi and 18 stations in Mumbai, representing inland and coastal metropolitan environments in India. Forecasting performance is assessed across multiple horizons and compared with established temporal and spatiotemporal benchmarks. The results show that GCSVR consistently improves predictive accuracy and maintains stable performance across seasons and outlier-prone pollution episodes. Statistical test further confirms the reliability of the proposed approach across the two cities. Finally, conformal prediction is integrated with GCSVR to generate calibrated prediction intervals, enhancing its practical value for uncertainty-aware air quality monitoring and public health decision-making.

</details>


### [169] [Realizable Bayes-Consistency for General Metric Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03823)
*Dan Tsir Cohen,Steve Hanneke,Aryeh Kontorovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在实现设置下，研究了通用度量损失学习的强通用贝叶斯一致性，扩展了经典的0-1分类和实值回归的特征化理论。通过引入无穷非递减(γ_k)-Littlestone树概念，给出了假设类存在风险几乎必然收敛到最优风险的分布无关学习规则的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将通用贝叶斯一致性的经典特征化从0-1分类和实值回归扩展到一般度量损失学习；解决Cohen等人提出的开放问题的实现情形。

Method: 引入无穷非递减(γ_k)-Littlestone树概念（γ_k → ∞），将经典Littlestone树结构推广到度量损失设置。

Result: 给出了假设类存在分布无关学习规则的充分必要条件：当且仅当假设类不包含无穷非递减(γ_k)-Littlestone树。

Conclusion: 完全解决了实现设置下的开放问题，提供了关于假设类可学习性的精确组合特征。

Abstract: We study strong universal Bayes-consistency in the realizable setting for learning with general metric losses, extending classical characterizations beyond $0$-$1$ classification \citep{bousquet_theory_2021, hanneke2021universalbayesconsistencymetric} and real-valued regression \citep{attias_universal_2024}. Given an instance space $(\mathcal X,ρ)$, a label space $(\mathcal Y,\ell)$ with possibly unbounded loss, and a hypothesis class $\mathcal H \subseteq \mathcal Y^{\mathcal X}$, we resolve the realizable case of an open problem presented in \citet{pmlr-v178-cohen22a}. Specifically, we find the necessary and sufficient conditions on the hypothesis class $\mathcal H$ under which there exists a distribution-free learning rule whose risk converges almost surely to the best-in-class risk (which is zero) for every realizable data-generating distribution. Our main contribution is this sharp characterization in terms of a combinatorial obstruction: Similarly to \citet{attias2024optimallearnersrealizableregression}, we introduce the notion of an infinite non-decreasing $(γ_k)$-Littlestone tree, where $γ_k \to \infty$. This extends the Littlestone tree structure used in \citet{bousquet_theory_2021} to the metric loss setting.

</details>


### [170] [A Domain Incremental Continual Learning Benchmark for ICU Time Series Model Transportability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03832)
*Ryan King,Conrad Krueger,Ethan Veselka,Tianbao Yang,Bobak J. Mortazavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了跨地区医疗数据迁移学习基准测试，将模型迁移建模为领域增量学习，评估数据回放和EWC两种方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型医院缺乏训练资源，而大医院模型仅基于单一医院数据，存在跨机构泛化问题；不同地区测量分布存在显著差异

Method: 提出跨地区医疗数据迁移学习基准，将模型迁移建模为领域增量学习问题，评估数据回放和EWC两种方法

Result: 评估了数据回放（存储先前数据源样本进行微调）和EWC（参数正则化方法）两种领域增量学习方法在跨区域迁移中的表现

Conclusion: 该基准测试能够有效评估模型跨区域迁移的能力，为医疗AI模型的普适性提供评估框架。

Abstract: In recent years, machine learning has made significant progress in clinical outcome prediction, demonstrating increasingly accurate results. However, the substantial resources required for hospitals to train these models, such as data collection, labeling, and computational power, limit the feasibility for smaller hospitals to develop their own models. An alternative approach involves transferring a machine learning model trained by a large hospital to smaller hospitals, allowing them to fine-tune the model on their specific patient data.
  However, these models are often trained and validated on data from a single hospital, raising concerns about their generalizability to new data. Our research shows that there are notable differences in measurement distributions and frequencies across various regions in the United States. To address this, we propose a benchmark that tests a machine learning model's ability to transfer from a source domain to different regions across the country. This benchmark assesses a model's capacity to learn meaningful information about each new domain while retaining key features from the original domain.
  Using this benchmark, we frame the transfer of a machine learning model from one region to another as a domain incremental learning problem. While the task of patient outcome prediction remains the same, the input data distribution varies, necessitating a model that can effectively manage these shifts. We evaluate two popular domain incremental learning methods: data replay, which stores examples from previous data sources for fine-tuning on the current source, and Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC), a model parameter regularization method that maintains features important for both data sources.

</details>


### [171] [Complex Equation Learner: Rational Symbolic Regression with Gradient Descent in Complex Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03841)
*Sergei Garmaev,Maurice Gauché,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出复权重扩展的Equation Learner，通过绕过实轴优化退化解决符号回归中除法、对数、平方根等运算符的奇点问题，实现稳定收敛和广泛运算符支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归旨在从数据中发现可解释的方程，但现代基于梯度的方法在处理引入奇点或域约束的运算符（如除法、对数和平方根）时会失效。现有Equation Learner类模型通常回避这些运算符或施加限制（如约束分母防止极点），缩窄了假设空间。

Method: 提出一种Equation Learner的复权重扩展，通过允许优化轨迹绕过实轴退化来缓解实值优化中的病态问题，从而实现对除法、对数和平方根等运算符的无约束使用。

Result: 方法在目标表达式具有实域极点时能稳定收敛，可在符号回归基准测试和实验频率响应数据上有效恢复奇点行为。

Conclusion: 该方法有效扩展了神经符号回归的表达能力，使其能够处理具有奇点特性的真实物理系统建模任务。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to discover interpretable equations from data, yet modern gradient-based methods fail for operators that introduce singularities or domain constraints, including division, logarithms, and square roots. As a result, Equation Learner-type models typically avoid these operators or impose restrictions, e.g. constraining denominators to prevent poles, which narrows the hypothesis class. We propose a complex weight extension of the Equation Learner that mitigates real-valued optimization pathologies by allowing optimization trajectories to bypass real-axis degeneracies. The proposed approach converges stably even when the target expression has real-domain poles, and it enables unconstrained use of operations such as logarithm and square root. We Validate the method on symbolic regression benchmarks and show it can recover singular behavior from experimental frequency response data.

</details>


### [172] [Memory-Efficient Continual Learning with CLIP Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03866)
*Ryan King,Gang Li,Bobak Mortazavi,Tianbao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对CLIP模型在增量学习中因记忆缓冲区小而导致的性能退化问题，提出一种动态类别损失重加权方法，在有限内存条件下有效平衡新任务适应与旧任务保留，实现快速且内存高效的任务适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP模型虽然在理解图像-文本关系方面表现出色，但在适应新数据时容易遗忘先前知识。传统方法需要同时使用新任务数据和旧任务记忆缓冲区，但当记忆缓冲区较小时，CLIP的对比损失会受到影响，导致先前任务的性能下降。

Method: 提出一种内存高效、分布鲁棒的方法，在训练过程中对每个类别动态重加权损失。该方法通过平衡新旧类别的梯度分布来缓解小内存缓冲区的对比损失问题。

Result: 在类别增量设置（CIFAR-100、ImageNet1K）和领域增量设置（DomainNet）上验证了该方法的有效性。该方法能够快速适应CLIP模型，同时最小化灾难性遗忘，即使在极少内存使用的情况下也能保持良好性能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种在有限内存条件下有效减少CLIP模型灾难性遗忘的方法，通过动态类别损失重加权策略，在保持模型适应性的同时保护先前任务的知识。

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models excel at understanding image-text relationships but struggle with adapting to new data without forgetting prior knowledge. To address this, models are typically fine-tuned using both new task data and a memory buffer of past tasks. However, CLIP's contrastive loss suffers when the memory buffer is small, leading to performance degradation on previous tasks. We propose a memory-efficient, distributionally robust method that dynamically reweights losses per class during training. Our approach, tested on class incremental settings (CIFAR-100, ImageNet1K) and a domain incremental setting (DomainNet) adapts CLIP models quickly while minimizing catastrophic forgetting, even with minimal memory usage.

</details>


### [173] [On Adaptivity in Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03869)
*Hassan Dbouk,Nidham Gazagnadou,Matthias Reisser,Christos Louizos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We investigate the effectiveness of adaptive zeroth-order (ZO) optimization for memory-constrained fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Contrary to prior claims, we show that adaptive ZO methods such as ZO-Adam offer no convergence advantage over well-tuned ZO-SGD, while incurring significant memory overhead. Our analysis reveals that in high dimensions, ZO gradients lack coordinate-wise heterogeneity, rendering adaptive mechanisms memory inefficient. Leveraging this insight, we propose MEAZO, a memory-efficient adaptive ZO optimizer that tracks only a single scalar for global step size adaptation. We support our method with theoretical convergence guarantees under standard assumptions. Experiments across multiple LLM families and tasks demonstrate that MEAZO matches ZO-Adam's performance with the memory footprint of ZO-SGD. Additional experiments on synthetic quadratic problems and LLM fine-tuning further demonstrate MEAZO's enhanced robustness to step size choices, particularly in grouped or block-structured optimization settings.

</details>


### [174] [Optimal Posterior Sampling for Policy Identification in Tabular Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03921)
*Cyrille Kone,Kevin Jamieson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限视野分段MDP中的(ε, δ)-PAC策略识别问题，提出一种结合后验采样与在线学习的随机化算法，在保持计算效率(O(S²AH)每回合)的同时，实现了样本复杂度的渐近最优性，并克服了现有方法对log(1/δ)的次优多项式依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PAC策略识别方法存在两个核心缺陷：(1) 计算成本高，难以实现；(2) 对log(1/δ)的依赖为次优的多项式形式，导致在渐进 regime 下保证无意义。需要一种既高效又具有理论保证的新方法。

Method: 提出一种随机化算法，核心创新在于：(1) 结合后验采样(Prior Sampling)引导探索；(2) 引入在线学习算法作为探索的"指南"。算法复杂度为O(S²AH)每回合，与标准基于模型的方法相当。

Result: 该方法实现了：(1) 样本复杂度的渐近最优性；(2) 后验收缩率的最优性；(3) 计算复杂度与标准模型方法相当O(S²AH)；(4) 克服了对log(1/δ)的次优多项式依赖。

Conclusion: 该工作为表格型MDP中的高效策略识别提供了理论与实践工具，弥合了计算效率与理论保证之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: We study the $(\varepsilon, δ)$-PAC policy identification problem in finite-horizon episodic Markov Decision Processes. Existing approaches provide finite-time guarantees for approximate settings ($\varepsilon>0$) but suffer from high computational cost, rendering them hard to implement, and also suffer from suboptimal dependence on $\log(1/δ)$. We propose a randomized and computationally efficient algorithm for best policy identification that combines posterior sampling with an online learning algorithm to guide exploration in the MDP. Our method achieves asymptotic optimality in sample complexity, also in terms of posterior contraction rate, and runs in $O(S^2AH)$ per episode, matching standard model-based approaches. Unlike prior algorithms such as MOCA and PEDEL, our guarantees remain meaningful in the asymptotic regime and avoid sub-optimal polynomial dependence on $\log(1/δ)$. Our results provide both theoretical insights and practical tools for efficient policy identification in tabular MDPs.

</details>


### [175] [TabSurv: Adapting Modern Tabular Neural Networks to Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03944)
*Stanislav Kirpichenko,Andrei Konstantinov,Lev Utkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Survival analysis on tabular data is a well-studied problem. However, existing deep learning methods are often highly task-specific, which can limit the transfer of new approaches from other domains and introduce constraints that may affect performance. We propose TabSurv, an approach that adapts modern tabular architectures to survival analysis using either the Weibull distribution or non-parametric survival prediction. TabSurv optimizes SurvHL, a novel histogram loss function supporting censored data. In addition to a baseline feed-forward network, we implement deep ensembles of MLPs for survival analysis within TabSurv. In contrast to prior work, the ensemble components are trained in parallel, optimizing survival distribution parameters before averaging, which promotes diversity across ensemble component predictions. We perform a comprehensive empirical evaluation of different proposed architectures on 10 diverse real-world survival datasets. Our results show that TabSurv consistently outperforms on average established classical and deep learning baselines, such as RSF, DeepSurv, DeepHit, SurvTRACE. Notably, deep ensembles with Weibull parametrization instead of non-parametric models achieve the highest average rank by C-index. Overall, our study clarifies how modern tabular neural networks can be adapted and trained to tackle survival analysis problems, offering a strong and reliable approach. The TabSurv implementation is publicly available.

</details>


### [176] [Integrating Feature Correlation in Differential Privacy with Applications in DP-ERM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03945)
*Tianyu Wang,Luhao Zhang,Rachel Cummings*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Standard differential privacy imposes uniform privacy constraints across all features, overlooking the inherent distinction between sensitive and insensitive features in practice. In this paper, we introduce a relaxed definition of differential privacy that accounts for such heterogeneity, allowing certain features to be treated as insensitive even when correlated with sensitive ones. We propose a correlation-aware framework, $\textsf{CorrDP}$, which relaxes privacy for insensitive features while accounting for their correlations with sensitive features, with the correlations quantified using total variation distance. We design algorithms for differentially private empirical risk minimization (DP-ERM) under the $\textsf{CorrDP}$ framework, incorporating distance-dependent noise into gradients for improved theoretical utility guarantees. When the correlation distance is unknown, we estimate it from the dataset and show that it achieves a comparable privacy-utility guarantee. We perform experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets and show that $\textsf{CorrDP}$-based DP-ERM algorithms consistently outperform the standard DP framework in the presence of insensitive features.

</details>


### [177] [Pretrained Model Representations as Acquisition Signals for Active Learning of MLIPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03964)
*Eszter Varga-Umbrich,Shikha Surana,Paul Duckworth,Jules Tilly,Olivier Peltre,Zachary Weller-Davies*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用预训练MACE模型的潜在空间信息进行主动学习采集，直接从预训练模型提取NTK核和激活核作为采集信号，无需辅助不确定性头、贝叶斯训练或委员会集成，在反应化学基准测试中达到相同性能目标所需数据量减少38%（能量）和28%（力）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应化学MLIP训练受限于量子化学标签的高成本和过渡态配置的稀缺性。现有主动学习方法通常需要复杂的辅助组件（不确定性头、贝叶斯训练、委员会集成），本文探索预训练MLIP的潜在空间是否已包含有效采集所需信息，以简化流程。

Method: 从预训练MACE势能中直接导出两种采集信号：有限宽度神经切线核（NTK）和基于隐藏层潜在空间特征的激活核。提出基于相似性的采集策略，利用预训练模型诱导的相似性空间保留化学有意义结构。

Result: 两种核方法一致优于固定描述符基线、委员会分歧和随机采集，达到性能目标所需数据平均减少38%（能量误差）和28%（力误差）。预训练模型诱导的相似性空间保留了化学有意义的结构，提供了比随机初始化或固定描述符核更可靠的残差不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 预训练将潜在空间几何与模型误差对齐，产生实用且充分的采集信号用于反应MLIP微调，无需额外的不确定性建模组件。这简化了主动学习流程，使其更易于应用于反应化学系统。

Abstract: Training machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) for reactive chemistry is often bottlenecked by the high cost of quantum chemical labels and the scarcity of transition state configurations in candidate pools. Active learning (AL) can mitigate these costs, but its effectiveness hinges on the acquisition rule. We investigate whether the latent space of a pretrained MLIP already contains the information necessary for effective acquisition, eliminating the need for auxiliary uncertainty heads, Bayesian training and fine-tuning, or committee ensembles. We introduce two acquisition signals derived directly from a pretrained MACE potential: a finite-width neural tangent kernel (NTK) and an activation kernel built from hidden latent space features. On reactive-chemistry benchmarks, both kernels consistently outperform fixed-descriptor baselines, committee disagreement, and random acquisition, reducing the data required to reach performance targets by an average of 38% for energy error and 28% for force error. We further show that the pretrained model induces similarity spaces that preserve chemically meaningful structure and provide more reliable residual uncertainty estimates than randomly initialised or fixed-descriptor-based kernels. Our results suggest that pretraining aligns latent-space geometry with model error, yielding a practical and sufficient acquisition signal for reactive MLIP fine-tuning.

</details>


### [178] [Flow Sampling: Learning to Sample from Unnormalized Densities via Denoising Conditional Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03984)
*Aaron Havens,Brian Karrer,Neta Shaul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow Sampling框架，通过条件于噪声样本的扩散模型和流匹配方法，从已知能量函数定义的未归一化密度中进行高效采样，并自然推广到黎曼流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非归一化密度采样是生成建模的重要变体，但评估能量函数代价高昂，需要学习高效的采样器。

Method: 提出Flow Sampling框架，条件于噪声样本而非数据样本，回归去噪扩散漂移项；利用插值过程减少能量函数评估次数；推导出常曲率黎曼流形上的闭合漂移公式。

Result: 在合成基准、小分子肽、大规模分子构象生成和球面分布等任务上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Flow Sampling提供了一种高效、可扩展的未归一化密度采样方法，具备流形泛化能力。

Abstract: Sampling from unnormalized densities is analogous to the generative modeling problem, but the target distribution is defined by a known energy function instead of data samples. Because evaluating the energy function is often costly, a primary challenge is to learn an efficient sampler. We introduce Flow Sampling, a framework built on diffusion models and flow matching for the data-free setting. Our training objective is conditioned on a noise sample and regresses onto a denoising diffusion drift constructed from the energy function. In contrast, diffusion models' objective is conditioned on a data sample and regresses onto a noising diffusion drift. We utilize the interpolant process to minimize the number of energy function evaluations during training, resulting in an efficient and scalable method for sampling unnormalized densities. Furthermore, our formulation naturally extends to Riemannian manifolds, enabling diffusion-based sampling in geometries beyond Euclidean space. We derive a closed-form formula for the conditional drift on constant curvature manifolds, including hyperspheres and hyperbolic spaces. We evaluate Flow Sampling on synthetic energy benchmarks, small peptides, large-scale amortized molecular conformer generation, and distributions supported on the sphere, demonstrating strong empirical performance.

</details>


### [179] [A Closed-Form Adaptive-Landmark Kernel for Certified Point-Cloud and Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04046)
*Sushovan Majhi,Atish Mitra,Žiga Virk,Pramita Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PALACE是一种基于持久同调的自适应分类引擎，通过覆盖理论和Lebesgue数准则提供四个封闭形式理论保证（ distortion bound、最优权重、分类率、预测证书），在多个数据集上表现最优或具有竞争力，尤其在领域膨胀场景下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久同调分类方法PLACE使用固定参数网格，缺乏数据自适应性。当数据分布集中时，均匀网格存在效率低下问题。该研究旨在通过自适应调整预算(budget)、半径(radii)、带宽(bandwidth)三个超参数（各≤5个选择），构建数据自适应的分类引擎。

Method: 方法核心基于覆盖理论：利用landmark覆盖上的Lebesgue数准则推导出四个封闭形式保证。(i) 结构下界λ(τ;ν)控制失真；(ii) 通过训练标签推导最优权重w_k=K^(-1/2)和最远点采样策略；(iii) 核-RKHS分类率及Mahalanobis margin作为排名器；(iv) 非渐近(Pinelis)和渐近(Gaussian)形式的预测证书，无需校准分割。

Result: 理论保证：分类率O((k-1)√K/(γ√m_min))，二元必要性阈值m=Ω(√K/γ)。实证表现：Orbit5k达91.3%±1.0%（匹配Persformer）；COX2和MUTAG领先所有图方法；DHFR在1pp内接近ECP。8倍领域膨胀时自适应方法保持94%准确率，均匀网格降至25%。

Conclusion: PALACE是首个封闭形式图分类方法在多个基准数据集上达到最优或竞争水平，证明了数据自适应机制在持久同调分类中的有效性。Mahalanobis margin(mean Spearman ρ≈+0.60)和预测证书机制为实际应用提供了可解释性和可靠性保证。

Abstract: We introduce PALACE (Persistence Adaptive-Landmark Analytic Classification Engine), the data-adaptive companion to PLACE, paying a small cross-validation tier on three knobs (budget, radii, bandwidth; $\leq 5$ choices each). A cover-theoretic core (Lebesgue-number criterion on the landmark cover) yields four closed-form guarantees. (i) A structural lower distortion bound $λ(τ;ν)$ on $\mathcal{D}_n$ under cross-diagram non-interference, with a $(D/L)^2$ budget reduction over the uniform grid when diagrams concentrate. (ii) Equal weights $w_k = K^{-1/2}$ maximizing $λ$, and farthest-point-sampling positions $2$-approximating the optimal $k$-center covering radius; both derived from training labels alone, no gradient training. (iii) A kernel-RKHS classification rate $O((k-1)\sqrt{K}/(γ\sqrt{m_{\min}}))$ with binary necessity threshold $m = Ω(\sqrt K/γ)$ from a matching Le Cam lower bound, and a closed-form filtration-selection rule. The kernel-Mahalanobis margin $\hatρ_{\mathrm{Mah}}$ is the strongest closed-form ranker across the chemical-graph pool (mean Spearman $ρ\approx +0.60$); the isotropic surrogate $\hatγ/\sqrt{K}$ admits a selection-consistency rate, and $\widehatλ$ from (i) provides an independent data-level signal (positive on COX2 and PTC). (iv) A per-prediction certificate, in non-asymptotic Pinelis and asymptotic Gaussian forms, with no calibration split. Empirically, PALACE is the strongest closed-form diagram-based method on Orbit5k ($91.3 \pm 1.0\%$, matching Persformer), leads every diagram-based competitor on COX2 and MUTAG, and is competitive on DHFR (within 1 pp of ECP). At $8\times$ domain inflation, adaptive placement maintains $94\%$ while the uniform grid collapses to chance ($25\%$ on 4-class data).

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [Enwar 3.0: An Agentic Multi-Modal LLM Orchestrator for Situation-Aware Beamforming, Blockage Prediction, and Handover Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03215)
*Ahmad M. Nazar,Abdulkadir Celik,Asmaa Abdallah,Mohamed Y. Selim,Daji Qiao,Ahmed M. Eltawil*

Main category: cs.MA
