TL;DR: Enwar 3.0 is an environment-aware reasoning framework for vehicular mmWave networks that uses multi-modal sensing and agentic LLMs to perform predictive beamforming, blockage detection, and handover management. The system detects sensor impairments in real-time and coordinates specialized agents for beam selection and environment perception.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maintaining robust mmWave connectivity in vehicular networks faces challenges from environmental dynamics, sensor degradation, and link variability, requiring real-time adaptation capabilities.

Method: The framework integrates: (1) a synthetic degradation pipeline for training sensor health classifiers; (2) a primed LLM with chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting and human-in-the-loop feedback; (3) multi-agent orchestration for beam selection, blockage forecasting, and environment perception; (4) dynamic sensor-specific model loading based on environmental context. Evaluated across 15 sensor combinations.

Result: Sensor degradation classifier achieves >99% accuracy; beam selection accuracy exceeds 88%; blockage F1-scores surpass 98%; reasoning correctness reaches 87% on complex decision prompts.

Conclusion: Enwar 3.0 establishes a scalable foundation for LLM-integrated wireless systems capable of real-time reasoning, perception, and adaptation in vehicular environments.

Abstract: Maintaining robust millimeter-wave (mmWave) connectivity in vehicular networks requires real-time adaptation to environmental dynamics, sensor degradation, and link variability. This paper presents Enwar 3.0, an environment-aware reasoning framework that unifies multi-modal sensing, agentic large language models (LLMs), and context-driven model selection for predictive beamforming, blockage detection, and handover management. Building upon prior iterations of Enwar, the proposed architecture integrates a classifier-driven assessment of sensor health with a primed LLM that orchestrates multiple specialized agents through structured, task-aware prompting. A novel synthetic degradation pipeline enables the training of a sensor degradation classifier that detects real-time impairments across camera, radar, LiDAR, and GPS inputs, achieving over 99% accuracy. The LLM, trained via chain-of-thought (CoT) priming and human-in-the-loop feedback, coordinates agent calls for beam selection, blockage forecasting, and environment perception while dynamically loading sensor-specific models based on environmental context. Extensive evaluations across 15 sensor combinations demonstrate that Enwar 3.0 delivers state-of-the-art performance in both predictive accuracy and interpretability, with beam selection accuracy exceeding 88%, blockage F1-scores surpassing 98%, and reasoning correctness reaching 87% on complex decision prompts. This work establishes a scalable foundation for LLM-integrated wireless systems that reason, perceive, and adapt in real-time.

</details>


### [181] [Coordination as an Architectural Layer for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03310)
*Maksym Nechepurenko,Pavel Shuvalov*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文将多智能体LLM系统的协调层抽象为可独立配置的架构元素，通过固定LLM、工具和提示模板仅改变协调配置的对照实验，验证Murphy分解可识别配置签名。实验覆盖100个Polymarket市场，3/5预测成立，2种配置主导Pareto前沿，证明协调层值得作为独立研究对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在生产中失败率达41%-87%，主因是协调缺陷而非基础模型能力；现有方法仅做失败模式枚举或提供声明式编排框架，缺乏从协调配置到可预测失败模式签名的原则性映射。

Method: 信息控制实验设计：固定LLM、工具、输出上限和提示模板，仅改变协调配置（5种），以每题总计算量为内生架构输出；采用Brier分数的Murphy分解分离校准与判别力；100个Polymarket二元市场测试+Bootstrap功效投影；Foresight Arena实时部署验证。

Result: 3/5预指定预测方向成立；2种配置在Pareto前沿占优（成本-质量权衡）；Bootstrap区间可分离共识对齐与其它配置，但Bonferroni校正后配对检验不显著（n=100）；Pareto前沿存在且可识别。

Conclusion: 协调应作为可配置的架构层独立处理；Murphy分解可分离校准与判别能力；五种协调配置留下可区分特征；3/5预测得到验证，2种配置在Pareto前沿占优；作为方法论验证的首次实例化，非跨模型通用结论。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems fail in production at rates between 41% and 87%, mostly due to coordination defects rather than base-model capability. Existing responses split between cataloguing failure modes empirically and shipping declarative orchestration frameworks as engineering tools; neither delivers a principled mapping from coordination configuration to predictable failure-mode signature. We argue that coordination should be treated as a configurable architectural layer, separable from agent logic and from information access, enabling architectural reasoning rather than only engineering productivity.
  We instantiate this with an information-controlled design on prediction markets: a single LLM, fixed tools, fixed per-call output cap, and fixed prompt template across five reference coordination configurations, with total compute per question treated as an endogenous architectural output. The Murphy decomposition of the Brier score separates calibration from discriminative power, so configurations leave distinguishable signatures even when aggregate scores coincide.
  On 100 Polymarket binary markets resolved after the model's training cutoff (claude-opus-4-6) we report Murphy signatures, a cost-quality Pareto frontier, category-conditioned analysis, and a bootstrap power-projection. Three of five pre-specified predictions are upheld in direction; two configurations dominate the Pareto frontier within this regime; exploratory bootstrap intervals separate consensus alignment from others, though pairwise tests do not survive Bonferroni correction at n=100. We also deploy the same configurations as live agents on Foresight Arena under web-search-enabled conditions, as an on-chain replication channel accumulating in parallel. Harness, trace dataset, and production agents are released. We position this as a methodology-validating first instantiation, not a general cross-model claim.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [ARIS: Autonomous Research via Adversarial Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03042)
*Ruofeng Yang,Yongcan Li,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ARIS是一个开源自主研究框架，通过跨模型对抗协作解决长时研究中的隐式证据失败问题，具有执行层（65+技能、MCP集成、研究wiki）、编排层（五工作流）和保证层（三阶段声明检查、五轮编辑、证明检查）的三层架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统的性能不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于其周围的框架（harness）。对于长时研究工作流，主要失败模式不是可见的崩溃，而是看似合理但实际缺乏证据支持的成功：运行时间较长的智能体会产生声称，其证据支持可能不完整、报告错误或被无声地继承自执行者的表述。

Method: ARIS采用跨模型对抗协作作为默认配置：执行模型驱动研究进展，来自不同模型家族的审查模型批判中间产物并请求修订。三层架构包括：(1)执行层：65+可复用Markdown定义技能、MCP模型集成、持久化研究wiki、确定性图表生成；(2)编排层：五个端到端工作流，支持可调努力程度和可配置审查模型路由；(3)保证层：实验声明支持检查的三阶段流程（完整性验证、结果-声明映射、声明审计）、五轮科学编辑流程、数学证明检查、渲染PDF视觉检查。

Result: 报告展示了ARIS的架构设计、保证机制和早期部署经验。原型自改进循环记录研究痕迹并提出框架改进建议，这些建议仅在审查模型批准后才被采纳。

Conclusion: ARIS是一个用于自主研究的开源研究框架，通过三层架构（执行层、编排层、保证层）解决LLM智能体在长时研究工作流中的隐式失败问题，特别是证据支持不完整或被错误继承的问题。

Abstract: This report describes ARIS (Auto-Research-in-sleep), an open-source research harness for autonomous research, including its architecture, assurance mechanisms, and early deployment experience. The performance of agent systems built on LLMs depends on both the model weights and the harness around them, which governs what information to store, retrieve, and present to the model. For long-horizon research workflows, the central failure mode is not a visible breakdown but a plausible unsupported success: a long-running agent can produce claims whose evidential support is incomplete, misreported, or silently inherited from the executor's framing. Therefore, we present ARIS as a research harness that coordinates machine-learning research workflows through cross-model adversarial collaboration as a default configuration: an executor model drives forward progress while a reviewer from a different model family is recommended to critique intermediate artifacts and request revisions. ARIS has three architectural layers. The execution layer provides more than 65 reusable Markdown-defined skills, model integrations via MCP, a persistent research wiki for iterative reuse of prior findings, and deterministic figure generation. The orchestration layer coordinates five end-to-end workflows with adjustable effort settings and configurable routing to reviewer models. The assurance layer includes a three-stage process for checking whether experimental claims are supported by evidence: integrity verification, result-to-claim mapping, and claim auditing that cross-checks manuscript statements against the claim ledger and raw evidence, as well as a five-pass scientific-editing pipeline, mathematical-proof checks, and visual inspection of the rendered PDF. A prototype self-improvement loop records research traces and proposes harness improvements that are adopted only after reviewer approval.

</details>


### [183] [ARISE: A Repository-level Graph Representation and Toolset for Agentic Fault Localization and Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03117)
*Shahd Seddik,Fatemeh Fard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ARISE通过多粒度程序图（含语句级定义-使用边）和数据流切片API，增强LLM智能体在仓库级故障定位和自动修复中的语义精度。实验表明该方法显著提升定位和修复性能，且改进来源于数据流图而非工具模式设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的系统提供仓库的结构表示（文件、类、函数及其关系），但未建模过程内变量值的流动方式，导致智能体缺乏函数级和行级定位所需的语义精度。

Method: 提出多粒度程序图，将结构关系扩展到语句级节点，通过过程内定义-使用边连接；通过三层工具API暴露该图，将数据流切片作为一等原生、可查询的智能体原语，允许模型在单次调用中追踪定义或消费感兴趣变量的语句。

Result: 在SWE-bench Lite（300个真实GitHub问题，11个Python仓库）上使用Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct作为骨干模型。相比未修改的SWE-agent基线，ARISE将Function Recall@1提升17.0分，Line Recall@1提升15.0分。这些定位改进直接转化为修复成功。

Conclusion: ARISE在SWE-bench Lite上达到22.0% Pass@1（66/300），比SWE-agent提升4.7个百分点。消融实验证实改进来自数据流图而非工具模式，大语言模型可直接消费结构化切片输出而无需自然语言摘要层。图构建器和切片API被设计为框架无关的即插即用工具集，可用于未来APR研究。

Abstract: Repository-level fault localization (FL) and automated program repair (APR) require an agent to identify the relevant code units across files, follow call and data dependencies, and generate a valid patch. Existing graph-based systems provide structural representations of repositories (files, classes, functions and their relationships) but do not model how variable values flow within procedures, leaving agents without the semantic precision needed for function- and line-level localization. We present ARISE (Agentic Repository-level Issue Solving Engine), which augments an LLM-based agent with a multi-granularity program graph that extends structural relationships down to statement-level nodes connected by intra-procedural definition-use edges. ARISE exposes this graph through a three-tier tool API, which brings data-flow slicing as a first-class, queryable agent primitive that allows the model to trace, in a single call, which statements define or consume a variable of interest. We evaluate on SWE-bench Lite (300 real GitHub issues, 11 Python repositories) using Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct as the backbone. Compared to the unmodified SWE-agent baseline, ARISE improves Function Recall@1 by 17.0 points and Line Recall@1 by 15.0 points. These localization gains translate directly into repair success, with ARISE achieving 22.0% Pass@1 (66/300), a 4.7 percentage-point improvement over SWE-agent. Controlled ablations confirm that the improvement is driven by the data-flow graph rather than the tool schema, and that large code models consume structured slice output directly without requiring a natural-language summarization layer. The graph builder and slicing API are designed as a framework-agnostic, drop-in toolset for future APR research.

</details>


### [184] [TeamUp: Semantic Project Matching and Team Formation for Learning at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03237)
*Dhruv Gulwani,Basem Suleiman,Aditya Joshi,Sonit Singh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TeamUp是一个基于嵌入的学生团队组建系统，通过预训练语言模型的语义嵌入匹配学生与项目，结合余弦相似度和教学约束生成个性化推荐，并通过嵌入方差建模技能互补性构建认知多样化团队，在250名学生×60项目的实验中显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional team-forming methods fail to create cognitively diverse teams at scale, causing equity issues where high-performing students monopolize visible projects while underrepresented students have reduced opportunities.

Method: Uses semantic embeddings from pretrained language models combined with a hybrid ranking algorithm incorporating cosine similarity and pedagogical constraints (difficulty alignment, domain preferences, demand balancing). Skill complementarity is modeled via embedding variance for team diversity.

Result: (1) Match quality: 0.74 vs 0.43 mean cosine similarity; (2) Difficulty alignment: 83% vs 34% within one level; (3) Team diversity: 82% vs 41% covering 3+ technical areas; (4) Scalability: sub-second latency at <$0.10/student.

Conclusion: TeamUp significantly outperforms traditional methods: 0.74 vs 0.43 cosine similarity, 83% vs 34% difficulty alignment, 82% vs 41% team diversity, with sub-second latency at <$0.10/student cost.

Abstract: Project-based learning improves student engagement and learning outcomes, yet allocating students to appropriately challenging projects while forming cognitively diverse teams remains difficult at scale. Traditional allocation methods (manual spreadsheets, preference surveys) can't construct the cognitively diverse teams that that collaborate cognitively. This mismatch perpetuates equity issues: high-performing students self-select visible projects while under-represented students face reduced access to opportunity. We propose TeamUp, a lightweight, embedding-based team-forming system designed to improve learning outcomes and equity in large-scale project-based courses. TeamUp uses semantic embeddings from pretrained language models to match students to projects aligned with their skill level. The system employs a hybrid ranking algorithm combining cosine similarity with pedagogical constraints (difficulty alignment, domain preferences, and demand balancing) to generate personalised and transparent recommendations. Beyond individual matching, TeamUp constructs cognitively diverse teams by modelling skill complementarity through embedding variance, ensuring teams possess well-distributed capabilities rather than homogeneous strengths. We evaluated TeamUp through a virtual experiment using 250 student profiles and 60 project descriptions. Results show: (1) substantially higher match quality (mean cosine similarity of 0.74 vs. 0.43); (2) better difficulty alignment (83% placed within one level vs. 34%); (3) more diverse teams (82% covering three or more technical areas vs. 41%); and (4) sub-second recommendation latency at operational costs under $0.10 per student.

</details>


### [185] [Exploring Sustainability in Scientific Software through Code Quality & Test Coverage Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03243)
*Sheikh Md. Mushfiqur Rahman,Gregory R. Watson,Nasir U. Eisty*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Context: Scientific open-source software (SciOSS) plays a foundational role in research and engineering, yet its long-term sustainability has often been overlooked and remains a significant concern. Objective: This study investigates the long-term sustainability of SciOSS through code and test quality metrics. Method: We analyze CASS Software Portfolio projects, classifying them by sustainability and comparing their code structure, test coverage, and links between code quality and testing across the dataset. Results: Sustainable projects show higher, more consistent test coverage and clearer code-test correlations, while unsustainable ones show weaker patterns. Overall, test coverage is low in scientific software, and high complexity and coupling reduce testability. Conclusion: In this study, we present a practical, data-driven approach for assessing sustainability in scientific software, offering a foundation for evaluating long-term software health and supporting future efforts in quality assurance and sustainability monitoring.

</details>


### [186] [Operationalizing Software Engineering Theories for Practical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03257)
*Isaque Alves,Fabio Kon,Jessica Diaz,Carla Rocha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统化的操作化程序，用于软件工程理论建设中概念的可测量化转换，通过扩展既有框架并结合DevOps团队分类学理论案例，为研究者提供了从抽象理论到实证检验的清晰路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程作为一门应用科学，需要将抽象概念转化为可测量的元素进行实证验证，但目前缺乏系统性的操作化程序来弥合抽象概念与实证验证之间的差距。

Method: 扩展Sjøberg等人提出的操作化框架，遵循Dubin的方法制定非因果假设，定义变量、选择指标、系统推导假设，并使用DevOps团队分类学理论进行说明。

Result: 提出了一个可复制的、循证的方法论指南，该指南保留了清晰的证据链并支持实践验证。

Conclusion: 该指南提供了从理论到可测试元素的透明证据链，使研究者能够将理论进展扎根于经验证据，并为实践者提供可操作的见解。

Abstract: Software Engineering often adapts theory-building frameworks from the social sciences to address socio-technical complexity. The key phases of the theory-building process are conceptual development, operationalization, testing, and application. Operationalization translates abstract concepts into measurable elements for empirical validation. This phase is essential for delivering the practical utility required by an applied science like Software Engineering. We propose a systematic procedure for the operationalization phase that bridges the gap between abstract concepts and empirical validation, ensuring the resulting theory is both rigorous and practically useful. We extend the operationalization framework proposed by Sjøberg et al. and formulate non-causal hypotheses following Dubin's approach. Our procedure defines variables, selects indicators, and systematically derives hypotheses. We present a replicable, evidence-based methodological guideline that preserves a clear chain of evidence and supports practical validation. We illustrate the procedure using the DevOps Team Taxonomies Theory. This guideline provides a transparent chain of evidence from theory to testable elements, empowering researchers to ground theoretical advancements in empirical evidence and deliver actionable insights for practitioners.

</details>


### [187] [POSTCONDBENCH: Benchmarking Correctness and Completeness in Formal Postcondition Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03356)
*Gehao Zhang,Juan Zhai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: POSTCONDBENCH 是一个多语言后置条件生成基准，从 121 个真实开源项目中提取 420 个 Python/Java 任务，通过可执行环境和缺陷区分机制评估后置条件的完整性与正确性，发现当前 SOTA LLM 在两者之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化后置条件对程序调试、测试和验证至关重要，但手动编写需要大量专业知识；已有基于 LLM 自动生成后置条件的研究，但现有基准评估存在局限性——主要依赖表面形式匹配或人工评估，且数据集规模小、来源单一。

Method: 构建了包含 420 个 Python 和 Java 任务的基准数据集，来自 121 个真实开源项目，每个任务配有高质量的专家标注真实后置条件集；提出基于缺陷区分的可执行评估方法，通过检验后置条件在错误实现上是否被违反来衡量完整性；设计了三种生成设置并评估了五种 SOTA LLM 模型。

Result: 实验揭示当前 LLM 在正确性与完整性之间存在显著差距；仓库级依赖关系和方法复杂度会进一步加剧这一差距。

Conclusion: POSTCONDBENCH 推进了后置条件自动生成领域的发展，揭示了当前 SOTA LLM 在正确性与完整性之间存在显著差距，尤其在依赖仓库级上下文和方法复杂度增加时表现更差。该基准为后续研究提供了可执行且可量化的评估框架。

Abstract: Formal postconditions precisely characterize program behavior and support debugging, testing, and verification, but writing them requires substantial expertise and effort. This has motivated recent work on automatically generating postconditions from code and natural-language artifacts using large language models (LLMs). However, evaluation remains a key bottleneck. Existing benchmarks primarily emphasize correctness under limited evaluation settings, often relying on surface-form matching or manual assessment on small or synthetic datasets.
  We introduce POSTCONDBENCH, a multilingual benchmark for evaluating method-level postcondition generation from real-world software. POSTCONDBENCH comprises 420 Python and Java tasks drawn from 121 open-source projects, each paired with a high-quality ground-truth postcondition set constructed with expert involvement. To enable automatic evaluation, POSTCONDBENCH provides a runnable execution environment and operationalizes completeness via defect discrimination: a postcondition set is more complete if it is violated by more defective implementations while remaining satisfied on correct executions. Using POSTCONDBENCH, we formulate three generation settings and evaluate five SOTA LLMs. Our results reveal a substantial gap between correctness and completeness, and show that repository-level dependencies and method complexity exacerbate this gap.

</details>


### [188] [Geographic Variation in Stack Overflow Code Quality: Evidence from a Cross-Regional Study of Coding Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03670)
*Elijah Zolduoarrati,Sherlock A. Licorish,Nigel Stanger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究调查了Stack Overflow美国贡献者提供的SQL、JavaScript、Python、Ruby和Java代码片段质量，从可靠性、可读性、性能和安全性四个维度进行评估，并结合地区多样性和经济指标进行区域分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者频繁复用Stack Overflow代码片段，但其质量在不同编程语言和地区间的差异缺乏系统理解。

Method: 使用各语言特定的linting和静态分析工具量化违规情况，计算违规密度以实现公平的区域比较，并结合州级多样性指标进行分析；对加州、犹他州和北达科他州进行归纳式内容分析以识别定性模式。

Result: 可读性违规在所有语言中最普遍，其次是可靠性、性能和安全问题。主要问题包括空白符使用不当、格式不一致、程序流错误、资源使用低效、未消毒的输入和不安全的动态评估。技术中心产生更多可解析代码片段，但违规密度不一定更低；计算设备访问、互联网订阅、收入和财富分配更均衡的州违规更少。

Conclusion: 代码质量具有社会技术属性，技术成熟的地区产生更复杂的违规，而技术欠发达地区显示更基础的错误。开发者应谨慎复用在线代码片段。

Abstract: Developers frequently reuse Stack Overflow code snippets, yet the quality of these snippets remains unevenly understood, particularly across programming languages and geographic contexts. This study investigates code quality in Stack Overflow answers from contributors located in the United States, focusing on SQL, JavaScript, Python, Ruby, and Java snippets. We evaluate four quality dimensions: reliability, readability, performance, and security. Using language-specific linting and static analysis tools, we quantify violations across states and cities, compute violation densities to enable fair regional comparison, and examine relationships between code quality and state-level diversity indicators. We further conduct inductive content analysis on code snippets from California, Utah, and North Dakota to identify qualitative patterns in code quality violations. Results show that readability violations are the most prevalent across all languages, followed by reliability, performance, and security. Common issues include improper whitespace, inconsistent formatting, program-flow errors, inefficient resource use, unsanitised inputs, and insecure dynamic evaluation. Regional analysis indicates that major technology hubs produce more parsable snippets but do not necessarily exhibit higher violation densities. States with broader access to computing devices, Internet subscriptions, higher income, and more equitable wealth distribution tend to show fewer code quality violations. Qualitative findings suggest that established technology regions often produce more complex violations, while less mature technology regions display more fundamental errors. These findings highlight the socio-technical nature of code quality in community question-answering platforms and suggest that developers should exercise caution when reusing online code snippets.

</details>


### [189] [Multi-Agent Systems for Root Cause Analysis in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03505)
*Alexander Naakka,Yuqing Wang,Mika V Mäntylä*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LATS-RCA是一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，用于微服务系统中的根因分析。该框架采用树结构搜索方法，结合反思机制引导诊断过程，通过多个LLM驱动的智能体迭代分析各微服务的执行日志和性能指标以收集证据。在Light-OAuth2数据集和实际生产环境中进行了评估，证明了其在真实微服务系统中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自动化根因分析(RCA)方法通常依赖单一诊断路径的线性推理过程，无法有效应对复杂微服务系统中多因素故障的诊断需求。

Method: 提出LATS-RCA框架，将根因分析形式化为基于反思引导的树结构搜索，采用语言智能体树搜索(Language Agent Tree Search)算法。多个LLM驱动的智能体通过迭代方式分析各微服务的执行日志和性能指标，收集操作证据；利用中间诊断状态的反思分数引导搜索聚焦最可能的根因。

Result: 在Light-OAuth2数据集上达到较高诊断准确率；在实际生产环境（多语言技术栈、多样化日志实践）中诊断准确率较低且计算成本较高。生产环境部署证明了框架的实际适用性。

Conclusion: LATS-RCA能有效进行根因分析，生产环境部署展示了其在真实微服务系统中的实用价值。多语言技术栈、组件日志实践差异及多因素根因等生产级复杂性为诊断带来显著挑战。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled early attempts to automate root cause analysis (RCA) in microservice-based systems (MSS). Yet, prior works typically rely on a linear reasoning process that proceeds along a single diagnostic path. In this paper, we propose LATS-RCA, an LLM-based multi-agent framework for RCA in MSS. LATS-RCA formulates RCA as a reflection-guided tree-structured search using a Language Agent Tree Search algorithm. In LATS-RCA, multiple LLM-driven agents iteratively perform RCA for each microservice by reasoning over its execution logs and performance metrics to collect operational evidence for root cause exploration. Reflection scores derived from intermediate diagnostic states are used to guide the search toward the most likely root cause based on accumulated evidence. We evaluate LATS-RCA on the open-source industrial MSS, Light-OAuth2 (LO2), using a publicly available dataset and in a production microservice environment (Prod) in a case company with substantially higher operational complexity. LO2 is a small-team Java system with a homogeneous technology stack. The results on LO2 show that LATS-RCA achieves high diagnostic accuracy, and we further benchmark its associated computational costs. Compared to LO2, Prod attains lower diagnostic accuracy and incurs higher computational cost. The Prod deployment demonstrates the practical applicability of LATS-RCA in real-world MSS and reflects the challenges introduced by polyglot tech stack, varied logging practices of source components, and multi-factor root-causes by production-scale MSS.

</details>


### [190] [ProgramBench: Can Language Models Rebuild Programs From Scratch?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03546)
*John Yang,Kilian Lieret,Jeffrey Ma,Parth Thakkar,Dmitrii Pedchenko,Sten Sootla,Emily McMilin,Pengcheng Yin,Rui Hou,Gabriel Synnaeve,Diyi Yang,Ofir Press*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ProgramBench是一个评估软件工程智能体整体开发能力的基准测试，涵盖200个任务。当前语言模型在该测试中表现不佳，最佳模型通过率极低，且实现方式与人类代码差异明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试仅衡量有限、聚焦的任务（如修复单个bug或开发单一功能），无法评估智能体在长期项目中进行高级软件架构决策的能力。

Method: ProgramBench基准测试：给定程序及其文档，要求智能体自主架构并实现匹配参考可执行文件行为的代码库。测试通过智能体驱动的模糊测试生成端到端行为测试，不规定实现结构。涵盖200个任务，涵盖紧凑型CLI工具到FFmpeg、SQLite、PHP解释器等广泛使用的软件。

Result: 对9个语言模型进行评估，结果显示无一模型能完全解决任何任务。最佳模型仅在3%的任务上通过95%的测试。模型倾向于采用单体式单文件实现，与人类编写代码风格差异显著。

Conclusion: 当前语言模型在需要架构决策的整体软件开发任务上仍有显著不足。最佳模型仅在3%的任务上通过95%的测试，且模型倾向于采用与人类代码差异较大的单体单文件实现方式。

Abstract: Turning ideas into full software projects from scratch has become a popular use case for language models. Agents are being deployed to seed, maintain, and grow codebases over extended periods with minimal human oversight. Such settings require models to make high-level software architecture decisions. However, existing benchmarks measure focused, limited tasks such as fixing a single bug or developing a single, specified feature. We therefore introduce ProgramBench to measure the ability of software engineering agents to develop software holisitically. In ProgramBench, given only a program and its documentation, agents must architect and implement a codebase that matches the reference executable's behavior. End-to-end behavioral tests are generated via agent-driven fuzzing, enabling evaluation without prescribing implementation structure. Our 200 tasks range from compact CLI tools to widely used software such as FFmpeg, SQLite, and the PHP interpreter. We evaluate 9 LMs and find that none fully resolve any task, with the best model passing 95\% of tests on only 3\% of tasks. Models favor monolithic, single-file implementations that diverge sharply from human-written code.

</details>


### [191] [AI Advocate: Educational Path to Transform Squads to the Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03800)
*Carla Soares,Gabriel Moreira,Ana Paula Camargo,Fabio Henrique Scacabarozi,Nicole Davila,Marselle Silva*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了一家巴西私营科技公司培养"AI倡导者"以推动团队从传统软件开发向人机协作模式转型的经验，探讨了技能提升作为文化和技木转型催化剂的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人机协作在提升生产力方面的潜力日益显现，传统软件开发团队需要转型为混合结构，但这一转型过程面临文化和技木层面的挑战，需要系统性的教育和赋能策略。

Method: 本文采用经验报告（experience report）的方式，描述了一家巴西私营科技公司中AI倡导者（AI Advocates）的教育与赋能过程，通过这些专业人员作为催化剂来推动组织变革。

Result: 研究识别了转型过程中的关键经验教训和面临的挑战，为其他希望在组织中推广人机协作的企业提供了实践参考。

Conclusion: AI倡导者在人机协作转型中发挥关键催化作用，系统性的教育赋能策略是实现文化和技术双重转型的有效路径，但仍需应对特定挑战。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the strategic education process aimed at transitioning traditional software development squads into hybrid structures centered on collaborative work between humans and Artificial Intelligence (AI). In a context where human-AI collaboration can significantly increase productivity, this study explores how the upskilling of XPTO professionals, referred to as AI Advocates, acts as a catalyst for cultural and technical transformation. The objective is to present an experience report on the education and enablement process of AI Advocates within a private Brazilian technology company, highlighting key lessons learned and identified challenges.

</details>


### [192] [KVerus: Scalable and Resilient Formal Verification Proof Generation for Rust Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03822)
*Yuwei Liu,Xinyi Wan,Yanhao Wang,Minghua Wang,Lin Huang,Tao Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: KVerus通过检索增强方法解决大语言模型在形式化验证中的根本问题——语义-结构鸿沟，使验证系统能够适应代码库和工具链的演化变化，显著提升验证成功率并实现对真实内核代码的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证虽能提供最高级别的软件正确性保证，但在大规模、演化系统上的应用仍面临重大挑战。现有LLM方法因无法处理复杂的跨模块依赖或工具链变更而失败，根本原因在于"语义-结构鸿沟"——LLM基于语义模式工作，而形式化验证依赖严格的结构依赖关系。

Method: KVerus采用检索增强范式，构建代码元数据、引理语义和工具链信息的动态知识库，结合依赖感知的程序分析、语义引理索引和错误驱动的自优化机制，导航复杂的跨文件依赖以合成和自动修复证明。

Result: 在单文件基准测试中达到80.2%验证率（优于AutoVerus的56.9%），跨文件依赖测试中达51.0%（vs 4.5%基线），在Asterinas Rust OS内核内存管理模块成功验证23个此前未验证的函数（占证明代码21.0%）。

Conclusion: KVerus是向使形式化验证成为现代安全关键软件可扩展、可持续实践的重要一步。

Abstract: Formal verification provides the highest assurance of software correctness and security, but its application to large-scale, evolving systems remains a major challenge. While large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in automating proof generation, they often fail in real-world settings due to their inability to handle complex cross-module dependencies or changes in the codebase or the verification toolchain. We identify the fundamental problem as the Semantic-Structural Gap: LLMs operate on semantic code patterns, whereas formal verification is governed by rigid structural dependencies, a disconnect that leads to brittle, unsustainable proofs.
  To bridge this gap, we propose a new paradigm of self-adaptive verification and present KVerus, a retrieval-augmented system for Verus-based Rust verification that can adapt to an evolving software environment. KVerus constructs a dynamic knowledge base of code metadata, lemma semantics, and toolchain specifics. By combining dependency-aware program analysis, semantic lemma indexing, and error-driven self-refinement, it can navigate intricate cross-file dependencies to synthesize proofs and automatically repair proofs when faced with common evolutionary changes. Across three single-file benchmarks, KVerus verifies 80.2% of tasks, outperforming the state-of-the-art AutoVerus (56.9%) and degrades less than AutoVerus under breaking Verus updates. On three repository-level benchmarks with cross-file dependencies, KVerus achieves a 51.0% success rate, compared to 4.5% for a multi-round prompting baseline. Finally, on the Asterinas Rust OS kernel, KVerus produces upstream-accepted proofs that verify 23 previously unverified functions (21.0% of proof code) in the memory-management module. KVerus represents a significant step towards making formal verification a scalable and sustainable practice for modern, security-critical software.

</details>


### [193] [Beyond Rules: LLM-Powered Linting for Quantum Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03943)
*Pietro Cassieri,Giuseppe Scanniello,Seung Yeob Shin,Fabrizio Pastore,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: This paper proposes two LLM-based approaches (LintQ-LLM+CoT and LintQ-LLM+RAG) to improve quantum program problem detection, demonstrating significant performance gains over traditional rule-based linters through comprehensive evaluation on 55 Qiskit programs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional static analysis for quantum programs (rule-based linters) struggle to keep pace with rapidly evolving APIs and fail to capture complex, context-dependent quantum programming problems, resulting in high maintenance overhead and limited detection capabilities.

Method: Two novel LLM-based approaches: LintQ-LLM+CoT (using Chain-of-Thought prompting) and LintQ-LLM+RAG (grounding model reasoning in a curated knowledge base via Retrieval-Augmented Generation). Evaluated against state-of-the-art rule-based tool LintQ using 55 Qiskit programs.

Result: LLM-based approaches outperform LintQ in both precision and recall (F1-score 0.70 and 0.68 vs. 0.41). RAG-enhanced variant demonstrates slightly superior precision with reduced false positives.

Conclusion: LLM-based approaches with CoT prompting and RAG significantly outperform traditional rule-based linters (F1: 0.70/0.68 vs 0.41), with RAG-enhanced variant showing superior precision by reducing false positives. This demonstrates that LLMs offer a scalable and adaptive foundation for next-generation quantum software linters.

Abstract: As quantum computing transitions from theoretical experimentation to its practical application, the reliability of quantum software has become a critical bottleneck. Traditional static analysis techniques for quantum programs, primarily rule-based linters, are increasingly inadequate; they struggle to keep pace with rapidly evolving APIs and fail to capture complex, context-dependent quantum programming problems. This results in high maintenance overhead and limited detection capabilities. In this paper, we introduce LintQ-LLM+CoT and LintQ-LLM+RAG, novel approaches that redefine the detection of quantum programming problems by employing Large Language Models (LLMs) specialized, respectively, via Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting and a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system that grounds the model's reasoning in a curated knowledge base of verified quantum programming problems and best practices. We conducted a rigorous and manual comparative evaluation against the state-of-the-art rule-based tool, LintQ, using a corpus of 55 Qiskit programs. Our results show that LLM-based approaches, with and without RAG, outperform LintQ in terms of quantum programming problems detection correctness (precision) and completeness (recall). Overall, LLM-based approaches were more effective than LintQ (F1-score equal to 0.70 and 0.68 vs. 0.41). Furthermore, the RAG-enhanced variant demonstrated a slightly superior precision, effectively reducing false positives. Our findings suggest that LLMs provide a scalable and adaptive foundation for the next generation of linters in quantum software engineering.

</details>


### [194] [Randomized and Diverse Input State Generation for Quantum Program Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03957)
*Maryse Ernzer,Seung Yeob Shin,Fabrizio Pastore,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对量子软件测试中态空间覆盖不足的问题，提出Brick-Circuit（BC）构造的测试电路生成器，通过硬件兼容门近似理想随机态，并建立多样性评分框架评估生成器的表达性和纠缠能力。实验表明BC生成器在较浅深度下即可获得优于现有生成器的性能，为量子软件测试提供了更实用高效的输入生成方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统被应用于模拟等关键任务，量子软件的可靠性至关重要；现有输入覆盖准则未能充分覆盖量子态空间的独特结构，完整态空间探索的概念尚未得到充分关注。

Method: 提出Brick-Circuit（BC）构造，使用硬件兼容门生成测试输入态；建立包括幅度、相位和纠缠的局部相关性和全局分布的多样性评分体系；对BC生成器与现有生成器进行表达性和纠缠能力的对比评估。

Result: BC生成器在更浅的电路深度下，相比现有电路生成器达到了更高的表达性和纠缠性能。

Conclusion: BC生成器在更浅的深度下实现了更高的表达性和纠缠能力，表明硬件兼容的门序列可以有效逼近理想随机态，为量子软件测试提供更实用的输入覆盖方案。

Abstract: With the accelerating development of quantum technologies and their growing computational potential, quantum systems are being adapted for simulations and other critical tasks across diverse domains, making the reliability of the corresponding quantum software an essential concern. Although recent efforts have started to incorporate quantum-specific properties such as magnitude, phase, and entanglement under the form of input-coverage criteria into software testing, the unique structure of the quantum state space demands for more comprehensive testing. In particular, the notion of complete state-space exploration has so far received little attention.
  To address this gap, we propose a framework for evaluating test circuit generators with respect to their coverage of the quantum state space. Our contribution is threefold: we develop a set of diversity scores that capture both local and global indicators of the extent to which the state space is explored; we propose a test circuit generator that produces test input states via a Brick-Circuit (BC) construction designed to approximate ideal random states using hardware-compatible gates; we compare the proposed construction with existing generators based on their ability to generate uniformly distributed random test input states.
  Our extended diversity scores quantify the local correlations and global spread of magnitude, phase and entanglement. Using these scores, we evaluate the expressibility, defined as the capability to span the quantum state space uniformly, and entangling capabilities of existing generators relative to the BC generator. Our results show that the hardware-compatible BC generator achieves higher expressibility and entanglement performance at shallower depths than existing circuit generators.

</details>


### [195] [Mitigating False Positives in Static Memory Safety Analysis of Rust Programs via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04000)
*P Akilesh,Leuson Da Silva,Foutse Khomh,Sridhar Chimalakonda*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出利用强化学习自动分类和抑制Rust静态内存安全分析中的虚假警告，通过从Rust MIR提取特征并结合cargo-fuzz动态验证，实现精度从25.6%大幅提升至59.0%，准确率较最佳LLM基线提高17.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Rust静态分析工具（如Rudra和MirChecker）存在高误报率问题，这削弱了开发者信任，增加了人工审查工作量，并可能掩盖真正的漏洞。

Method: 设计了一个从Rust MIR中提取上下文特征的RL智能体，通过与静态分析输出交互优化警告抑制决策；引入cargo-fuzz作为辅助反馈机制，对可疑警告进行针对性模糊测试验证。

Result: 准确率达65.2%，F1分数为0.659，较最佳LLM基线提升17.1%；成功识别74.6%的真实bug；结合动态模糊测试后，准确率额外提升10.7个百分点，F1分数额外提升8.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于强化学习的警告抑制策略，结合cargo-fuzz动态验证机制，显著降低了Rust静态内存安全分析中的误报率，将精度从25.6%提升至59.0%，同时保持74.6%的召回率，证明了混合静态-动态分析在提升Rust内存安全验证工具实用性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Static analysis tools are essential for ensuring memory safety in Rust programs, particularly as Rust gains adoption in safety-critical domains. However, existing tools such as Rudra and MirChecker suffer from high false positive rates, which diminish developer trust, increase manual review effort, and may obscure genuine vulnerabilities. This paper presents a novel reinforcement learning (RL)-based approach for automatically classifying and suppressing spurious warnings in static memory safety analysis for Rust. To achieve this, we design an RL agent that learns a warning suppression policy by extracting contextual features from Rust's Mid-level Intermediate Representation (MIR) and optimizing its decisions through interaction with static analysis outputs. To improve decision quality, we integrate dynamic validation via cargo-fuzz as an auxiliary feedback mechanism, allowing the agent to selectively validate suspicious warnings through targeted fuzz testing. Our evaluation shows that the proposed approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art LLM-based baselines, achieving 65.2% accuracy and an F1 score of 0.659, an improvement of 17.1% over the best LLM baseline. With a recall of 74.6%, our method successfully identifies nearly three-quarters of true bugs while substantially reducing false positives, improving precision from 25.6% in raw Rudra output to 59.0%. Incorporating dynamic fuzzing further boosts performance, yielding additional improvements of 10.7 percentage points in accuracy and 8.6 percentage points in F1 score over the RL-only variant. Overall, our work demonstrates that combining reinforcement learning with hybrid static-dynamic analysis can substantially reduce false positives and improve the practical usability of memory safety verification tools for Rust.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Tutti: Making SSD-Backed KV Cache Practical for Long-Context LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03375)
*Shi Qiu,Yifan Hu,Xintao Wang,Wenhao Zhu,Jianqin Yan,Hao Chen,Kaiqiang Xu,Kai Chen,Yiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Tutti是一个高效的SSD后端KV缓存解决方案，通过消除CPU干预，实现GPU与SSD之间的高速数据传输。与最先进的GDS方案LMCache相比，Tutti将TTFT降低78.3%，吞吐量提升2倍，服务成本降低27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型上下文增长，KV缓存远超GPU HBM和CPU DRAM容量，需卸载到NVMe SSD。但现有方案因GPU内存碎片化导致大量小型随机I/O，CPU成为瓶颈，即使使用GPU Direct Storage(GDS)仍需CPU介入发起I/O。

Method: (1) GPU原生对象抽象支持批量KV缓存传输和管理；(2) 重构GPU存储栈，引入GPU io_uring支持异步GPU直连对象I/O；(3) 提出了slack感知的I/O调度避免GPU资源竞争。CPU仅负责异步加载每层I/O内核到GPU一次。

Result: 与GDS启用的LMCache相比，Tutti在严格SLO约束下TTFT降低78.3%，可实现请求率提升2倍，服务成本降低27%。Tutti达到接近DRAM后端LMCache的推理性能，同时提供近乎无限的容量。

Conclusion: 通过消除CPU干预和引入GPU io_uring等创新设计，Tutti成功饱和NVMe SSD带宽，将GPU停顿降至接近零，为LLM服务中的KV缓存卸载提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: LLM serving relies on prefix caching to improve inference performance. As growing contexts push key-value (KV) cache footprint far beyond GPU HBM and CPU DRAM capacity, KV cache is increasingly offloaded to NVMe SSDs. Unfortunately, restoring KV cache from SSDs suffers from poor I/O performance and incurs significant GPU stalls. This is primarily because the fragmented GPU memory layout results in a massive number of tiny random I/Os, rendering the low-parallelism CPU a severe bottleneck even with GPU Direct Storage (GDS), which still relies on CPU intervention to initiate each I/O and thus remains CPU-centric. This paper presents Tutti, an efficient SSD-backed KV caching solution that eliminates CPU intervention from the critical data and I/O control paths between HBM and SSDs. At the core of Tutti is a GPU-centric KV cache object store, in which the CPU is only responsible for asynchronously loading I/O kernels once per layer to the GPU. Tutti saturates NVMe SSD bandwidth and reduces GPU stalls to near zero through the following designs: (i) we provide a GPU-native object abstraction that enables bulk KV cache transfers and management; (ii) we re-architect the GPU storage stack by introducing GPU io_uring to support asynchronous GPU direct object I/O; and (iii) we propose slack-aware I/O scheduling to avoid GPU resource contention. We have implemented Tutti and integrated it to vLLM. Extensive evaluation shows that compared to the state-of-the-art GDS-enabled, SSD-backed LMCache, Tutti reduces TTFT by 78.3% under strict SLO constraints and improves the achievable request rate by 2x. The serving cost is reduced by 27%. Tutti achieves nearly the same inference performance as DRAM-backed LMCache, while providing almost infinite capacity.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [A Poisson Process for Submodular Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03071)
*Amit Ganz Rozenman,Ariel Kulik,Roy Schwartz,Mohit Singh*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了单调次模函数在拟阵独立约束下的最大化问题，提出一种基于随机泊松过程的新颖简单方法，无需离散化或舍入，仅需少量单元素交换即可达到(1-1/e)的紧近似比，并应用于次模福利最大化和一般/可分离分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题已研究超过十年：2008年连续贪婪算法获得紧近似保证，2012和2024年非遗忘局部搜索技术也达到相同保证。现有方法较为复杂，作者寻求更简洁的替代方案。

Method: 提出基于随机泊松过程的新方法，与现有两种技术不同，无需离散化或舍入，在执行极少的单元素交换操作即可完成。

Result: 该方法达到了(1-1/e)的紧近似比，并成功应用于次模福利最大化问题和一般/可分离分配问题，获得快速算法。

Conclusion: 该方法以极简的方式达到了最优近似保证，为次模优化领域提供了一种简洁有效的新范式。

Abstract: We study the problem of maximizing a monotone submodular function subject to a matroid independence constraint. For more than a decade, a rich body of work has studied this problem. Initially, a tight approximation of $ (1-\frac{1}{e})$ was given using the continuous greedy algorithm [Calinescu-Chekuri-Pal-Vondr{á}k STOC`2008] and later non-oblivious local search techniques were able to match this tight approximation guarantee [Filmus-Ward FOCS`2012] and [Buchbinder-Feldman FOCS`2024].
  We propose a new and remarkably simple approach to this problem that is based on a stochastic Poisson process. Our approach matches the tight $ (1-\frac{1}{e})$ approximation guarantee and it differs from the known two techniques since it does not require discretization or rounding while performing very few single element swaps. We also present applications of our approach and obtain fast algorithms for submodular welfare maximization, and for the general and separable assignment problems.

</details>


### [198] [Dynamic Detours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03225)
*Daniel Dadush,Michał Pilipczuk,Amadeus Reinald,Marek Sokołowski,Michał Włodarczyk*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文设计了支持边插入和删除的动态图数据结构，能够高效回答长路径查询、绕行查询和奇偶路径查询。其核心发现是：与已知的长度受限短路径查询下界不同，长路径查询可以高效处理（摊销时间$O(\log^2 n \log^2 \log n)$或$2^{O(k^3)} \log n$级别）。这与三角形猜想失败时短路径查询不存在$O(n^{o(1)})$时间数据结构的已知条件_lower bound形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文研究的动机来自于对动态图上路径查询数据结构的探索。给定参数k，需要在完全动态无向图（支持边插入和删除）中回答以下查询：(1)长(u,v)路径：判断是否存在长度至少为k的路径；(2)长(u,v)绕行：判断是否存在长度至少为dist_G(u,v)+k的路径；(3)偶/奇(u,v)路径：判断是否存在偶/奇长度的路径。

Method: 论文的主要技术贡献是"延迟边插入"(delayed edge insertion)机制，该机制在双连通分量(biconnected components)的层面上局部运作。这种技术使得在动态更新过程中能够高效地维护和查询路径信息。

Result: 论文实现了以下摊销时间复杂度：对于前两类查询，更新或回答查询的时间为$2^{O(k^3)} \log n + O(\log^2 n \log^2 \log n)$；对于奇偶路径查询，时间为$O(\log^2 n \log^2 \log n)$，其中n为图的顶点数。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了动态图数据结构中"长路径查询比短路径查询更容易处理"这一反直觉现象。虽然已知短路径（如长度至多2的路径）查询在条件下难以获得亚线性时间，但长路径（长度至少k）和奇偶路径查询却可以高效实现。这挑战了该领域的传统认知，并为动态图算法的研究方向提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Fix a parameter $k\in \mathbf{N}$. We give dynamic data structures that for a fully dynamic undirected graph $G$, updated over time by edge insertions and edge deletions, can answer the following queries:
  - Long $(u,v)$-path: Given $u,v\in V(G)$, is there a path from $u$ to $v$ of length at least $k$?
  - Long $(u,v)$-detour: Given $u,v\in V(G)$, is there a path from $u$ to $v$ of length at least $\text{dist}_G(u,v)+k$?
  - Even/odd $(u,v)$-path: Given $u,v\in V(G)$, is there a path from $u$ to $v$ of even/odd length?
  The amortized time of executing an update or answering a query is $2^{O(k^3)} \log n + O(\log^2 n \log^2 \log n)$ in the first two cases, and $O(\log^2 n \log^2 \log n)$ in the last, where $n$ is the number of vertices of $G$.
  The first result is in sharp contrast with known conditional lower bounds for reporting paths of length at most $k$. Specifically, there is no data structure supporting queries about $(u,v)$-paths of length at most two in time $n^{o(1)}$ unless the Triangle Conjecture fails. Our main technical contribution is a mechanism of "delayed edge insertion" that works locally on the level of biconnected components.

</details>


### [199] [Provable Accuracy Collapse in Embedding-Based Representations under Dimensionality Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03346)
*Dionysis Arvanitakis,Vaggos Chatziafratis,Yiyuan Luo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 证明了嵌入维度与精度之间的尖锐权衡：除非嵌入维度接近真实维度，否则精度会骤然崩塌至50%的平凡基线，并基于Unique Games Conjecture证明即使在一维可实现的情况下，多项式时间算法也无法克服这一精度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中，基于欧氏空间R^d的嵌入表示是核心方法，主要目标是使用最小维度来忠实捕获数据关系。关键问题在于维度选择如何影响表示精度。

Method: 研究标准对比学习设置中的锚点-正样本-负样本三元组(i,j,k)，这些三元组编码距离比较dist(i,j) < dist(i,k)，假设这些三元组可由未知D维真实嵌入实现。

Result: 存在常数c<1，使得维度至多cD的任何嵌入都会违反一半的三元组，精度低至仅50%，等同于忽略输入的一维平凡解。

Conclusion: 除非嵌入维度d接近真实维度D，否则精度会发生骤然崩塌。即使数据几乎可在一维实现，在Unique Games Conjecture下，多项式时间算法也无法超越50%的平凡基线。

Abstract: Embedding-based representations in Euclidean space $\mathbb{R}^d$ are a cornerstone of modern machine learning, where a major goal is to use the \emph{smallest dimension} that faithfully captures data relations. In this work, we prove sharp dimension--accuracy tradeoffs and identify a fundamental information-theoretic limitation: unless the embedding dimension $d$ is chosen close to the ground-truth dimension $D$, accuracy undergoes a sudden collapse. Our main result shows that this phenomenon arises even in standard contrastive learning settings, where supervision is limited to a set of $m$ anchor--positive--negative triplets $(i,j,k)$ encoding distance comparisons $\mathrm{dist}(i,j) < \mathrm{dist}(i,k)$. Specifically, given triplets realizable by an unknown ground-truth embedding in $D$ dimensions, we prove that there exists constant $c < 1$, such that \emph{every embedding of dimension at most $cD$ violates half of the triplets}, yielding accuracy as low as a trivial one-dimensional solution that ignores the input. We complement our information-theoretic bounds with strong computational hardness results: under the Unique Games Conjecture, even if the given triplets are nearly realizable in $D=1$ dimension, no polynomial-time algorithm -- \textit{regardless of its dimension} -- can achieve accuracy above the trivial $50\%$ baseline.

</details>


### [200] [An Optimal Algorithm for Cardinality-Constrained Diameter Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03431)
*Chao Xu,Mingdong Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究基数约束直径划分问题，提出O(n²)最优算法（改进自O(n² log n)），通过将问题归约为最大生成树瓶颈2-着色问题求解，并证明下界；同时对欧几里得权重给出亚二次算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进已有的O(n² log n)算法，解决基数约束直径划分问题

Method: 将问题归约为最大生成树上的瓶颈2-着色问题，通过树动态规划求解；同时对欧几里得权重利用几何结构特性

Result: 提出O(n²)算法和匹配的Ω(n²)下界；同时输出所有基数的最优解；欧几里得权重下实现亚二次时间

Conclusion: 算法达到最优且匹配下界；欧几里得权重下可实现亚二次时间

Abstract: Cardinality-constrained diameter partitioning asks for a partition of $n$ items into two classes of prescribed sizes that minimizes the larger of the two class diameters. We give an $O(n^2)$ algorithm and a matching $Ω(n^2)$ lower bound if we can only query the weight between two elements. The algorithm computes the optimum for every cardinality simultaneously, improving Avis's $O(n^2\log n)$. The reduction is to a bottleneck 2-coloring problem on the maximum spanning tree, solved by a standard tree DP. For a single cardinality with Euclidean weights, we obtain a subquadratic time algorithm in any fixed dimension.

</details>


### [201] [The Parameterized Complexity of Scheduling with Precedence Delays: Shuffle Product and Directed Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03727)
*Hans L. Bodlaender,Maher Mallem*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有优先约束和延迟值的调度问题的参数化复杂性。通过证明Shuffle Product和Directed Bandwidth两个经典问题的XNLP完全性，建立了弹性调度问题关于图宽度和延迟值的计算下界，为这些长期悬而未决的问题提供了完整的复杂性分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决调度与优先约束问题的长期悬而未决的参数化复杂性状态，特别是Shuffle Product和Directed Bandwidth这两个数十年未被分类的问题。

Method: 证明了Shuffle Product和Directed Bandwidth两个经典问题关于目标值的XNLP完全性，然后利用这些结果推导弹性调度问题的参数化复杂性下界。通过参数化规约建立了这些问题的计算硬度。

Result: 证明了Shuffle Product和Directed Bandwidth关于目标值是XNLP完全问题；推导出具有最小/最大延迟的调度问题关于有向无环图宽度和/或最大延迟值的XNLP下界；完全分类了树结构上Directed Bandwidth问题的参数化复杂性。

Conclusion: Shuffle Product被确立为XNLP完全问题的一个非常规且有前景的补充；该研究为具有延迟约束的调度问题的参数化复杂性提供了完整的分类。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the parameterized complexity of several variants of scheduling with precedence constraints between jobs. Namely, we consider the single machine setting with delay values on top of the precedence constraints. Such scheduling problems are related to several decades-old problems with open parameterized complexity status, notably Shuffle Product and Directed Bandwidth. We obtain XNLP-completeness results for both problems, and derive implications to scheduling with minimum (resp. maximum) delays parameterized by the width of the directed acyclic graph giving the precedence constraints, and/or by the maximum delay value in the input. Regarding Directed Bandwidth, we also settle the case of trees by showing XNLP-completeness parameterized by the target value. Beyond these results, we believe that Shuffle Product is an unusual and promising addition to the list of XNLP-complete problems.

</details>


### [202] [On Computing Total Variation Distance Between Mixtures of Product Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03839)
*Weiming Feng,Yucheng Fu,Minji Yang,Anqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究了混合乘积分布间总变差距离的近似计算问题。对一般离散域给出多项式时间随机近似算法；对布尔子立方体这一特殊情形给出精确算法，但证明精确计算问题在参数线性增长时是#P-难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两个混合乘积分布之间的总变差距离近似问题，这是概率分布距离估计的基础问题，在统计学习和理论计算机科学中有重要应用。

Method: 对于一般情况，使用随机化近似算法，时间复杂度为poly((nq)^(k1+k2), 1/ε)；对于布尔子立方体特殊情况，采用确定性精确算法。

Result: 给出了两个结果：(1)一般q元域混合分布：随机算法在poly((nq)^(k1+k2), 1/ε)时间内达到(1±ε)乘法误差近似；(2)布尔子立方体混合：确定性精确算法复杂度poly(n, 2^{O(k1+k2)})，且当k1+k2=Θ(n)时精确计算是#P-难的。

Conclusion: 对于一般的q元域混合分布，提供了多项式时间随机近似算法；对于布尔子立方体混合分布，提供了精确算法但可能是指数级的，并证明了精确计算问题是#P-难的，当组件总数与维度线性增长时。

Abstract: We study the problem of approximating the total variation distance between two mixtures of product distributions over an $n$-dimensional discrete domain. Given two mixtures $\mathbb{P}$ and $\mathbb{Q}$ with $k_1$ and $k_2$ product distributions over $[q]^n$, respectively, we give a randomized algorithm that approximates $d_{\mathrm{TV}}\left({\mathbb{P}},{\mathbb{Q}}\right)$ within a multiplicative error of $(1\pm \varepsilon)$ in time $\mathrm{poly}((nq)^{k_1+k_2},1/\varepsilon)$. We also study the special case of mixtures of Boolean subcubes over $\{0,1\}^n$. For this class, we give a deterministic algorithm that exactly computes the total variation distance in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,2^{O(k_1+k_2)})$, and show that exact computation is $\#\mathsf{P}$-hard when $k_1+k_2=Θ(n)$.

</details>


### [203] [Parallel Reachability and Shortest Paths on Non-sparse Digraphs: Near-linear Work and Sub-square-root Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03892)
*Vikrant Ashvinkumar,Aaron Bernstein,Maximilian Probst Gutenberg,Thatchaphol Saranurak*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出在稠密图上突破Ω(√n)深度瓶颈的并行算法，reachability和shortest path在近线性工作量下分别达到n^0.136和n^0.25+o(1)深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近线性工作量的并行reachability和shortest path算法在所有密度下都需Ω(√n)深度，存在下界瓶颈，限制了并行计算效率。

Method: 提出接近线性工作量的并行算法，在m≥n^{1+o(1)}时实现亚平方根深度，采用新的图分解或组合技术。

Result: 在m=Ω(n²)极端稠密情况下，reachability算法深度仅n^0.136，shortest path算法深度仅n^0.25+o(1)。

Conclusion: 首次在稠密图上突破了o(√n)深度瓶颈，reachability可达n^0.136深度，shortest path可达n^0.25+o(1)深度。

Abstract: We present parallel algorithms for computing single-source reachability and shortest paths on directed $n$-vertex $m$-edge graphs using near-linear $\tilde{O}(m)$ work and $o(\sqrt{n})$ depth whenever $m\ge n^{1+o(1)}$. At the extreme of $m=Ω(n^{2})$, our reachability and shortest path algorithms have depth only $n^{0.136}$ and $n^{0.25+o(1)}$, respectively. The state-of-the-art parallel algorithms with near-linear work for both problems require $Ω(\sqrt{n})$ depth in all density regimes.

</details>


### [204] [Optimal Hardness of Online Algorithms for Large Common Induced Subgraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03893)
*David Gamarnik,Miklós Z. Rácz,Gabe Schoenbach*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文研究两个独立Erdős-Rényi随机图$G_1, G_2 \sim \mathbb{G}(n,1/2)$的最大公共导出子图问题。证明贪心在线算法可找到大小为$(2-o(1))\log_2 n$的公共导出子图，并证明任何在线算法都无法以恒定概率找到大小至少为$(2+\varepsilon)\log_2 n$的公共导出子图，揭示了该问题存在计算-优化gap。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来Chatterjee和Diaconis证明两个独立随机图的最大公共导出子图大小以高概率为$(4-o(1))\log_2 n$，但该最优解难以通过在线算法高效找到。研究旨在探究是否存在计算-优化间隙(computation-to-optimization gap)，即最优解大小与在线算法可达到的大小之间是否存在显著差距。

Method: 1. 设计并分析简单贪心在线算法；2. 利用解空间的重叠间隙性质(OGP/overlap gap property)证明下界；3. 运用Gamarnik等人发展的插值论证方法建立OGP与在线算法局限性的联系。

Result: 1. 简单贪心在线算法可找到大小为$(2-o(1))\log_2 n$的公共导出子图；2. 证明了任何在线算法都无法以恒定概率找到大小至少为$(2+\varepsilon)\log_2 n$的解；3. 建立了问题解空间的(多重)重叠间隙性质。

Conclusion: 该问题存在显著的计算-优化间隙：最优解大小为$(4-o(1))\log_2 n$，而在线算法最多只能达到约$2\log_2 n$，间隙因子约为2。这一结果为利用OGP理论分析组合优化问题的计算复杂性提供了新的证据。

Abstract: We study the problem of efficiently finding large common induced subgraphs of two independent Erdős--Rényi random graphs $G_1, G_2 \sim \mathbb{G}(n,1/2)$. Recently, Chatterjee and Diaconis showed that the largest common induced subgraph of $G_1$ and $G_2$ has size $(4-o(1))\log_2 n$ with high probability. We first show that a simple greedy online algorithm finds a common induced subgraph of $G_1$ and $G_2$ of size $(2-o(1)) \log_2 n$ with high probability. Our main result shows that no online algorithm can find a common induced subgraph of $G_1$ and $G_2$ of size at least $(2+\varepsilon) \log_2 n$ with probability bounded away from $0$ as $n \to \infty$. Together, these results provide evidence that this problem exhibits a computation-to-optimization gap. To prove the impossibility result, we show that the solution space of the problem exhibits a version of the (multi) overlap gap property (OGP), and utilize an interpolation argument recently developed by Gamarnik, Kizildağ, and Warnke that connects OGP and online algorithms.

</details>


### [205] [An $\widetilde{O} (n^{3/7})$ Round Parallel Algorithm for Matroid Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03979)
*Sanjeev Khanna,Aaron Putterman,Junkai Song*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究 matroid 找基问题的并行轮复杂度，通过新的结构分析框架将最优轮数从 $\widetilde O(n^{7/15})$ 改进至 $\widetilde O(n^{3/7})$，缩小了存在近四十年的上下界差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决近四十年悬而未决的难题：K UW证明 $O(\sqrt{n})$ 上界与 $\widetilde Ω(n^{1/3})$ 下界之间存在巨大差距，KPS 于2025年首次打破僵局达到 $\widetilde O(n^{7/15})$，但仍需进一步改进以缩小差距。

Method: 开发了新的结构和算法框架，将分析视角从单个元素转移到理解依赖关系如何在多个元素间同时传播。

Result: 给出 $\widetilde O(n^{3/7})$ 轮的新算法，比 KPS 的 $\widetilde O(n^{7/15})$ 有显著提升（因 $3/7 < 7/15$，即 $n^{0.429} < n^{0.467}$）。

Conclusion: 提出新算法将找基轮数减少至 $\widetilde O(n^{3/7})$，首次超越 KPS 的 $\widetilde O(n^{7/15})$ 结果；关键创新在于从单一元素分析转向多元素依赖关系的整体框架。

Abstract: We study the parallel (adaptive) complexity of the classic problem of finding a basis in an $n$-element matroid, given access via an \emph{independence oracle}. In this model, the algorithm may submit polynomially many independence queries in each round, and the central question is: how many rounds are necessary and sufficient to find a basis?
  Karp, Upfal, and Wigderson (FOCS~1985, JCSS~1988; hereafter KUW) initiated this study, showing that $O(\sqrt{n})$ adaptive rounds suffice for any matroid, and that $\widetildeΩ(n^{1/3})$ rounds are necessary even for partition matroids. This left a substantial gap that persisted for nearly four decades, until Khanna, Putterman, and Song (FOCS~2025; hereafter KPS) achieved $\widetilde O(n^{7/15})$ rounds, the first improvement since~KUW.
  In this work, we make another conceptual advance beyond KPS, giving a new algorithm that finds a matroid basis in $\widetilde O(n^{3/7})$ rounds. We develop a structural and algorithmic framework that brings a new lens to the analysis of random circuits, moving from reasoning about individual elements to understanding how dependencies span multiple elements simultaneously.

</details>

### [206] [FINER-SQL: Boosting Small Language Models for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03465)
*Thanh Dat Hoang,Thanh Trung Huynh,Matthias Weidlich,Thanh Tam Nguyen,Tong Chen,Hongzhi Yin,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: FINER-SQL proposes a reinforcement learning framework with fine-grained execution feedback (memory reward + atomic reward) to train small language models (3B) for Text-to-SQL, achieving competitive accuracy with LLMs while being cost-efficient and privacy-preserving.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large language models for Text-to-SQL suffer from high computational cost, long inference latency, and data privacy concerns, making them impractical for real-world deployment. While small language models (SLMs) offer efficient on-premise deployment, they struggle with weak reasoning and poor instruction following. Existing reinforcement learning methods using sparse binary rewards (0/1) provide insufficient learning signals when SQLs are incorrect, leading to unstable or collapsed training.

Method: FINER-SQL is a scalable and reusable reinforcement learning framework built on group relative policy optimization (GRPO). It replaces sparse binary supervision with dense and interpretable rewards offering continuous feedback: (1) Memory reward aligns reasoning with verified traces for semantic stability, (2) Atomic reward measures operation-level overlap to grant partial credit for structurally correct but incomplete SQLs. This transforms discrete correctness into continuous learning for stable, critic-free optimization.

Result: On BIRD and Spider benchmarks, FINER-SQL achieves up to 67.73% and 85% execution accuracy respectively with only a 3B model, matching much larger LLMs while reducing inference latency to 5.57s/sample.

Conclusion: FINER-SQL provides a cost-efficient and privacy-preserving path toward high-performance Text-to-SQL generation, enabling stable training of SLMs without critics through continuous dense rewards.

Abstract: Large language models have driven major advances in Text-to-SQL generation. However, they suffer from high computational cost, long latency, and data privacy concerns, which make them impractical for many real-world applications. A natural alternative is to use small language models (SLMs), which enable efficient and private on-premise deployment. Yet, SLMs often struggle with weak reasoning and poor instruction following. Conventional reinforcement learning methods based on sparse binary rewards (0/1) provide little learning signal when the generated SQLs are incorrect, leading to unstable or collapsed training. To overcome these issues, we propose FINER-SQL, a scalable and reusable reinforcement learning framework that enhances SLMs through fine-grained execution feedback. Built on group relative policy optimization, FINER-SQL replaces sparse supervision with dense and interpretable rewards that offer continuous feedback even for incorrect SQLs. It introduces two key reward functions: a memory reward, which aligns reasoning with verified traces for semantic stability, and an atomic reward, which measures operation-level overlap to grant partial credit for structurally correct but incomplete SQLs. This approach transforms discrete correctness into continuous learning, enabling stable, critic-free optimization. Experiments on the BIRD and Spider benchmarks show that FINER-SQL achieves up to 67.73\% and 85\% execution accuracy with a 3B model -- matching much larger LLMs while reducing inference latency to 5.57~s/sample. These results highlight a cost-efficient and privacy-preserving path toward high-performance Text-to-SQL generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/thanhdath/finer-sql.

</details>


### [207] [In-memory Multidimensional Indexing Using the skd-tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03640)
*Achilleas Michalopoulos,Dimitrios Tsitsigkos,Nikos Mamoulis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为slicing kd-tree（skd-tree）的新型多维数据索引结构。该结构通过将每个节点的空间划分为多个切片并压缩分割器，结合数据并行性和SIMD指令优化，显著降低了树的深度和查询计算复杂度。同时提出了创新的自顶向下构建算法、平衡机制和更新算法，在真实和合成数据集上的实验表明其性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着主存数据库的发展，需要高效索引多个维度以支持多维范围查询和最近邻查询。现有数据结构（R树、kd树、四叉树、网格分区）以及最近提出的多维学习索引都存在一定局限性，亟需更高效的解决方案来提升查询性能。

Method: 通过将每个节点的空间划分为多个切片来压缩分割器，结合数据并行性，减少树的层数并限制查询计算量。设计了三项核心贡献：(1)创新的自顶向下构建算法，(2)支持树平衡的内节点和叶节点设计，(3)新型更新算法。在遍历过程中，每个节点仅应用少量SIMD指令完成查询处理。

Result: skd-tree通过切片压缩大幅降低树的层数，结合SIMD指令实现常数级计算量优化，在多维范围查询和KNN查询中展现了显著的性能提升，优于现有主流索引方法。

Conclusion: 实验评估表明，skd-tree在真实和合成数据集上相比现有方法实现了优异的性能表现。这种通过切片压缩和SIMD并行化来优化kd-tree的方法，为主存数据库中的多维数据索引提供了一种高效解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we revisit the problem of indexing multi-dimensional data in memory for the efficient support of multi-dimensional range queries and nearest neighbor queries. This is a classic problem in main-memory databases, where there is a need for indexing multiple columns simultaneously. Established data structures include the R-tree, kd-tree, quad-tree, and grid-based partitioning. More recently, multi-dimensional learned indexes have also been proposed to address this problem. We propose slicing kd-tree (skd-tree), a variant of the kd-tree, where each node partitions the space of its subtree into multiple slices across a single splitting dimension. By compressing the splitters of the partitions and with the help of data-parallelism, we (i) radically reduce the number of levels of the tree and (ii) limit the number of computations required for multi-dimensional range and proximity queries. The nodes of the skd-tree resemble the nodes of a main-memory B+-tree, however, a different dimension is used at each level. Our novel range and kNN algorithms on the skd-tree apply only a small constant number of SIMD instructions at each node during tree traversal. Our contributions also include a novel top-down construction algorithm, different types of inner and leaf nodes that warrant tree balancing, and a novel update algorithm. Our skd-tree achieves strong performance compared to existing methods, according to our experimental evaluation on real and synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [208] [ConRAD: Conformal Risk-Aware Neural Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03806)
*Sonia Horchidan,Fabian Zeiher,Xiangyu Shi,Vasiliki Kalavri,Henrik Boström,Ioannis Kontoyiannis,Paris Carbone*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Querying incomplete knowledge graphs with neural predictors is powerful but dangerous. Errors compound across multi-hop pipelines with no formal bound on the completeness of results. We introduce ConRAD, the first framework to enforce declarative recall guarantees natively within a neural graph database query engine. Given a user-specified risk budget, ConRAD automatically derives per-operator prediction thresholds that satisfy the recall target with finite-sample, distribution-free statistical validity via Conformal Risk Control, while maximizing end-to-end precision. To scale calibration across multi-operator query topologies, we introduce a quantile-space scalarization that reduces intractable high-dimensional threshold searches to a single parameter. We further design the conformal gate, a novel physical operator that dynamically bypasses neural inference when local graph evidence suffices, eliminating unnecessary model inferences in dense graph regions. Evaluated across three benchmarks and three query topologies, ConRAD strictly satisfies all risk budgets, with empirical recall falling below the target by at most 0.046 across all settings. It reduces neural invocations to zero in near-complete graph regions, and achieves precision that matches or exceeds best-case static baselines that offer no guarantees and require manual threshold search.

</details>


### [209] [Inconsistent Databases and Argumentation Frameworks with Collective Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03954)
*Yasir Mahmood,Jonni Virtema,Timon Barlag,Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文建立了不一致数据库修复与SETAFs之间的形式化对应关系。研究表明，子集极大修复在声明约束下对应多种论证扩展语义，而在LAV元组生成依赖下仅对应preferred扩展。不同IC类型决定了对论证框架复杂度的需求：某些IC可简化为传统AF，另一些则需SETAFs的集合攻击机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不一致数据库的子集极大修复与论证框架可接受论证集之间的深层联系，特别是当IC包含声明约束和LAV元组生成依赖时的对应关系。

Method: 通过建立数据库修复与SETAFs语义之间的映射关系，证明修复与各种扩展语义（naive、preferred、stable）之间的对应性，并分析不同IC类型对映射的影响。

Result: ①声明约束下，子集极大修复对应naive扩展，也等于SETAFs中的preferred和stable扩展；②元组生成依赖下，修复对应preferred扩展，预处理后可得唯一stable和naive扩展；③两类约束混合时，仅preferred语义能刻画修复；④函数依赖和包含依赖无需集合攻击，可映射为传统AF。

Conclusion: SETAFs在建模包含声明约束和LAV元组生成依赖的数据库修复方面优于传统AFs，但并非所有IC都需要集合攻击，函数依赖和包含依赖可映射到传统AFs。

Abstract: The connection between subset-maximal repairs for inconsistent databases involving various integrity constraints and acceptable sets of arguments within argumentation frameworks has recently drawn growing interest. In this paper, we contribute to this domain by establishing a new connection when integrity constraints (ICs) include denial constraints and local-as-view tuple-generating dependencies. It turns out that SET-based Argumentation Frameworks (SETAFs), an extension of Dung's argumentation frameworks (AFs) allowing collective attacks, are needed. It is known that subset-maximal repairs under denial constraints correspond to the naive extensions, which also coincide with the preferred and stable extensions in the resulting SETAFs. Our main findings establish that repairs under the considered fragment of tuple-generating dependencies correspond to the preferred extensions. Moreover, for these dependencies, additional preprocessing allows computing a unique extension that is stable and naive. Allowing both types of constraints breaks this relationship, and even the pre-processing does not help as only preferred semantics captures these repairs. Finally, while it is known that functional dependencies do not require set-based attacks, we prove the same regarding inclusion dependencies. Thus, one can translate inconsistent databases under these restricted classes of ICs to plain AFs with attacks only between arguments.

</details>

### [210] [Cheap Expertise: Mapping and Challenging Industry Perspectives in the Expert Data Gig Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03295)
*Robert Wolfe,Aayushi Dangol*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析五家人工智能数据标注组织及其CEO的公开传播内容，揭示了AI行业如何构建专业知识未来的愿景：即AI专业知识廉价且高效，人类专业知识被重新定位为可被提取和定价的资源，而机构专业知识则需被"解放"以融入AI系统。该研究警示这一趋势可能重塑白领工作形态和专业知识的社会价值体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI行业对专家标注数据需求的激增，一个以数据标注为形式的"专家零工经济"正在兴起。该研究旨在探究这一新兴行业如何通过公共传播渠道塑造并合理化其对"专业知识未来"的愿景，以及这种愿景对人类专家和社会制度可能产生的影响。

Method: 研究者收集并分析了五家行业数据标注组织及其CEO在社交媒体（Twitter/X、LinkedIn等）和播客公开露面中的沟通内容，采用质性文本分析方法探索这些组织对未来专业知识愿景的叙事框架与话语策略。

Result: 研究发现AI行业对专业知识前景的描述呈现三个核心隐喻：①AI专业知识是"廉价的"——因能提供比人类专业知识更高的投资回报率而具有优势；②人类专业知识被视为"可提取的资源"——其价值可相对于AI专业知识被评判和定价；③机构专业知识（如高校和企业持有的）被认为需要"解放或改革"——以便整合进最新AI系统。

Conclusion: 研究提出了若干"挑衅性"问题，呼吁学界与政策制定者思考社会应如何应对AI驱动的专家零工经济及其"廉价专业知识"愿景。暗示需要重新审视现有专业制度设计，以应对行业对专业知识价值的重新定义和再定价。

Abstract: Demand for expert-annotated data on the part of leading AI labs has created an expert gig economy with the potential to reshape white collar work and society's understanding of expertise. In this research, we study the vision for the future of expertise described in the public communication of five industry data annotation organizations and their CEOs, as reflected on social media feeds and public appearances on podcasts. We find that the industry envisions AI expertise as cheap, meaning that it can offer a better return on investment than human expertise. Human expertise, meanwhile, is viewed as an extractable resource, the value of which can be judged relative to AI expertise. Finally, institutional expertise (such as that created or possessed by universities and corporations) is viewed as in need of liberation or reform, such that it can be incorporated into the latest artificial intelligence systems. Our findings have implications for human experts, whose professional lives may be transformed and revalued by this industry, as well as for societal institutions that mediate expertise. We close this work with a series of provocations intended to elicit consideration of how society can best approach an AI-driven expert gig economy and the cheap expertise it intends to produce.

</details>


### [211] [Beyond Distributive Justice: Hermeneutical Fairness in Ad Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03419)
*Camilla Quaresmini,Valentina Breschi,Jessica Leoni,Viola Schiaffonati,Mara Tanelli,Giulia De Pasquale*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文引入 Miranda Fricker 的认知不正义概念来分析在线广告公平性，提出认知公平约束和认知感知效用成本，将其整合到分配公平广告分配框架中，通过模拟发现分配约束与认知公平需要协同考虑，纯效用导向分配会损害弱势群体利益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线广告的公平性通常被形式化为分配正义问题，但广告仍可能通过内容本身以及接收者对广告的解释和接受方式造成伤害。传统的分配正义框架无法捕捉这种认知层面的不公正。

Method: 基于1986-1987年艾滋病广告评估调查的探索性相关模式，提出群体层面的认知公平约束和认知感知效用成本，并将其整合到现有的分配公平广告分配框架中，通过控制变量模拟探索经济效用、分配公平约束与认知成本之间的权衡。

Result: 模拟结果显示：纯效用导向分配导致对弱势群体的投放不足；分配约束在高认知风险情况下可在适度效用损失下降低认知成本；不加分配约束地加权认知成本可能产生集中于弱势群体的政策。这表明分配约束与认知公平需要协同考虑。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，应将在线广告的公平性分析扩展到分配公平之外，包含解释和接受的认知条件。单纯基于效用的分配会导致对弱势群体投放不足；分配约束可在适度效用损失下降低认知成本；而不加分配约束地加权认知成本可能产生集中于弱势群体的政策。这项研究为在线广告公平性研究开辟了新的认知正义维度。

Abstract: Fairness in online advertising is often formalized as a distributive justice problem, aiming to ensure that impressions, opportunities, or outcomes are allocated comparably across protected groups. Yet online advertising can still produce harms arising from ads' content and from how recipients interpret and uptake them. To capture this dimension, we draw on Miranda Fricker's notion of hermeneutical injustice. We model ad delivery as a mechanism that distributes interpretative resources and can fail in two ways: relevant concepts can be withheld through systematic under-exposure, leading to hermeneutical deprivation; and recipients may experience hermeneutical distortions when saturated with low-uptake or skewed framings. Grounded in exploratory correlational patterns from the AIDS Advertising Evaluation surveys (1986-1987), we introduce a group-level hermeneutical fairness constraint and a hermeneutically aware utility cost. We integrate them into a benchmark, utility-driven ad allocation framework that already enforces distributive justice, yielding a distributively fair, hermeneutically aware framework that prevents deprivation and distortion from concentrating within protected groups. Through controlled simulations, we explore trade-offs between economic utility, classical distributive fairness constraints, and hermeneutical cost. The results show that purely utility-based allocation drives under-delivery to the disadvantaged group. When the hermeneutical stakes of withholding ads are high, distributive constraints reduce hermeneutical cost at modest utility loss. Conversely, weighting hermeneutical cost without distributive constraints can yield policies concentrated on the disadvantaged group. These findings motivate expanding fairness analyses of online advertising beyond distributive notions to include epistemic conditions of interpretation and uptake.

</details>


### [212] [Brainrot: Deskilling and Addiction are Overlooked AI Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03512)
*Ilias Chalkidis,Anders Søgaard*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: The paper identifies a gap in AI safety and alignment research: existing work focuses on discrimination, harmful content, information hazards, and malicious use cases, while largely ignoring public concerns about cognitive and mental health risks from over-reliance on GenAI (e.g., deskilling, critical thinking atrophy, and addiction). The authors quantify this gap and propose extending safety and alignment work, alongside information campaigns and regulation, to address these overlooked risks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The AI safety and alignment literature has primarily focused on a limited set of harms (discrimination, harmful content, information hazards, malicious use cases), while the public discourse also highlights threats to cognition, mental health, and human welfare — such as deskilling, critical thinking atrophy, and addiction to GenAI systems. These latter risks are largely unaddressed in existing AI safety research.

Method: The paper (1) quantifies the discrepancy between current AI safety focus areas and public concerns, and (2) discusses initial approaches for addressing cognitive and mental health risks within the safety and alignment framework, including information campaigns and regulatory measures.

Result: N/A — This is an abstract describing the scope and direction of the paper rather than reporting completed results.

Conclusion: The paper proposes that safety and alignment work should expand to include cognitive and mental health concerns, and suggests information campaigns and regulation as mitigation strategies.

Abstract: The scope of AI safety and alignment work in generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) has so far mostly been limited to harms related to: (a) discrimination and hate speech, (b) harmful/inappropriate (violent, sexual, illegal) content, (c) information hazards, and (d) use cases related to malicious actors, such as cybersecurity, child abuse, and chemical, biological, radiological, and nuclear threats. The public conversation around AI, on the other hand, has also been focusing on threats to our cognition, mental health, and welfare at large, related to over-relying on new technologies, most recently, those related to GenAI. Examples include deskilling associated with cognitive offloading and the atrophy of critical thinking as a result of over-reliance on GenAI systems, and addiction associated with attachment and dependence on GenAI systems. Such risks are rarely addressed, if at all, in the AI safety and alignment literature. In this paper, we highlight and quantify this discrepancy and discuss some initial thoughts on how safety and alignment work could address cognitive and mental health concerns. Finally, we discuss how information campaigns and regulation can be used to mitigate such prominent risks.

</details>
